## The gate — OPEN-EVERY-FRAME, 2026-08-10 (the second pass of the day). **ALL 16 CLAIMED FRAMES OPENED, NONE TAKEN ON TRUST. B27 AND B35 HOLD UNDER INDEPENDENT MEASUREMENT. THE BELT WAS RE-DRIVEN AND THE PRICES COUNTED IN THE DOM. ONE CLOSED ROW RESTS ON A FRAME THAT PREDATES THE FIX BESIDE IT. EXACTLY TWO REDS.**

> **Why this pass exists.** The previous verifier certified two rows and ran out of budget for eleven;
> when the lead opened three of those himself, two had not been fixed at all. So the standing rule was
> made absolute: **open every frame.** This gate opened all sixteen images the wave cited, plus nine
> more it produced itself, and re-derived the two contested rows from the product's own code rather
> than from the lanes' prose.

**THE HEADLINE: THE WAVE'S CLAIMS SURVIVED, AND THE TWO THE LEAD PERSONALLY DOUBTED SURVIVED HARDEST.**
Both B27 and B35 were re-measured from scratch — B27 through `buildWorldGeometry`, B35 by sampling the
same pixel column in the before and after frames — and both came back exactly as the lanes reported,
to the byte in B35's case. What this gate adds that no lane had is a **provenance check on the frames
themselves**, and that is where the one real problem is.

### 1 · B27 — the lead's own question, answered with a measurement

He asked: *is there a Б2 stop sign in the windscreen at 15–25 m?* **Yes, and the number is 17.55 m.**

Measured, not inherited. `content/world/tj-stop-v1.json` has **no `signs` key at all** — the Б2 is
synthesised by `builders/props.ts` from `junctionPriorityControls`. Building the district through the
product's own builder emits **exactly one** `kind=stop`, at world `(8.93, 0.02, 28.52)`; with
`toWorld=(x,y,h)=>[x,h,-y]` (`builders/mesh.ts:14`) that is district **(x 8.93, y −28.52)**, yaw 0°,
facing the approaching driver. The same build reports **0 traffic lights** and `intersections[0].signalized=false`,
so this is not a signal row.

Range from each pose, computed against that one sign:

| pose | range | along-track | in the lead's band? |
|---|---|---|---|
| y −60.5 | 32.34 m | +31.98 AHEAD | — |
| **y −45.4 (the lane's frame)** | **17.55 m** | **+16.88 AHEAD** | **YES** |
| y −36.3 | 9.15 m | +7.77 AHEAD | — |
| y −29.9 | 5.02 m | +1.38 AHEAD | — |
| **y −22.8 (the lead's frame)** | 7.49 m | **−5.72 BEHIND** | no — past it |
| **y −15.8 (the lead's frame)** | 13.61 m | **−12.72 BEHIND** | no — past it |

I opened both. The lane's frame carries the octagon plainly in the glass with A-pillar, dash, wheel and
cluster at 0 км/ч D. The lead's frame `RR/b27/b27-y-22.png` reads `y=−22.8 obj=2/3 events=3`, its mirror
is captioned «Завий надясно и продължи по пътя с предимство» and the shadow car is mid-right-turn — it is
the **turn phase**, downstream of the sign and of the graded stop line. A sign behind the car cannot be in
the windscreen.

**And the lane did not do the thing the lead was worried about.** It did *not* conclude "the copy is
wrong rather than the world". It concluded that both copy and world are right and the two refused frames
were shot past the sign. That conclusion is now independently confirmed.

under-represented topic`, and `src/modules/lesson/__tests__/compose.test.ts > the catalogue covers the whole

`clearanceProvenance.test.ts` timeout two lanes reported did **not** recur — this run had the box to itself,
which is exactly the condition those lanes said it needed; grep over the log gives **0** occurrences of
„timed out" and **0** of „Failed to start … worker". Targeted re-run of the three suites this wave added or
changed — `signal-lens-look.test.ts`, `offences.test.ts`, `corpus.test.ts` — **159 passed, 3 files**.

**No repo file was changed by this gate except this register.** Probe scripts and the temporary attack
battery were deleted; the working tree is back to the same 41 entries it started with; all frames live in
the scratchpad, never in the repo.


## The gate — CONSEQUENCE-AUDIT, 2026-08-10. **5 OF 53 → 53 OF 53, EVERY FIGURE RE-CUT BY THIS GATE. THE WATERMARK TOOL WAS ACTUALLY RE-RUN. THREE OF THE SIX KNOWN ATTACKS ARE CLOSED, THREE ARE NOT, AND TWO NEW ONES GET THROUGH EVERYTHING. EXACTLY TWO REDS.**

Six lanes reported closing the founder's „tens or hundreds" complaint and the last of his review
rows. **This gate read none of their claims on trust.** Every number below was re-derived here —
the acts parsed off disk by this gate's own reader, the ingest tool re-executed from the pinned
originals, the six known attacks re-run in their exact register form, and every frame opened.

*The headline, and it is bigger than the lanes claimed.* The five structured rows at `HEAD` are
now **53 of 53** — `git show HEAD:…/consequences.ts` carries exactly `SPEEDING_DANGEROUS`,
`SPEEDING_OVER_LIMIT`, `RED_LIGHT_CROSSED`, `PEDESTRIAN_NOT_YIELDED`, `STOP_SIGN_NO_FULL_STOP`, and
the working tree carries every code in the catalogue. **307 `LawQuote`s, 307 verbatim, 307 inside
the unit their own citation names, 0 exceptions.** **167 numeric figures** (глоба + контролни точки,
counting every ladder rung, every gated branch and every escalation) and **167 of 167** state their
own number inside their own quote. The matcher that produced those figures was negative-controlled:
it says `true` for „100 лв." in „Наказва се с глоба 100 лв. водач, който:" and `false` for „5000 лв."
in the same sentence.

*The honest blanks are honest, and this gate re-derived every negative rather than accepting one.*
„регулировчик" occurs **13 times** in ЗДвП — чл. 7 (2), чл. 10 (8), чл. 113 (1), чл. 120 (1),
чл. 184 (1) — and **exactly one** of those is inside the penal chapter (чл. 174–189, 42 units), the
one that opens „наказва **пешеходец**, който … не спазва … сигналите на регулировчика". „загас"
occurs **0** times in the whole act; „ръчна спирачка" **0**; „подготовка" appears 3 times in the
penal chapter and all three are about driving schools and psych-testing bodies, none about a
driver's pre-drive routine. чл. 22, ал. 1 says „не трябва да **се движи** … с твърде ниска скорост"
and чл. 23, ал. 1 measures distance to „**движещото се** пред него" — so the stalled engine, the
hesitation on green and the standstill gap are right to say the street charges nothing. чл. 74,
ал. 1 reads „може да се използват **само** при…", a permission with a limit, never a duty.
**No lane manufactured a fine.** A scan of every free-prose field on all 53 rows for „N лв." /
„N контролни точки" / „N €" found **0** figures that do not occur in a quote that row cites, and the
six `exam-only` rows and the one honest blank state **no figure at all**.

*The attacks. Three of the six are closed and three are not, and that is exactly the scope the
loader lane claimed.* Re-run in their register form against the real bank:

| # | the known attack | before | **now** |
|---|---|---|---|
| A3 | чл. 182 alinea twins — flip `paragraphRef` to „ал. 2" | through everything | **CAUGHT by the loader** — „contextQuote is in ЗДвП чл. 182 but NOT in ал. 2" |
| A9 | `titleBg` rewritten to another offence | through everything | **CAUGHT by the loader** — „„превишена, скорост, населено, място" occurs in none of this row's verified quotes" |
| A13 | offence phrase truncated so „ако…" falls off | through everything | **CAUGHT by the loader** — „the offence phrase stops before the act does" |
| A5 | right offence, wrong act — red-light к.т. re-cited to the **2025 snapshot**, whose т. 20 is word-identical | through the loader, caught by the repo | **UNCHANGED** — `verifyCitations` still returns `[]`; `corpus.test.ts:1457` still catches it |
| A7 | declaration loosened by **deleting** an anchor group | through the loader, caught by the repo | **PARTIALLY closed.** Deleting the group that carries „Б2" is now caught — but *by the digit rule*, not the widening rule. Deleting the **verb** group (`не спира`/`не спре`) is still `[]`. Measured consequence: the pinned discrimination matrix goes 4 → 5, the new pair being a **контролни-точки phrase from the наредба crossing a row**, which the repo does catch. |
| A10 | the row `id` rewritten | through the loader, caught incidentally | **UNCHANGED** — `[]` from the loader and from the schema; caught only because `corpus.test.ts` names ids 20 times |

*And three attacks of this gate's own, two of which get through everything.* **(1) `instrument`
`фиш` → `електронен фиш`, `instrumentSource` left alone: through the loader, through the schema,

against the rule cited for it. **(2) Two rows sharing one `id`: `verifyCitations` `[]`, schema

the offence is absent from an exhaustive list, so amountBgn must be 0"). Worth naming because the

**Negative control ran and was correctly silent on the untouched bank.**

## The gate — ATTACK-THE-CHECK, 2026-08-09 (the third pass of the day). **SIX OF THIRTEEN ATTACKS GOT THROUGH THE LOADER. THREE GET THROUGH THE WHOLE REPO. THE TREE IS GREEN WITH EXACTLY THE TWO PROTECTED REDS.**

Three lanes reported closing what the SCALES gate had found by attacking rather than reading: the
citation loader's blind spot, the bare „Наредба № Iз-2539", and the theory exam's unnamed scale.
**This gate read none of their claims on trust.** It re-ran the known-good attack, invented twelve
more, fed both watermark specimens to every check in the tree, and drove the exam in a browser.

Three things worth saying before the detail:

*First: the fix is real and it holds against the attack it was built for.* The known-good attack —
give a speeding row the traffic-light case in `contextQuoteBg` **and** `offencePhraseBg` — is now
refused by name („the offence named is not the offence this row prices"), and so are six others,
including two the lane never claimed: the whole-article quote and the danger-variant control-points
citation moved onto the no-danger row.

*Second: the survivors are not the ones the lane disclosed.* It disclosed two. There are three, and
the third — `O-3` below — is **live on a shipped row**, not hypothetical: `pen-b2-no-stop-danger`'s
fine phrase can be truncated to drop „ако от това е създадена непосредствена опасност за
движението", which is the entire difference between 200 лв. + 10 к.т. and 50 лв. The lane wrote that
class up as „nothing in the corpus reads that way today". It does.

*Third: the loader and the CI gate are different guarantees, and this is the first gate to measure
both.* Every survivor was re-run as a **committed** mutation of `content/law/penalties.json` against
the repo's own suites. Three are caught by a test even though `verifyCitations` accepts them. Three
are caught by nothing at all.

### 1 · THE ATTACK BATTERY — 13 attacks, run against the real bank, every one reported

`verifyCitations(acts, [tamperedRow])` on a `structuredClone` of a real row. „through" means it
returned `[]`.

| # | attack | verdict |
|---|---|---|
| A1 | **the known-good one.** Speeding row given the traffic-light case in `contextQuoteBg` AND `offencePhraseBg` | **REFUSED** — „the offence named is not the offence this row prices … satisfies none of [превишаване \| превиши], [от 21 до 30 \| с повече от 10 km/h]" |
| A2 | **adjacent tier, same article.** The 21–30 row priced by the 11–20 sentence, on the `not-listed` disqualification figure so no amount contradicts it | **REFUSED** — the tier anchor |
| A3 | **the identical twin.** The in-town row's `paragraphRef` flipped to „ал. 2" — чл. 182 ал. 1 т. 3 and ал. 2 т. 3 are word for word | **THROUGH** → `O-1` |
| A4 | **the tier where the ladders differ.** A 31–40 row quoting ал. 2's 300 лв. under a coordinate saying ал. 1 (in town it is 400) | **REFUSED** — „quote is in ЗДвП чл. 182 but NOT in ал. 1". *The new alinea-span parser earning its keep.* |
| A5 | **right offence, wrong act.** Red-light control points re-cited to the **2025 snapshot**, whose т. 20 is word-identical | **THROUGH the loader, CAUGHT by the repo** — `corpus.test.ts > the whole bank now cites the consolidation, not the 2025 snapshot` fails with `[ 'pen-red-light' ]` |
| A6 | same, on the alcohol row, where the editions disagree on the number | **REFUSED** — „quote is NOT in Наредба № Iз-2539/2012 чл. 6" |
| A7 | **the declaration loosened by DELETION, then the swap.** Two anchor groups replaced by the stem `[["превиш"]]`, numbers spelled out of `statementBg`, then the 11–20 phrase moved in | **THROUGH the loader, CAUGHT by the repo** — 3 tests red, loudest the discrimination matrix (6 pairs, 4 pinned) → `O-5` |
| A7b | the same loosening with the citations untouched | **THROUGH the loader** — all four declaration guards pass |
| A8 | **the declaration as a paraphrase of its own title** — „Превишена скорост … двадесет и един до тридесет километра в час", one generic anchor „скорост" | **REFUSED** — and by the row's *own honest phrases*, which do not contain „скорост". Self-consistency is doing work here. |
| A9 | **`titleBg` rewritten** to another offence, every citation left honest | **THROUGH everything** → `O-2` |
| A10 | **the row `id` rewritten** | **THROUGH the loader, CAUGHT by the repo** — but only incidentally: 12 tests name the id |
| A11 | **`quoteBg` widened to the whole article**, so every phrase is inside it | **REFUSED** — the alinea span |
| A12 | **the danger-variant control-points citation moved onto the no-danger row** | **REFUSED** — „satisfies none of [не спира \| не спре]" |
| A13 | **the offence phrase truncated so its limiting clause falls off** | **THROUGH everything** → `O-3` |

**The discrimination matrix reproduces at 4, independently.** Re-derived with a private
implementation of the anchor matcher rather than the exported one, so a bug in the exported matcher
could not hide inside its own measurement: exactly four ordered (row, foreign-phrase) pairs survive,
all four the single Наредба № 38 exam indent that `pen-b2-no-stop`/`-danger` and the two speeding
tiers genuinely share. **Nothing from ЗДвП or the контролни-точки наредба crosses a row.** The lane's
headline claim is accurate.

**And one structural finding the lane did not name.** Check (5b) requires *every* offence phrase on a
row to satisfy *every* anchor group, so **a row's `conduct` is forced to be the weakest common
denominator of its figures.** That is why `pen-b2-no-stop-danger` cannot declare „непосредствена
опасност": its контролни-точки and наказателни-точки citations come from acts that never say it, and
adding the group would turn the row's own honest citations red. The two rows that differ **only** by
the danger clause are precisely the two rows the mechanism cannot tell apart.

### 2 · THE BARE ACT NAME — answered by resolving every hit, not by grepping

Every `lawRef` in `content/` whose `act` string mentions 2539, put through `actIdForActName`:

| act string as written | resolves to | count |
|---|---|---:|
| `Наредба № Iз-2539` (**bare**) | `naredba-iz-2539-consolidated-dv49-2026` — **the text in force** | 11 |
| `Наредба № Iз-2539 (изм. ДВ, бр. 49 от 2026 г.)` | `naredba-iz-2539-consolidated-dv49-2026` | 8 |
| direct `actId` in `penalties.json` / `sources.json` | the consolidation (7) + registry rows | 11 |

The eleven bare ones are `content/concepts.json` ×2 and nine questions across
`alkohol-i-godnost`, `dokumenti-i-sanktsii` and `osnovni-ponyatia`. **Zero land on the snapshot.**
All eleven cite чл. 2 or чл. 6, which both editions carry, so the move is a strict improvement.

**And the snapshot is still reachable when named — which is the point of keeping it.** All four
documented markers resolve to `naredba-iz-2539`: `(ред. 28.01.2025 г.)`,
`(изм. ДВ, бр. 108 от 2024 г.)`, `(снимка)`, `(редакция към 2025 г.)`. Both the Ukrainian „і" and the
Cyrillic „и" spellings of the Roman numeral resolve too. **Breaking that would be a different defect
and it has not been broken.**

One thing left standing: `lib/content/pointScales.ts:122` renders `"Наредба № Iз-2539"` bare **to the
student** as the контролни-точки scale's source. Harmless today — it names no article and no figure —
and named in STILL OPEN as `O-8` because the next person to put an article number beside it inherits
the original problem.

### 3 · THE WATERMARK — „Хумор" is caught twice, „Сиела" only once, and not by an ingest

> **CLOSED 2026-08-10 by the CONSEQUENCE-AUDIT gate, and closed by EXECUTION rather than by a
> filter being written.** Everything below was true when it was written. It is no longer.
> `content/law/tools/page-furniture.mjs` now strips both signatures in the **extraction** and
> `assertNoVendorPagination` refuses to emit an act that still carries either; `build-corpus.mjs`,
> `build-speed-acts.mjs` and `build-naredba-24.mjs` all call it. The blocker this section names —
> „the extraction's inputs are gitignored scratch, so the tool cannot be re-run" — was cleared:
> all four originals are on disk and **all four sha256 match `sources.json`**, and this gate
> re-executed both builders into a scratch directory and got **all six emitted acts byte-identical**
> to the working tree. **Count: 188 pieces across 505 units at `HEAD` → 0.** (The published „185"
> reported *units* for НСИПМК where it reported *pieces* for the other two acts.) The reason it was
> never cosmetic is now repaired and testable: чл. 6, ал. 1, т. 3 of the snapshot is one sentence
> again and the article carries a consecutive 1…22.

Both specimens fed to every furniture check in the tree:

| specimen | `build-naredba-24.mjs` ingest signatures | its stop-line headings | `pageFurniture.test.ts` FURNITURE | its PAGINATION |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| lex.bg „Хумор" sidebar | **5 of 7 fire** | **4 stop lines** | **5 fire** | — |
| „Източник: Правно-информационни системи „Сиела"" + `24/01/2025 г.` | **0 fire** | none | none | **both fire** |

So the answer to „does the new ingest check actually fail on page furniture" is **half**. The Хумор
class is stopped at the ingest *and* at the repo test. The Сиела class is stopped **only by the repo
test's ledger** — and the ingest that has a guard (`build-naredba-24.mjs`) is not the ingest that
produced the damage. **`content/law/tools/build-corpus.mjs`, which emitted all 185 occurrences across
naredba-38 (124 / 34 units, приложение № 5 among them), naredba-iz-2539 (58 / 16) and
naredba-sredstva-za-izmervane (3 / 3), contains no furniture check of any kind** — zero signatures,
one unrelated `throw`.

The ledger was verified by mutation rather than by reading: a Сиела stamp injected into
`naredba-24.json` `чл. 9` turns the ledger test red with `+ "naredba-24": { "чл. 9": 2 }`. The file
was then restored **byte-identical**. `O-4` carries the owner and the reason it cannot be fixed from
here.

### 4 · THE THEORY EXAM — driven, and the „ten" were twenty-two

**Measured against `HEAD` with a scanner written for this gate** (different regexes from the shipped
one, so a blind spot would have to be shared to survive both): the four exam files carried
**8 bare „т." + 14 unqualified „точки" = 22**, not ten. The repairing lane reported 21. **The working
tree is 0 and 0.**

The undercount was of the *defect*, not the abbreviation — twelve of the twenty-two were „точки"
spelled out („точки максимум", „/ 97 точки", „Загубени точки", and „Праг за успех 87 точки" read
aloud by the screen reader). Unqualified „точки" in Bulgarian **is** контролни точки. It is the
founder's own misreading with the abbreviation expanded.

**Driven, on the rig already listening on `:3200`** — `/dev/fold-rig?mode=exam&rank=3`, hydrated
(`data-hydrated="1"`), a full 45-question paper, clock at 39:52, counter „1/45":

- **1280 px** — the visible chip reads **„3 точки от правилни отговори"**; the phone twin is
  `display:none`.
- **375 px** — the visible chip reads **„3 т. по теорията"**; the desktop twin is hidden.
- Both carry the same `title`: „Толкова тежи този въпрос на **ТЕОРЕТИЧНИЯ** изпит. Печели се с верен
  отговор и няма нищо общо с наказателните точки от карането, нито с контролните точки по книжката."

The result view, rendered and read back end to end:

> „Резултат от пробния изпит **74 / 97 точки от правилни отговори на теоретичния изпит** ·
> Неиздържан · **Праг — точки по теорията ≥ 87** · **Загубени точки по теорията** 23 · Използвано
> време 33:00 · Точки от правилни отговори на теоретичния изпит (Наредба № 38, чл. 39, ал. 1): 97
> максимум, изпитът е издържан при не по-малко от 87. Печелят се с верен отговор. **НЕ са контролни
> точки по книжката и НЕ са наказателните (изпитни) точки от практическия изпит.**"

Pass, fail and late papers all carry that footing; the review chip reads „0 / 3 т. по теорията"
(and „1 / 1 т. по теорията" at one).

**THE OPPOSITE ERROR WAS NOT MADE, and this gate went looking for it specifically.** A grep for
`изпитни точк` / `наказателн* точк` across `app/(dashboard)/exams/**`, `components/exam/**` and
`modules/exam/**` returns **only the test that forbids them**. Nobody stamped the practical sheet's
10/3/1 label on a 97-point written test to clear a scanner. And it is **not a fifth scale**:
`POINT_SCALES.theory` already counted this unit for the simulator's micro-quiz, so it was grounded
rather than duplicated — the inline form stayed „N т. по теорията" so no sim frame moves, and doc 93
§2 now names all five systems with the act each rests on.

**Two bare units remain in the repo** and neither is the student's exam:
`app/(dashboard)/review/ReviewClient.tsx:635` and `app/(dashboard)/admin/page.tsx:415`. Both are
outside the shipped guard's directories — `O-6`.

### 6 · Claims this gate REFUSES

| claim | who said it | what the measurement shows |
|---|---|---|
| „A FIFTH ATTACK STILL GETS THROUGH" *(one survivor: the row's own label)* | the citation lane | **Three survive the whole repo, not one.** The label (`A9`), the identical-alinea twin (`A3`) and the truncated phrase (`A13`). Three more survive the loader and are caught only by a test — a distinction that lane did not draw. |
| „No substring check can see negation or limitation … **Nothing in the corpus reads that way today**" | the citation lane | **It does.** `pen-b2-no-stop-danger`'s own fine phrase truncates cleanly to a general clause that drops the danger limb, satisfies the row's conduct, and turns nothing red. That is not a class of defect waiting to happen; it is the shipped row. |
| „the new ingest check … refuses to emit" *(implied general)* | the watermark lane | Only for the lex.bg class, and only on the ingest that never produced the problem. **`build-corpus.mjs` — the tool that produced 185 of the 185 — has no check.** The lane says the fix belongs in the extraction and it is right; what is not stated is that the guard it did add cannot see the Сиела stamp at all. |
| „the ten bare „т."" *(as an inventory)* | doc 93 §7 G-5 (3), and the repairing lane's own count | **22 defects at `HEAD` on those four files** by an independent scanner — 8 abbreviations and 14 spelled-out. The repairing lane found and fixed the spelled-out half itself and said so; the *register* still said ten. |

Nothing in this section is a claim that a lane's work was wrong. Every one of the four fixes holds
against the attack it was designed for. What is refused is the **completeness** each of them
reported.

## `D5` — „it turns to R (reverse) but the car does not move did it break or ?" · the 2026-08-11 PHOTOGRAPH-THE-GESTURE gate

**Verdict: `FIXED-SEEN`.** Frames:
`scratchpad/g2-B-held-2.6s.png` (desktop 1280×800, the moment) ·
`scratchpad/ov-iphone16-portrait-open.png` (iPhone 16 portrait, deck open) ·
`scratchpad/guard2-B.png` · `guard2-C.png` · `guard2-D.png` (the three manual routes into R).

### What he did, and what he now sees

This is the other half of `D1`. `D1` stopped the car reversing into traffic under a held brake; it
never told him why the car had gone dead. Driven on `/dev/drive-rig`, `sc-junction-stop@L1`, real
shell, **real keyboard** (trusted CDP events, not the rig's injector):

| | what the car did | what the cockpit said |
|---|---|---|
| **A — hold ↓ from a roll and never lift** (his words in the brief) | braked 22.2 → 0.001 km/h and **stayed in D for the whole 7.27 s** — max 0.146 km/h | nothing, **and correctly nothing**: LAW 1 means this gesture never reaches R, so there is no refusal to explain |
| **B — stop, lift, press ↓ and HOLD** (the gesture he was actually standing in) | selector **D → R at 0.44 s** after the press, gear −1, and **0.001 km/h for the whole 8.38 s hold** | **the message, 1.45 s after the cluster showed R** (1.89 s after the press). `REVERSE_STUCK_HINT_S` is 1.5 s counted from the flip itself, which the cluster renders one frame later — the two agree. Measured on the assist route in isolation: **1.55 s** |
| **C — release, press ↓ again** | **first movement 0.25 s after the second press**, reaching **23.85 km/h** backwards | — |

> **Вдигни крак от педала, натисни пак**
>
> Лостът е на R, но колата не тръгва: педалът, който държиш, допреди миг беше спирачката — с него
> спря. Затова не става на газ под крака ти. Докато не го отпуснеш, той продължава да спира. В
> истинска кола е същото — първо вдигаш крак от спирачката, чак после даваш газ. Вдигни крак,
> натисни пак и тръгваш назад.

**One correction to the brief this gate was given.** „Hold S from a roll, let the car stop, keep
holding past the 0.35 s shift" is a gesture that **no longer exists**: `REVERSE_ASSIST_LIFT_S` (LAW 1)
makes a pedal held through a stop permanently unarmable, so it never shifts and there is nothing to
be confused by. The confusion he hit is row B above — stop, lift, press, hold — and that is the row
that is photographed.

### The guard is intact — every route into R, with the pedal already held

All four driven, each with the brake genuinely down at the moment the selector reached R. Routes
B/C/D were set up by **braking to a stop from a roll and never lifting**, which LAW 1 proves cannot
arm the assist (the selector still read `D` at the end of every roll-down), so the route under test
is the only thing that moved the lever.

| route into R | gate walk | max &#124;v&#124; while the pedal is held | said it | after the foot lifts and presses again |
|---|---|---:|---|---:|
| **A** the assist (stop, lift, press ↓) | D → R under the held foot | **0.001 km/h** over 6 s | yes, +1.55 s | **17.96 km/h** |
| **B** the `[` keys | D > N > R | **0.001 km/h** over 7 s | yes | **21.44 km/h** |
| **C** the touch gear sheet (finger on the drivetrain axis) | D > N > R | **0.001 km/h** over 7 s | yes | **25.05 km/h** |
| **D** the cockpit lever (right-click ×2) | D > N > R | **0.001 km/h** over 7 s | yes | **21.27 km/h** |

**The 16.8 km/h reverse into traffic is unreachable from all four.** `reverseAssist.test.ts` is green
(**30 tests**, not the 32 the lane reported — the file is green either way), and its LAW 2 case pins
the half a drive cannot see: 600 frames of `throttle === 0` **and** `brake === 1`, i.e. the car is
still *braked*, not merely coasting.

*Checked on the way and reported as a non-defect:* one `[` press steps the gate exactly one station
(D→N→R→P→reject, and `]` back), measured with 3 s between presses. An earlier run of this gate read
it as a double-step; that run was wrong — the brake it was holding had armed the assist, which walked
to R by itself before the keys were pressed.

#### → 2026-08-11 · HIS RULING ON THE ASSIST, DRIVEN. „thats automatic transmition"

His words: *„before when pressing S or Arrow Down it automatically went to R and moved backwards and
it has to stay like that because thats automatic transmition."* The table above is the state he was
ruling against: on the assist route the car reached R and then **sat at 0.001 km/h under his foot**
until he lifted and pressed again. LAW 2 never *prevented* that reverse — it charged a second press
for it, which is exactly what an automatic does not do. `ReverseShiftSource` now tells the pedal
mapper which route flipped the selector; the assist's own armed press is exempt, every hand-worked
route is not. Driven on `/dev/drive-rig` (`sc-junction-stop` L1, real keyboard / mouse / finger,
canvas 1264×742 fullscreen, 0 page errors on every run):

| his gesture | selector → R | first movement | speed reached | card |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| stand still, foot off, **one unbroken press of ↓** | **+0.381 s** | **+0.539 s** (0.158 s after R) | **25.10 km/h backwards** | **silent** |
| repeat run | +0.538 s | +0.639 s (0.101 s after R) | 25.10 km/h backwards | silent |

Photographed at the moment it moves: `scratchpad/rev/shots/A-assist-MOVING.png` — the reverse view
with the rig readout `v=-3.3km/h gear=-1 phase=driving` in the same frame. **The card is silent on
that route by mechanism, not by a second gate: nothing was refused, so there is nothing to explain.**

**The guard, re-driven on all three hand-worked routes.** Each one ROLLS IN and brakes to rest
*without lifting*, so the held pedal is a genuine braking press and LAW 1 never arms the assist — a
probe that presses the brake at a standstill first hands the car to the assist, which reaches R by
itself, and the subsequent steps then walk **R→P** instead of D→R. That is how a route can look
tested and never have run.

| route into R | entered at | gate walk | max &#124;v&#124; while the foot is held | said it | after a genuine lift |
|---|---:|---|---:|---|---:|
| **B** the `[` keys | 21.1 km/h | D → N → R | **0.246 km/h** over 11.9 s | yes, **+2.96 s** | **25.10 km/h** |
| **C** the touch gear sheet (852×393, real finger, ◄P ×2) | 28.8 km/h | D → N → R | **0.500 km/h** over 14.2 s | yes, **+5.36 s** | **20.26 km/h** |
| **D** the cockpit lever (right-click ×2 on the real 3D hotspot) | 20.9 km/h | D → N → R | **0.424 km/h** over 13.0 s | yes, **+3.86 s** | **25.07 km/h** |

`scratchpad/rev/shots/C4-touch-heldfoot.png` is the guard with its voice: «Вдигни крак от педала,
натисни пак» on screen, `v=0.0km/h gear=-1`, the foot still down. **And the case he originally
reported is unchanged:** rolled to 21.1 km/h, pressed ↓ **while moving** and held it 9 s through the
stop — selector trail `2 → 1`, R never reached, max reverse **0.143 km/h**, no card. LAW 1 does not
move, and it must not: it is the only thing standing between a held brake and the 16.8 km/h reverse
into traffic that opened this whole thread.

**What it costs, stated and not buried.** The warning between the R telltale lighting and the car
moving collapses from a deliberate lift-and-press to **0.10–0.16 s**. A nervous re-dab of the brake
at a Б2 line — foot fully off ≥ 0.25 s, then down again — reverses NOW where it used to reverse one
press later. It always reversed; the student used to get a lift and a press of thinking time. That is
the ruling working as ruled.

**Two things this gate found in the instruments themselves.** (1) `page.waitForFunction(fn, options)`
passes `options` as the function's **argument** — `drive-rig.mjs`, `seat.mjs` and this gate's first
probe all ask for 120 s and silently get the 30 s default. It cost three failed runs on a contended
box before anyone looked. (2) On a phone a teach card **hides the entire touch rail**, so a probe
that reaches for the gear sheet while «ИНСТРУКЦИИ» is up finds nothing and reports the route as
untestable rather than as untested — photographed at `scratchpad/rev/shots/C2-touch-heldfoot.png`.

### No new overlap — four profiles, WebKit, real insets, deck closed and open

Reached with the **finger only** (the drivetrain axis: one continuous press, never lifted). A
synthetic drive *key* sets `TouchControls.keyboardActive` and hides the whole overlay — that is how
an earlier survey counted 2 controls instead of 12 and called the screen clean.

| profile | card box | left frac (rule ≥ 0.6) | area frac (budget ≤ 0.12) | controls · overlaps · dead — deck **closed** | deck **open** | nearest control, closed / open |
|---|---|---:|---:|---|---|---:|
| iPhone 16 portrait | `[239.5, 67, 141.5×97.5]` | **0.609** | **0.041** | 14 · **0** · **0** | 20 · **0** · **0** | 183.9 px / 110.5 px |
| iPhone 16 landscape | `[541, 8, 240×83.8]` | **0.635** | **0.060** | 14 · **0** · **0** | 20 · **0** · **0** | 63.8 px / **15.0 px** |
| Small Android portrait 360×780 | `[218.4, 8, 129.6×97.5]` | **0.607** | **0.045** | 14 · **0** · **0** | 20 · **0** · **0** | 162.8 px / 131.5 px |
| Small Android landscape 780×360 | `[528, 8, 240×83.8]` | **0.677** | **0.072** | 14 · **0** · **0** | 20 · **0** · **0** | **52.3 px** / **15.0 px** |

*(14 = 12 real controls plus the card's own „Защо" and „Скрий известието". 20 = the six deck
transport buttons joining them.)*

**THE NEGATIVE CONTROL, on every profile and every state.** The first version of this survey reported
`0 overlaps` **while its shift function was a no-op** — a blind probe and a clean screen produce the
same number, so the probe is now made to fail on purpose before any zero is believed. The **real**
card is translated onto the drivetrain pad, re-measured, and translated back:

| profile | overlap the probe caught | control it killed | after restore |
|---|---:|---|---|
| iPhone 16 portrait | **13 793.2 px²** | „Ход — назад…" 1/5 hit points stolen | **0 · 0** |
| iPhone 16 landscape | **19 681.3 px²** | „Ход — назад…" 1/5 | **0 · 0** |
| Small Android portrait | **12 635.4 px²** | „Ход — назад…" **5/5** | **0 · 0** |
| Small Android landscape | **14 740.0 px²** | „Ход — назад…" 1/5 | **0 · 0** |

**It displaces nothing.** Every `[data-hud]` surface was boxed with the message down and again with
it up: on three profiles **not one box moved**, and on iPhone portrait the only change is the notify
column's own height (83.8 → 97.5 px) because the line wraps to three there.

**Landing.** `l1-preparation` (`preDrive: true`): **20 seconds** of held brake produced no message,
selector still `P`, phase still `preDrive` — the watch is reset while the drive is locked, exactly as
written, so on the landing phase there is nothing to overlap. 13 controls, `scratchpad/final-landing.png`.

**The one number to watch.** On both **landscape** phones with the demonstration deck open the card's
bottom edge comes within **15.0 px** of the deck's „Повтаряй участъка" button. That is a clearance,
not an overlap — 0 area, 0 stolen hit points — but it is the number that goes negative first if this
copy ever grows a line.

#### → 2026-08-11 · RE-MEASURED, AND THE THING THAT GOES NEGATIVE FIRST IS NOT THE COPY

Four profiles (393×852, 852×393, 360×780, 780×360), deck closed AND open, on the real shell, with
the car actually in **R** so the reverse-time cues exist — a resting frame cannot see any of this.
**The negative control passes on all four**: a transparent full-viewport sheet kills **22 of 22**
controls, removing it brings back **22 of 22**. *(It failed on both iPhone profiles first time round,
and that failure is worth keeping: a `<body>` child is not rendered while another element is
fullscreen, so the sheet was invisible to `elementFromPoint` and the probe was quietly proving
nothing. It now appends to `document.fullscreenElement ?? document.body`. **A "0 dead controls" from
a probe whose negative control has not fired is not a measurement.**)*

**DEAD CONTROLS: 0 of 22, on every profile, in both deck states.** Nothing is unreachable.

**THREE CONTROLS ARE UNDER THE 44 px FLOOR, on every profile and in both states** — and they are not
in `TouchControls`, whose own cells measure exactly 44×44 (the ⚙ station, the mirror/indicator
glyphs and every gear-sheet cell, verified in the same frames):

| control | measured | where |
|---|---:|---|
| tier pill „Начинаещ" | **78 × 25 px** | `[data-hud="difficulty"]`, top of every profile |
| tier pill „Нормален" | **78 × 25 px** | same |
| tier pill „Напреднал" | **85 × 25 px** | same |
| demo-deck handle „🎬 Демонстрация ▸" | **134 × 27 px** (130 × 43 on 360×780) | `[data-hud="demo-deck"]` |
| the reverse cue's own button „⌖ Тясно е — виж мястото отгоре" | **224 × 31 px** | landscape only |

**THE REAL COLLISION, PHOTOGRAPHED, AND IT IS NOT THE ONE THIS ROW WAS WATCHING.** On a **portrait**
phone with the deck open, the overhead cue is printed **on top of** the demonstration deck's caption:
`scratchpad/phone/out/iphone-portrait-open.png` — «Тясно е — виж мястото отгоре» and «Подмини …
задната броня …» interleaved character for character, **8 294 px²** (189 × 44) on 393×852 and
**5 220 px²** (180 × 29) on 360×780. That is row **B13**'s defect returning in the deck-OPEN state.
**The mechanism is measured, not guessed, and it is not the copy growing a line:** the cue is pinned
at `CUE_MAX_BOTTOM_FRACTION = 0.55` — its bottom edge sat at y=384 in the colliding run and y=383 in
a clean one, i.e. it could not move — while the **deck's own height** varied 110 px → 156 px between
two runs of the same probe on the same viewport minutes apart. When the deck renders tall its top
climbs to 340 and the clamped cue has nowhere to go. **So the fragile input is the deck's height, and
the fix belongs at the clamp, not at `CUE_GAP_PX`.**

**THE 15 px ITSELF: STILL EXACTLY 15.0 px, AND IT IS A DIFFERENT CARD FROM THE ONE ABOVE.** This
row's number is the **reverse message** («Вдигни крак от педала, натисни пак») against the deck's
«Повтаряй участъка» 🔁 — not the overhead cue. Earned rather than simulated: rolled in at 28.8 km/h,
braked to rest **without lifting**, shifted D→N→R by finger through the gear sheet, card up at
+4.23 s. Landscape, canvas 836×377 fullscreen, card at `[592, 16, 240×78]`. **Deck closed:** nearest
deck control is the collapsed handle at **438.7 px**. **Deck open** (deck 512×108 @ 72,16): nearest is
**«Повтаряй участъка», 44 × 44 px, gap 15.0 px, overlap 0 px²**, then 63 px to the speed button and
111 px to the scrubber. So the number has not moved, the 🔁 it nearly touches **does** meet the 44 px
floor, and nothing is dead. **The OVERHEAD cue's own gap is a separate, smaller number:** it resolves
to exactly `CUE_GAP_PX` — **10.5 px** (393×852) and **10.0 px** (360×780) with the deck closed — and
its nearest control of any kind falls from **118.8 px** / **94.7 px** (852×393 / 780×360, closed) to
**37.5 px** / **22.5 px** with the deck open. **Two smaller box-on-box overlaps, both landscape, both
deck-open:** tier-picker × demo-deck **398 px²** (12 × 35), of which **160 px²** lands on the
„Начинаещ" pill itself. It steals no hit point — the pill's centre still hit-tests to the pill — but a
25 px control with a deck corner on it is not a control anyone should have to aim at.

### Seen while looking, not this row's defect

- On a **portrait phone with the deck open**, the deck's own caption card clamps its last line: „Спри
  НАПЪЛНО преди стоп-линията: колелата неподвижни, **броим до три**" renders with the final line cut.
  Inside `[data-hud="demo-deck"]`, present with the message down as well as up, and it moves nothing
  of ours. Belongs to whoever owns the compact deck.
- The **D direction is now driven, not only unit-tested**: pressing the remapped brake in R at a
  standstill toggles back to D and the same card appears for „…тръгваш напред".
- The dev overlay's „1 Issue" on every profile is a **hydration attribute mismatch** on
  `/dev/drive-rig`, plus two THREE.js deprecation warnings and two dev-only CSP notices. `pageerror`
  count is **0** on all four profiles. Pre-existing; nothing in this row touches it.

### Gate — `tsc` 0 · content 0 · tools 290/290 · vitest 2 red, and the third red was not ours

|---|---|

**A third red appeared and was traced rather than reported.** The first full run came back
`3 failed | 773 passed`, the extra being `src/lib/tsconfigHygiene.test.ts`. Cause: a **concurrent
lane started a dev server with `KNIJKA_DIST_DIR=.next-harness` while the suite was running**, and
`next dev` rewrote the shared, tracked `platform/tsconfig.json` to add `.next-harness/types/**` and
`.next-harness/dev/types/**`. Restoring the file and re-running gives the table above. **This is the
third time that mechanism has produced a phantom red in this register** — it is documented in
`platform/AGENTS.md` and it keeps costing a gate an hour. Owner: whoever is on `.next-harness`.

## The gate — SEVEN-LANE VERIFY gate #2, 2026-08-09 (later the same day). **THE RESULT SCREEN IS PHOTOGRAPHED. THE CAMERA ROWS HAVE THE WRONG CULPRIT IN THIS FILE, AND THE FRAME PROVES IT.**

Seven lanes reported: the cockpit camera (**B58 · B27 · B55 · B67 · B35**), the ten penalty points
(**THE-TEN-POINTS** + the corridor + `EMERGENCY_LANE_DRIVING`), the phone (**C1 · C8**), six lesson
rows (**B5 · B23 · B40 · B41 · B64 · B70**), the seven pedestrian streets (**B50 · B53 · B54**), the
look/anisotropy brief, and the citations/tooling lane. **Every headline was disbelieved.** This gate
opened 16 frame files with its own eyes, re-derived the draw-call census itself rather than quoting
either the register or the lane, read the load-bearing predicates out of the shipped source, and ran
the full four-command gate.

### 1 · THE NUMBER HE ASKED FOR TWICE — photographed, both directions

The last gate refused this row for the right reason. It is now closed for the right reason.

| frame | what it reads |
|---|---|
| `scratchpad/ovsr/TENPOINTS-result-3pts-FINAL.png` | **„3 наказателни точки · ИЗДЪРЖАН"** · Опасни грешки (10 т.) **0 / 0** · Основни грешки (3 т.) **1 / 3** · Второстепенни **0 / 0** · Общо (допустими 9) **1 / 3** · ★★☆ · 88 с при ориентир 85 с. Telemetry burned into the frame: `t=87.70s phase=completed obj=3/3 events=2`. `sc-ov-solid-return` @L3. |
| `scratchpad/ovsr/ONCOMING-on-M1-20pts-FINAL.png` | **„20 наказателни точки · НЕИЗДЪРЖАН"** · Опасни грешки **2 / 20** · „Настъпи сблъсък — реалният изпит се прекратява незабавно". Same М1 paint, a closing car, `sc-animal-hazard` @L3. |
| `scratchpad/ovsr/TENPOINTS-teach-first-FINAL.png` | The teach card, in the product's own Bulgarian: **„Първа среща — не се брои в резултата. При повторение: −3 т. (основна грешка)"**. |

**Read those three together, because the first one alone would mislead the lead.** It takes **two**
crossings to put 3 on that screen — the first raises the card, the second bills it. **A single
crossing reads 0.** That is A12 teach-first working as designed, and it is also exactly why still-open
row 6 below (the recommender hole) matters: the encounter we are surest represents a knowledge gap is the
one that reaches the theory recommender as nothing at all.

**Demoting the paint did not leave the danger unmeasured, and that is verified in source, not
inherited.** `runtime/worldRuntime.ts` no longer carries `tick.solidCenterLine !== true` in the
corridor's arming: the predicate is split at **:1824 `ocBase` → :1832 `ocArmed = ocBase`** for the
head-on measurement and **:1834 `orArmed = ocBase && tick.solidCenterLine !== true`** for the OV-09
return tracker, so the corridor widens while the М1 seam stays byte-for-byte. Read here, in the file.

### 2 · THE CAMERA CONSTANT — this register's own diagnosis is wrong, and a lane would have lost a day to it

**Still-open row 7 of the previous gate is measurably false and is corrected below.** It attributes
B58 / B27 / B55 to CameraRig's `rv` quad and concludes *"one camera constant sits between the product
and three rows."* Read out of the shipped source by this gate:

- `LessonScene.tsx:704` — `const [cockpit, setCockpit] = useState(true)`. **Cockpit is the default view.**
- `CameraRig.tsx:964–965` — `const rvSide: MirrorGlanceKind | null = mode === "chase" ? (heldSide ?? "rear") : null;` and at `:966` `if (rvSide === null) { rv.glass.visible = false; rv.bezel.visible = false; … }`.

**In the view he plays, that quad is never drawn.** A fix landed in `chaseRearView.ts` would have moved
nothing he can see. The real occluder is the cabin's own interior mirror — `hotspot_mirror_rear` plus
the code-built pod in `VitokCockpit.tsx` — **which is the very window he asked for in B74/B76.** Same
object, two row numbers.

**And there is no camera constant to turn.** Three independent hard stops, each checked against the
shipped constants: `COCKPIT_CAM_OFFSET = { aft: 0.375, inboard: 0.1, up: 0.05 }` (`vehicle/tuning.ts:555`)
sits inside a band the contract test pins at `inboard ∈ [0.05, 0.15]` (`cockpit-camera-contract.test.ts:156–157`)
— outboard makes the sign's emergence *later*, inboard pushes the graded left door mirror (already at
`fx 0.001`) off the frame; vertically `ROOF_Y = 0.9` (`VitokCockpit.tsx:322`) leaves ~16 mm above the
mirror hood and an unmovable authored GLB casing below; and pitch moves mirror and sign by the same
angle. **~1–2° of travel against a ~7° problem.** The lane refused to ship a cosmetic change so the row
could be closed on it, and that refusal was correct.

**The cheap real fix is not in the camera at all.** `SCENARIO_SIGN_SCALE = 1.5`
(`world/builders/constants.ts:335`, his own „those signs must be big" ruling) scales the sign post
**about its base**, so making the disc 1.5× also raised its centre from ~2.2 m to ~3.30 m — straight
into the mirror's box. Legal Bulgarian mounting is a 0.6 m disc with its lower edge at 2.0–2.2 m.

### 4 · The phone — C1 answered by sweep, C8 answered with a real number

**C1 — the four-surface stack is genuinely gone, and the row's own quoted evidence is a
misdiagnosis.** Landing state, WebKit, real device insets, world pixel-gated before anything was read:
iPhone 16 portrait **2 named surfaces, 0 intersections, 0 covered controls, road 93.6%**; landscape
**1 surface, road 95.4%**; small-portrait 2 / 0. Against the row's 0%. Frame opened:
`scratchpad/p0p7/c1-frames/iphone16-portrait__landing.png` — the road dominates the picture and the
seatbelt pill is now inside the notification column instead of floating free.

The row quoted „«Разбрах» was not tappable (something is painted over it)". **That sentence is the
harness's, not the screen's** — `tools/mobile/lib/routes.mjs:248` prints it for *any* Playwright
refusal, and this gate confirmed the string is hard-coded there. Measured, `elementFromPoint` at the
button's own centre returns the button; the refusal is the actionability STABLE check, because the
notification column swaps content underneath it. **Two things really are still stacked, both named
with coordinates** — see still-open rows below.

**C8 — there is now a real number and it is nowhere near the budget.** 4 of 4 requested runs produced
a full row, zero lost, each gated on pixels showing a rendered street (253–303 distinct colours) before
a phase was read, each number `atRestMs` timestamped **inside the page** so the instrument is excluded
by construction rather than subtracted afterwards, with the probe's own share printed beside it:

```
phase          n   min  median  worst  spread  wall(med)  probe%   over 1200 ms
base(idle)     4   152    340    421     269      570      18%       0 / 4
overlayOpen    4   275    337    507     232      514       2%       0 / 4
overlayClose   4   235    296    382     147      544      10%       0 / 4
worst STATE-CHANGE phase per run: min 275 / median 346 / worst 507 ms   →  0 / 4 over
```

**693 ms of margin, wider than both the run-to-run spread (232 ms) and the instrument's own resolution
step (336 ms).** The „1 198 ms" was never about the driving shell. One honest caveat the lane
volunteered: a fifth idle sample from a colder start read **1 680 ms**, so 1 of 5 *idle* samples is over
— and idle is the phase in which the probe changes nothing.

### 5 · The seven streets — opened at the SPAWN, and he could tell them apart

`E:\AI driver\scratchpad\sf\frames\SHEET-SEEN-spawn.png`, opened by this gate. All seven tiles are
cockpit frames with wheel, cluster and A-pillars. **No pair reads as the same street.**

**And the differentiator is not the weather.** Weather does exactly one of the seven (pe-rain is night
and snow). The other six separate on furniture and traffic: pe-bus a blue panel van plus pickups at the
right kerb and a kerbed median standing in the road; pe-cane both kerbs parked and **every car tail-on**
(the one-way tell); pe-child dark green estates at the bonnet with the left bay empty; pe-dart the only
street whose **near** kerb is the empty one; pe-slow one saloon far out against an open forecourt;
pe-clear both kerbs dense from behind the bonnet.

**The cause was worse than „similar" and it is fixed at source.** `computeParkedCars` hashed
`(edgeIndex, slot)` with **nothing naming the district**, so pe-cane / pe-bus / pe-child seated *the
same three cars, in the same paint, in the same order*, three consecutive lessons.
`districtParkedSalt` (`traffic/TrafficLayer.tsx:410`) now salts the **appearance only** — placement is
deliberately untouched, and this gate confirmed the graded side by diffing the JSON: the seven PE
districts changed **1–7 lines each and not one graded key** (`nodes`, `edges`, `crossings`,
`spawnPoints`, `zones`, `geometry`, `points` — zero hits across all seven diffs).

**What all seven still share, named rather than smoothed over:** the tarmac tone, the dashed centre
line and the white edge lines are one picture in every frame. That is honest, and it is the next lever.

### 6 · The look — it is filtering, not base maps, and this gate re-measured the draw calls itself

`scratchpad/lookfall/frames/PAIR-8m-20m.png` and `AFTER-low.png` opened (the latter a full cockpit
frame, so the crops came from a seat). **The honest reading is PARTIAL and the lane said so first:** at
4× the 8 m pair is nearly indistinguishable; the 20 m pair is visibly grainier but the gain is modest.
It is **not** another base-map pass — the change is one texture filter. A cockpit eye 1.3614 m up meets
the road at 3.9° at 20 m, a 17:1 pixel footprint, and the ground was bound at anisotropy 4, so two mip
levels of detail were fetched and then thrown away. Ground anisotropy now 16, clamped to the device
ceiling: pixel-scale structure +44.9% at 20 m, +51.7% at 12 m, and the 8 m→20 m fall goes −46% → −27%.
Zero draw calls, zero triangles, zero bytes.

**THE DRAW-CALL CENSUS, RE-MEASURED BY THIS GATE THROUGH `buildWorldGeometry` OVER ALL 100 SHIPPED
DISTRICTS, IN BOTH COPIES.** The brief was right to demand it:

```
low-tier soft cap 70 · hard cap 100
  67  hz-roadworks-v1     67  ov-crest-v1     67  pe-zone-v1     67  pk-rail-v1
  66  d2-v1               65  mw-exit-v1      65  ov-oneway-v1   65  pe-cane-v1
WORST 67  ·  HEADROOM 3  ·  over soft cap 0  ·  max triangles 81 528 (32.6% of 250 k)
content/world and platform/public/world agree exactly — no mirror drift
```

**The previous list’s still-open row 11 is wrong twice, and is replaced by still-open row 7 below:** the worst is **67, not 66**, on **four maps
this register does not name**, and headroom is **3, not 4**. `pe-cane` 65 and `d2-v1` 66 both confirmed.

### 9 · A TENTH AND AN ELEVENTH INSTRUMENT DEFECT — and one of them invalidates the aspect of earlier cockpit measurements

1. **`LessonPlayShell` sizes the picture to `PLAY_ASPECT` (16:9) only once its ResizeObserver fires.**
   In a scripted page nothing ever resizes, so the canvas sat at the reading column's full width —
   **1248×520, aspect 2.40** — for entire 60-second drives. At 2.40 the hFOV lock drops vFOV from 47 to
   35.7, which is **not** the frame `cockpit-camera-contract.test.ts` is authored for, and `playArea.ts`'s
   own table says the bands stop holding past aspect 2.00. **Every pixel rectangle in this register that
   cites a 1264×620 or 1248×520 canvas — including still-open row 7's — was measured on that frame.**
   Someone should check whether a real first paint in a real browser also lands there; if it does, it is
   a product defect, not a harness one.
2. **In Playwright's WebKit, once a WebGL canvas's drawing buffer changes SIZE, every subsequent
   screenshot of it is a flat fill, permanently** — isolated to one variable on a page containing none of
   this app, with `gl.readPixels` in the same rAF tick returning a bright frame and `isContextLost()`
   false. That is what „the canvas dies at the resize step, 5 of 5 runs" always was: **the camera, not
   the world.**
3. **`page.evaluate` has no timeout in Playwright** and `setDefaultTimeout` does not apply to it, so the
   reads inside `tools/mobile/lib/ready.mjs` can hang a sweep for ever (observed: 391 s, zero CPU).
4. **An unbuckled car raises a teach modal ~2 s into every drive** that both pauses the scene and shrinks
   the play area — four frames of one lane's first sweep are photographs of that modal.
5. **A scene remount still eats drives under load.** One attempt teleported the car 88 m in 1.06 s while
   vitest was running at 2 workers. Anyone driving for evidence on this box should have nothing else
   running.

In the cockpit view — the default — the `rv` quad is `visible = false`. The occluder is the cabin mirror, the three rows are not one problem, and **B55 is not a mirror row at all.** |

Still PARTIAL.** |

The half that is true is the unmaintained SARS URL. |

Seven lanes reported: the founder's phone (**HIS-ACTUAL-PHONE**), the ten penalty points
(**THE-TEN-POINTS**), seven cockpit rows (**B27 · B35 · B55 · B58 · B60 · B64 · B67**), the seven
pedestrian streets (**B50 · B53 · B54**), the five doors and the sim citations, the look/texture
brief, and the street furniture (**B65**). This gate disbelieved all seven, opened their frames,
re-derived every headline number from raw with predicates written here, and **read Наредба № 38 out
of the ingested act itself rather than out of any lane's table.**

### 1 · The frames — 20 cockpit tiles opened, none refused for a missing seat

**Every row that is about what he sees from the driving seat was answered with a frame that has a
seat in it.** Checked before acceptance: A-pillar, dashboard, steering wheel, windscreen.

| frame opened | what is in it |
|---|---|
| `b50frames/SHEET-CROSS-L3.png` | Seven tiles, each with both A-pillars, dash, wheel, cluster at **0 км/ч D** and the interior mirror. |
| `b50frames/SHEET-SPAWN.png` | Seven tiles, same furniture, all at `y=15.0`. |
| `b50frames/L3__28-pe-bus-CROSS__y76.png` | Full cockpit; pedestrian, zebra, blue bus, kerb railing. |
| `b65furn/B65-before-after.png` | Four cockpit frames (2 before / 2 after) at `y=119` and `y=239` on `sp-creep-v1`, telemetry burned in. |
| `cockpitlane/b67/b67-step01-t36.1-y577-v119.png` | Both A-pillars, wheel, full dash row, door mirror, binnacle reading **„120 км/ч D"**, 140 roundel lit, at 119.9 km/h. |

**Two frames were correctly NOT cockpit frames and are accepted as such**
(`mlfold/mlBEFORE|mlAFTER3/iphone16-portrait--practice--text4-q-predimstvo-042.png`): the phone rows
are about the theory app's own chrome, no simulator is involved, and a cockpit would be the wrong
evidence. That is not the `/dev/scene-still` failure the earlier gates refused three times.

**B67's premise is dead, and the frame kills it.** The look-wave recorded „at motorway speed there is
NO instrument cluster in frame at all". Opened: at **119.9 km/h** the cluster, wheel, dash and both
A-pillars are all in shot. The lane's reverse-view-glance hypothesis (a held or leaked `KeyF`
producing every one of those headrest frames at any speed) is the first explanation anyone has
offered that also survives a throttle-shut coast. It is a hypothesis, it is unproven, and it is
cheap to test.

### 2 · THE TEN POINTS — his complaint is real, and this gate found it independently

**Read out of `content/law/acts/naredba-38.json` here, not taken from any lane.** приложение № 5
към чл. 36, ал. 1, т. 1 is the act's only fault classification. Its б. „в" reads „за опасна грешка
се поставят 10 наказателни точки **в следните случаи:**" and then enumerates **exactly six** cases —
counted in the ingested text: the light/controller signal, entering against traffic at a junction or
**one-way** road, failing to stop at **Б2**, the commission intervening, creating a **предпоставка за
допускане на ПТП**, and exceeding the limit by **more than 10 km/h**.

`CROSSED_SOLID_LINE` charged 10 and matched **none of the six**. Verified clause by clause:

* **Case 2 is expressly excluded by its own words** — the clause names „път с **ЕДНОПОСОЧНО**
  движение", and `engine.ts` arms the detector only when `tick.oneway === false`.
* **Case 5 was the only candidate and the detector establishes no предпоставка** — authored М1 span,
  opposing bank, moving, forward gear, sustained 0.6 s, **no other road user required and none
  queried**. `solidLineCrossSustainSec: 0.6` is documented in `types.ts` as a paint-flicker guard: it
  answers „did he really cross", never „was it dangerous".
* **The catalogue convicted itself, and this gate re-read both entries to confirm it.**
  `OVERTAKING_IN_BAN_ZONE` — знак **В24**, the explicit statutory ban on overtaking — requires a
  denoised lane change **and a real lead vehicle** (`overtakeBeat(cfg.banOvertakeLeadGapM)`,
  `engine.ts:852-857`) and bills **3**. The paint out-charged the sign it merely repeats.

**Now основна (3).** `SPEEDING_DANGEROUS` was checked against case 6 and matches to the letter:
`dangerousSpeedOverKmh: 10`, detector `speed > bands.dangerousAbove`, strictly greater.
`PEDESTRIAN_CROSSING_TOO_FAST` requires `z.pedestrianSeen` — a real pedestrian, a real предпоставка.

**The brief's own number was wrong and the lane was right: 53 codes, not 52.** Parsed from
`catalog.ts`: **14 опасна / 22 основна / 17 второстепенна** after the ruling (15/21/17 before).

### 4 · The seven streets — answered honestly, and not upgraded

**At the crossing they are seven different crossings.** Opened `SHEET-CROSS-L3.png`: three refuge
islands (2.4 / 2.0 / 2.2 m, all three present in the shipped JSON — re-read here), three raised-table
plateaus, two skewed zebras (+18°, −12°). He could tell these apart.

**Two things this gate will not let the row claim.**
1. **The strongest differentiator on that sheet is the weather, not the furniture.** `pe-rain` reads
   as a different street instantly because it is night and snowing. Strip the weather and the skew
   and the remaining separation is thinner than the table implies.
2. **`pe-bus`'s refuge island is in the data and is NOT legible from the seat.** Opened its own
   full-resolution frame at 11 m: the zebra reads as unbroken white bars. The island exists
   (`island: {widthM: 2, approachM: 9, departM: 9}`) — it is 2.0 m with a 9 m nose, the most compact
   in the family, and at that distance and angle it does not read. **A field in the JSON is not a
   thing he can see.**

**At the spawn `pe-bus`, `pe-child` and `pe-cane` still read as one composition** — same right-hand
parked row, same white-car-then-red-car, same mid-rise slabs, same dashed centreline. The lane said
three of seven and **three of seven is what the sheet shows.** `pe-clear` is close behind them.
The rows stay **PARTIAL-SEEN**. This is not an upgrade.

### 5 · The look — it is object work, it is real, and the cost is measured

Opened `B65-before-after.png`. **This is not another lighting constant, and it is not texture work
either.** The road surface is visually identical between the two columns; what changed is that
objects now exist. At `y=239` BEFORE, the left of the windscreen is open green field with one lone
tree and the carriageway running to a bare horizon. AFTER: frontage on both kerbs to the end of the
street, lamp columns, an overhead line, a pavement parapet, varied tree silhouettes.

**The texture lane shipped nothing and said so.** Its measurement stands and is the more useful
deliverable: the surfaces are *not* untextured — road, ground and sidewalk all bind PBR
colour+normal+roughness — but detail **collapses with distance** (against a validated flat control
of 0.16 and a detailed control of 5.02, the carriageway measures 1.34–1.60 at 8 m and **0.72–0.93 at
20 m**). The next lane should fix falloff, not author new base maps.

**THE COST — measured in this gate, through `buildWorldGeometry`, against `environment/perfBudget.ts`.**

`low` tier (the phone — **the tier his FPS complaint is about**): **70 draws soft / 100 hard,
250 000 tris soft / 300 000 hard.**

**Verdict on cost: it does not drop frames, but it has eaten most of the remaining headroom.**
Triangles are comfortable everywhere — the worst map is at **33% of the low-tier soft budget**. Draw
calls are not: `pe-cane` at **65** and `d2-v1` at **66** sit **5 and 4 calls under the soft cap**.
The whole furniture pass cost **+3 draws**, each mounted only when its list is non-empty, and
**`public/` gained zero bytes** — verified by `git status`: not one new binary asset, only world JSON.
**One more instanced kind added to the PE family breaches the soft cap.** That is the number the next
lane needs and it is why this table is here rather than a sentence saying „within budget".

*(Correction to the B65 lane's own table: it reported 59–63 draws. Measured here, the range is
**59–66** — `pe-cane` 65 and `d2-v1` 66 were not in it.)*

**THE PROBE HAD TO PROVE ITS OWN REACH BEFORE THE GREEN WAS WORTH ANYTHING** — a check that reads

### 7 · A NINTH INSTRUMENT DEFECT — this gate's own, and it is the same bug for the third time

**My first enumeration reported 34 leaks. All 34 were false.** Naive `includes()` over the serialised
row matches a forbidden row's whole option text against a *fragment inside a longer approved one*:
„Движение на заден ход" (21 chars), „Около 10 метра" (14), „На кръстовище на равнозначни пътища" (35).
All three rows were `status=approved`. Exact field comparison takes it to **0**.

This is the third time in this programme that the same class of instrument error has produced a
confident wrong number — the predecessor's 38 phantom leaks, its 331 phantom citation refusals from
an ASCII `\b` against Cyrillic, and now these 34. **The lesson is not „be careful"; it is that a
probe reporting a defect must be made to fail on a known-clean input before its output is quoted.**

*(A second, smaller one: this gate's first perf probe read `world.skidMarks` and reported 0 for
every map. The field is `world.stats.skidMarks`. Re-measured before anything was written down —
`sp-creep-v1` is **6** and `d2-v1` is **66**, exactly what the B65 lane claimed. The lane was right
and the instrument was wrong.)*

## The gate — VERIFY-BY-DRIVING, 2026-08-05 (the fourth pass of the day). **ALL SIX CHECKS DRIVEN. `tsc` IS 0. THE SIMULATOR TREE IS GREEN AND EVERY RED IN THE REPO BELONGS TO ANOTHER WAVE.**

The last gate refused five claims, three of them „`tsc` exit 0" from three different lanes. So this
one ran every command itself and pasted the counts, and it drove every check instead of reading a
diff: **21 browser drives on the shipped `LessonPlayShell` — two of them on the real keyboard
channel rather than the rig's injected axes — and 12 frames opened and read, not merely written.**
Nothing below is quoted from a lane's report.

**The four defects this wave fixed are recorded as `D1`–`D4` below, as rows in their own right.**
None of them is one of his 107. The reverse bug, the silent minute, the six-metre gap between the
grader and the paint, and the two lessons that could not be finished were all found by measuring or
by driving — and **a register that only tracks what he happened to notice will keep missing the
things he did not.** The 107-row count is deliberately left alone; these are a separate ledger.

### D1 — a held brake became a floored reverse accelerator, and the input that triggered it is the single most correct thing this product teaches

| | |
|---|---|
| **What was wrong** | `ReverseAssist` toggled D→R after 0.35 s of a HELD brake at a standstill; the in-R pedal swap then handed that same held pedal to the reverse throttle. `VehicleSim`'s R branch does `engineTotal = −REVERSE_FORCE_N · throttle` = **−3000 N, capped at 25 km/h**. Standing on the brake at a Б2 line — the behaviour a whole lesson exists to build — accelerated the car backwards at 16.8 km/h into whatever was behind it, and stripped its service brake at the same instant. |
| **Fix** | Two laws in the INPUT PATH, not in the trigger. **LAW 1:** a press arms only on its rising edge, only at a standstill, and only after `REVERSE_ASSIST_LIFT_S` = 0.25 s of a genuinely lifted pedal at rest. **LAW 2 (`ReversePedalMapper`):** whichever channel inherits the throttle role at a remap flip is DISOWNED until genuinely released — zero throttle, and it goes on braking with the value the driver is applying. Deliberately asymmetric: a held pedal may always gain „stop", never „go". `GatedSimInput.read()` now goes through the mapper, so the `[`/`]` keys, the touch gear sheet and the cockpit lever are covered too. |
| **Driven, not asserted** | `sc-junction-stop@L1`, brake held continuously for **34.98 s / 1955 frames**: `brake` **1.0000 on every one of them**, `throttle` **0.0000 on every one of them**, gear values seen **`[1]` — D only, never −1**, max \|v\| **0.0156 km/h**, position drift **0.0011 m**, **0 collisions, 0 violations, 0 session-clock resets.** It then drove off and earned `FULL_STOP_AT_STOP_SIGN` „Правилно спиране на знак Б2". |
| **Provoked the other way** | Real keyboard, `KeyS` pressed at 20.6 km/h and **never lifted for 52 s**: `[` tapped twice mid-hold → D→N→**R** — **925 frames in R, max \|v\| 0.0010 km/h, y span 0.0000 m**; `]` twice back to D → **456 frames, same zeros**. The measured-pedal repeat (brake injected by the rig so the value is in the record): **1227 frames in gear R at `brk` 1.00, y span 0.0000 m.** Then foot off 1.5 s, select R, press again → the car reverses to 8.9 km/h. **The feature the founder asked for on 2026-07-17 still works; only the gesture changed: stop, lift, press.** |
| **Frames** | `scratchpad/verify/b2hold-step01-t35.9-v0.png` — cockpit, **0 км/ч, D**, `thr=0.00 brk=1.00 events=0`, step „HOLD BRAKE 35s". · `scratchpad/verify/prov2-D-R-plus20s.png` — reverse camera swung round, `gear=−1 v=0.0 brk=1.00`, and **„Кола отзад · 2 м"** on screen: a car two metres off the rear bumper, which is exactly what the old code drove into. |
| **Pinned** | `engine/__tests__/reverseAssist.test.ts` — 30 tests, including the 30 s Б2 hold, the rolling press, the sub-threshold lift, the pre-drive handover, 600 frames of a brake held into R, and the mirror case (braking to a stop mid-reverse must not pop the car back into D). |

### D2 — the minute in which the student is most correct was the minute the product said nothing

| | |
|---|---|
| **What was wrong** | A lawful give-way wait produced no words at all. The engine knew the driver was holding — `YieldWaitState` published a boolean — but nothing downstream could say WHAT he was waiting for, so the screen was silent for the whole wait and then graded him on the way out. That is a requirement-zero violation (doc 64 THEO-4) in the one place it matters most. |
| **Fix** | `YieldWaitState` now publishes a `reason`; `advisor.ts` gained `YIELD_VOICE_COPY` (five reasons × four stages), `yieldWaitAdvisorPrompt`, and the pure `stepYieldVoice` fold, folded by the engine only when `!examMode` — the advisor's own single gate, not a new one. Three stages: **name the duty at 1.2 s**, **confirm at 10 s that the waiting IS the manoeuvre**, **judge the gap 4 s after the wheels turn** — and the verdict is dropped silently if a yield-family fault is graded inside that window. |
| **The first thing it says — transcribed from the screen, not from the source** | Wheels stopped at t = 24.27 s. At **t = 25.24 s, 0.97 s later**, verbatim: „**📚 НАУЧИ · Защо чакаш: в кръга имат предимство** — Спрял си правилно. На входа на кръгово кръстовище не може да стои знак „Път с предимство" — там винаги е Б1 или Б2, тоест ти си на пътя без предимство и пропускаш движещите се в кръга. Гледай НАЛЯВО. Интервалът, който чакаш, е такъв, че да влезеш и да набереш скоростта на кръга, без движещият се в него да намалява заради теб." + the chip „ЗДвП чл. 50, ал. 1; Наредба № РД-02-21-1/2023 правила за поставяне на знак Б3". **It is not a number. It names the duty, the reason, where to look and what a good gap is.** |
| **And it does not nag** | **60.01 s of standing still produced exactly two lines.** The second, at t = 33.94 s: „Чакането Е маневрата — Стоиш вече 10 секунди и това е правилно… Тези секунди не ти струват нито точка и се изваждат от ориентировъчното време на урока". Then **nothing for the remaining 50 s.** The advisor card is constant for the whole wait by design: „Чакаш правилно — в кръга имат предимство. Гледай НАЛЯВО и тръгвай, когато можеш да влезеш, без някой в кръга да намалява заради теб." After the wheels turned, one verdict at t = 88.48: „Интервалът беше добър". |
| **All three other controls driven too** | **Б2** (`sc-junction-stop`): „Защо чакаш: знак Б2 „Спри! Пропусни движението!"" at t = 36.62 (0.83 s after the stop) → „Чакането Е маневрата" at t = 45.42 → 25 s of silence → „Спря докрай и пропусна" at t = 74.99. **Б1** (`sc-jx-giveway-b1`): „Защо чакаш: знак Б1 „Пропусни движението"" t = 75.85 → „Чакането Е маневрата" t = 84.63 → „Пропусна и тръгна в истински интервал" t = 102.24. |
| **Exam mode is silent, and that was driven, not trusted** | `sc-roundabout-entry@L4` („Изпитни условия · ИЗПИТ"), stopped on the same paint, **45.01 s at a standstill: ZERO new lines of text on screen.** Grepped the transcript for every voice string — „Защо чакаш", „Чакането Е маневрата", „Интервалът беше добър", „Чакаш правилно", „НАУЧИ" — **all absent.** |
| **Frames** | `scratchpad/verify/rbfull2-step01-t26.1-v0.png` — cockpit at the paint, 0 км/ч D, `brk=1.00`, the advisor card in the top rail and the circulating car visible in the ring. · `scratchpad/verify/exam-A-wait-t30.png` — the same pose in exam mode: **no card, no toast, no shadow line, no route line, `events=0`.** |
| **One thing the transcript caught that the source does not** | During the wait the **shadow-demo subtitle track keeps talking on a 49 s loop** — „Приближаваме кръговото — намали отрано и гледай наляво" / „Кола се движи в кръга — тя е с предимство. Спри и я пропусни." / „Тя премина — влизаме плътно след нея, в нейния интервал." Correct in content and it is the demo's own voice, not the advisor's, but he asked for EVERY word and these are words. Recorded here rather than filtered out. |
| **Pinned** | `lessons/__tests__/yield-voice.test.ts` — 21 tests, including two minutes of continuous waiting producing exactly two lines, the creep-and-restop episode rule, the rolling give-way that is never lectured, the exam silence, the verdict dropped on a graded fault, and the citation rules (retrieved byte-identical to the graded card; no article number on an act the repo does not hold; the roundabout never cites the retracted чл. 50а). |

### D3 — the roundabout grader was armed six metres inside the paint, so the student's whole decision happened where the instrument was blind

| | |
|---|---|
| **What was wrong** | The entry tracker armed at `ringRadius + ROUNDABOUT_ENTRY_MARGIN_M` — a constant bolted to the RING. The give-way paint is bolted to the MOUTH, and on `rb-mini-v1` it is at **35.73 m** from the ring centre against an armed reach of **30.00 m**. A student who stops exactly where the road tells him to stop stands **outside the instrument** — so his stop, his wait and the circulator he let past were all invisible, and `YIELDED_TO_PRIORITY` was unreachable by construction. **19 of 22 mouths across the six shipped rings were outside.** |
| **Fix** | `roundaboutGiveWayReachM` derives an OBSERVATION reach per ring from that ring's own М7 line plus the М18 triangle run. `ROUNDABOUT_ENTRY_MARGIN_M` keeps its old job as the COMMIT radius and nothing else: a violation may still only fire, and its sustain clock may still only start, inside it. **The change is additive on the punishing side.** |
| **Driven — stop on the paint, wait, pull out clean** | `sc-roundabout-entry@L1`. Stopped at (4.06, −37.31) = **37.53 m from the ring centre** (the origin sample; the bumper is on the 35.73 m paint). Stood **30.01 s**. Pulled out on a clear gap, circulated, and `YIELDED_TO_PRIORITY` „Правилно отстъпено предимство" landed at (27.29, −12.51) = **30.02 m — exactly the unchanged commit radius.** **Zero yield violations on that drive.** |
| **Driven — the barge still convicts** | Same map, same rung: stop 10 s, then floor it. `FAILED_TO_YIELD <roundabout>` **опасна, 10 т.** at (4.06, −24.31) = **24.65 m** — inside the commit radius, outside the ring — followed **0.74 s later** by `COLLISION <vehicle>`, which is the independent proof that the car really was there. Frame `scratchpad/verify/barge3-card-t47.0.png`. |
| **Honest caveat, because it looks like a miss and is not** | Two barges that **never slowed at all** (21.6 and 13.6 km/h straight through) produced **no conviction — and no collision either.** The staged circulator was not in the band at the moment of entry, so entering was lawful and the detector was right to stay quiet. It does mean a live barge is not reproducible on demand; see the open list. |
| **The traces: the answer is SIX, not five, and none of them moved** | The margin did **not** move — the watch radius was added BESIDE it — so nothing needed re-recording. Verified rather than argued: `RECORD_TRACES=1` over all six roundabout gates (`sc-roundabout-entry`, `sc-rb-busy-gap`, `sc-rb-circulate-priority`, `sc-rb-exit-signal`, `sc-rb-lane-choice`, `sc-rb-ped-exit`) → **6 files, 81 tests, green**, and `git status content/traces platform/public/traces` afterwards shows **zero roundabout traces changed.** All 18 (6 shadow + 12 mistake) came back byte-identical. **This register has been saying „five shadow traces" and there are six.** |
| **New finding, and it only shows up if you open the frame** | The conviction is now produced by the right adjudicator and **explained by the wrong law.** See the open list, row 1. |

### D4 — two lessons could not be finished at all, and both now finish clean

| | |
|---|---|

Result screen: **„0 наказателни точки · ИЗДЪРЖАН"**, ★★★, 88 с в ориентира от 100 с. Frame `scratchpad/verify/queue2-end-t87.6-v6.png`. |

Before this detector existed the entire rule log for that drive was a `CLEAN_DRIVING` commendation on the way into the queue. Frame `scratchpad/verify/queue-card-t57.2.png`, with the telemetry burned into the same PNG: `v=19.6km/h y=243.9 lead=6.4m = 1.17s`. |
| **Honest caveat** | A rig that holds 20 km/h and **never lifts** still ends in two collisions — correctly. The drill's whole subject is lifting; the pass above is a drive that lifts, and the warning above is what the student gets 2 s before if he does not. |
