## The gate — THREE-LANE COCKPIT gate, 2026-08-05 (later the same day). **EVERY FRAME HAD A SEAT IN IT. ONE LANE WAS RIGHT TO REFUSE ITS OWN BRIEF, AND THE TREE IS GREEN.**

Three lanes reported — the roundabout shape (**B16**), the priority road's speed repeat (**B34**), and
the seven pedestrian streets (**B50 / B53 / B54**). This gate disbelieved all three, opened every frame
they cited, and **re-derived the two headline measurements from the shipped code rather than reading
their tables.**

### First, the thing the last gate had to refuse three times: the frames

**Not one frame this round was shot through `/dev/scene-still`, and none had to be refused.** The last
gate threw out B27, B34 and B60 because a world fact proved in a wide-angle still does not answer a row
about what a driver sees. Every frame below was checked for the furniture before it was accepted —
**both A-pillars, the dashboard, the wheel, the instrument cluster and the interior mirror.**

| frame | what is in it |
|---|---|
| `b16round/shots/b16-before-canvas-y-45.png` | Both A-pillars, dash, wheel, cluster at **0 км/ч D**, interior mirror, the Б1 give-way triangle on the right kerb. Stopped 23.7 m off the ring. |
| `b34lane/shots2/b34lane-xm19-t59.3-v14.png` | Both A-pillars, dash, wheel, cluster at **14 км/ч D**, left wing mirror, telemetry burned in: `t=59.62s v=13.6km/h x=-23.0 y=4.2 hdg=279°`. The В26 «50» repeat standing in the windscreen. |
| `zebra/frames/SHEET-PE-7-CARRIAGEWAY.png` | Seven tiles, each with A-pillar, wheel, dash and cluster. |
| `b16round/shots/b16-after46-canvas-y-73.8.png` · `b16-r31-canvas-y-58.8.png` | Same cockpit, two candidate ring radii at an equal 23.7 m to the kerb. |

### The measurement I re-derived instead of believing — and it refutes this register's own number

**Every district in the corpus was swept, not the six the lane chose.** `content/world` holds 100
districts and **exactly six register a ring.** Running the shipped `analyzeNetwork` +
`analyzeRoundabouts` over all of them, sampling the circle in 0.1° buckets:

| district | ring R | arm half-width | kerb arc (sum) | kerb arc (union) |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `rb-mini-v1` | 17.85 m | 8.13 m | **152.9°** | 152.8° |
| `rb-ped-v1` | 17.85 m | 8.13 m | **152.9°** | 152.8° |
| `rb-2lane-v1` | 25.78 m | 16.25 m | **109.1°** | 108.8° |
| `rb-single-v1` | 37.68 m | 8.13 m | **254.1°** | 254.4° |
| `district-v1` `rb-1` (real Sofia OSM) | 19.01 m | **4.06 m** | **252.8°** | 252.8° |
| `d2-v1` `rb-1` (OSM, island refused) | 26.69 m | 4.06–12.13 m | **127.1°** | 187.3° |

Those reproduce the 153 / 254 figures this register has carried since 2026-08-03, so it is the same
measure. **Two things fall out of it that no lane had to be trusted for.**

**1. This register's own fix number is wrong, and it has been wrong in print for two gates.** Line
"`rb-mini` needs radius ≈ **31 m** instead of 18" does not reach the 270° bar it was written to
satisfy. At R = 31 the mouths are `4 × 2 × asin(9.625 / 35.06)` = 127.5°, leaving **232.4° of kerb —
37.6° short.** Solving `asin((armHalf + 1.5) / (R + ringHalf)) = 11.25°` gives the real break-even:
**R = 45.3 m** for the three single-lane families (ship 46 → 271.3°) and **R = 83.0 m** for `rb-2lane`
(ship 84 → 271.1°). That is **2.6× and 3.2×**, not 1.7×. Corrected in place below.

**2. The builder is innocent and the proof is in the table.** `district-v1`'s real-Sofia ring gets
**252.8°** from the same code at a nearly identical **R = 19.01** — because its arms register at
**4.06 m** half-width where `rb-mini`'s register at **8.13 m**. The generators author the arms at
`PERCEPTUAL_ROAD_SCALE` 2.5 and the ring radius at real-world scale. **The mouths are not too wide;
the ring is too small for the roads we drew into it** — and 18 × 2.5 = 45 is within half a metre of
the measured break-even.

### What I refused, and what I refused to reverse

- **B16 stays BROKEN-SEEN and nothing was landed — which was the right call, not a failure.** The lane
  proved the change needs three `lessons/scenario/templates-*.ts` files that pin the ring **by value**
  (`templates-flow.ts` hard-codes `acceptBeforeMarkM 1.725` against the district's М8 bars at
  `y=-35.725`; `templates-roundabout.ts` declares `RING_R = 18`), all of `content/world`, **36 trace
  files**, and that it would red two **untracked** runtime probes another lane is writing this minute
  which hard-code `MOUTH = { x: 0, y: -18 }`. I verified the blast radius is untouched: all four
  `rb-*` districts and their public copies, all 18 committed roundabout traces and their 18 published
  copies, the four generators and the three templates are **byte-identical to HEAD**.
- **A lane's cockpit note that outranks its own brief.** I opened both candidate frames at an equal
  23.7 m to the kerb. **At R = 46 the top-down is a perfect annulus and the windscreen reads worse
  than at R = 31** — a 41.5 m island fills the horizon as parkland and the ring's curvature leaves the
  glass, so it reads as a T-junction. **270° is a top-down metric.** Which shape he meant is a ruling,
  and this gate will not guess it. Both frames are cited on the row.
- **B50 / B53 / B54 stay PARTIAL. I opened the contact sheet and I am not reversing the last gate out
  of politeness.** `pe-rain` (night, rain, bare verge) and `pe-clear` (widest at the seat, low tan
  arcade) are unmistakable. **`pe-dart`, `pe-slow`, `pe-cane`, `pe-bus` and `pe-child` still read as
  one composition** — grey-and-gold slab blocks either side, one dashed line down the middle, one row
  of parked cars on the right at varying distance. The differences are real and you have to look for
  them. Five of seven is not seven.
- **Two numbers in the lanes' own reports are wrong and are corrected here rather than reprinted.**
  (a) B34 reported "**four** new В26" on `tj-emerge-v1`; its own enumeration lists three and its own
  6 → 9 total is +3. I measured **three**, all at station 35.13 m from the node. (b) B50 reported the
  spawn "byte-identical to HEAD on all seven"; **x, y and heading are — the record is not.** On six of
  the seven the spawn's `edgeId` moved `pe-e-street` → `pe-e-street-s1` (`-s2` on `pe-child`), and
  `pe-e-street` itself lost its first 18–55 m to the new chain. Not a defect — see the traces below —
  but the register will not carry "byte-identical" for a field that changed.

### The trace question, answered by opening a trace instead of counting files

**PE: 0 of 22 committed traces re-recorded, and that is correct.** The rule is that a district whose
geometry moved must have its traces re-recorded, and this geometry did move — the street is now a
chain of collinear segments and six districts carry 4 m kerbside bays. I opened
`content/traces/sc-crossing-white-cane/shadow-correct.trace.json`: **918 samples, and `x` is 4.0600 on
every one of them**, y running 15.00 → 135.95, eight events that read exactly as the drill does (the
white cane steps out, full stop, „търпение", he clears, done). `edgeParkingWidthM` adds the band
**outside** `edgeTravelHalfWidth`, so the rail the trace drives never moved — and the traces prove it
by replaying green through the production stack. **Roundabout: 0 of 36 needed, because nothing landed.**

## The gate — SEVEN-LANE INSTRUMENT gate, 2026-08-05. **THE INSTRUMENTS ARE REAL. THE TREE IS STILL RED, AND THIS TIME ONE RED IS OURS.**

Seven lanes reported. This gate disbelieved all seven, **opened 20 of their frames**, and — because
the whole point of this wave was two new instruments — **drove those instruments itself three times
instead of reading anyone's account of them.**

### The two instruments, driven by the gate rather than believed

**1. Does `/dev/drive-rig` really put per-tick telemetry and a fault card on the same screen? YES.**
I drove `sc-roundabout-entry@L1` with my own script and deliberately barged the mouth. The card that
came up carries the live readout burned into the same PNG:
`scratchpad/VERIFY/vbarge-card-t24.0.png` — the teach modal „Пътнотранспортно произшествие" (ЗДвП
чл. 20, ал. 2) with `t=23.96s v=0.3km/h x=4.2 y=-15.3 gear=1 phase=driving obj=1/2 events=1 thr=1.00
step=8 "BARGE +5.6"` in the bottom-left corner. **That is the thing no route in this product could do
before: the conviction and the car's state, in one image, at one instant.**

**2. Does the finish gate now survive a 60-second wait at a give-way line? YES — I waited it out.**
`scratchpad/VERIFY/vwait2-step07-t82.7-v0.png` and its telemetry: **70.42 s of continuous
standstill** on the М8 paint at `y=−36.2`; `y` moved **6 mm** across the whole wait (−36.177 …
−36.171), `x` never moved at all, `gear` stayed 1 (D) so the reverse-assist never grabbed it, `phase`
stayed `driving` on every one of 4 077 samples, **0 clock resets**, and **0 events during the wait**.
The entire 96-second drive fired **one** event — the approach objective completing at t=18.96. His
„about 40 seconds" now has a 70-second answer.

**3. And the detector still convicts, which is the other half nobody may skip.** My own barge did
*not* draw a yield fault — I mistimed the circulator and hit the central island instead
(`COLLISION` at `y=−15.37`, no `FAILED_TO_YIELD` anywhere in the run). **That is corroboration, not a
miss:** it says the fault does not fire when there is no car to yield to. The other direction is
photographed in the B15 lane's own frame, which I opened — `b15-ROUNDABOUT-FINAL/b15a-card-t76.9.png`
— „Непропускане на пътно превозно средство с предимство", ЗДвП чл. 47, with `lead=3.2m = 0.89s @
13km/h` and `events=1` inside the same frame. **Fires with a car, silent without one.**

### Something the instrument caught that no lane reported

My **first** 70-second wait was silently destroyed and the rig said so out loud:

> `!! SESSION CLOCK RESET 2× — the scene remounted mid-drive. This telemetry is`
> `!! more than one drive spliced together; re-run before trusting any row of it.`

Measured: at `t=56.07 s`, standing still at the paint, the session clock jumped to 0 and the car
teleported `y=−36.17 → y=0` (the district-origin placeholder pose) and then to `y=−93` (spawn). It
happened twice in one run. **The re-run was clean — 0 resets — so the cause is almost certainly a
Fast Refresh from another lane editing source on the shared `:3200` dev server, not a product
defect.** I am recording it anyway for one reason: **every long drive on a shared dev server can be
spliced without the driver noticing, and this guard is the only thing standing between that and a
confident wrong answer in this register.** Two waves of B15 evidence were produced on exactly that
server. The guard is the best thing either instrument lane built.

### What I refused

| # | the claim | why it is refused |
|---:|---|---|
| 1 | **"`npx tsc --noEmit` → EXIT 0"** — asserted by three separate lanes | **It exits 2, with 9 errors.** Seven are in `src/modules/sim/world/__tests__/pe-districts.test.ts` — a **tracked, modified product test**: `PeMapCase` gained `carriageway`, `seatCurbToCurbM`, `widthChangesAtM` and `parkedBodies`, and seven of its `CASES` were never updated to match. Two more are in untracked `src/modules/lesson/__tests__/zzprobe-{citations,resolve}.test.ts`. The `zz-b29-*` files three lanes blamed are **gone** — that half of their story was true and has been cleaned up. **This red is ours, it is in the parked-bodies/world lane, and it is the first time in four gates that the tree's red belongs to us rather than to a neighbouring wave.** |
| 2 | **"no PNGs in the repo" / "zero PNGs added to the repo"** — B29/B30/B31 lane | **Two are.** `tools/clips/headless/sheets/sc-jx-giveway-b1__m0.strip.png` and `__m1.strip.png`, both written 23:55 by that lane's clip render, both showing as `??`. **In fairness, and this matters:** that directory already held 48 untracked strips dating from 21–26 July, so it is an un-gitignored render-output convention rather than a new sin. The claim as written is still false, and **the fix is one line in `.gitignore`, not a scolding.** |
| 3 | **`tsconfigHygiene`** — claimed clean by one lane, "four scratch globs" by the register | **Six globs, three families, and a family that is new since the last gate.** `platform/tsconfig.json` `include` now carries `.next-harness/**`, `.next-rig/**` **and `.next-exhdr/**`. Item 10 of the last STILL OPEN said four; it is six. Reverting is futile while those dev servers run — they re-inject on the next compile — so this needs the servers stopped and the file stripped in the same minute. |
| 4 | **"`KNIJKA_DIST_DIR` … passes in isolation in 903 ms — not a code failure"** — the drive-rig lane | **Half true, and the false half matters.** It *is* a timeout rather than an assertion failure, so the safety property still holds — that much was honest. But **it does not pass in isolation**: run alone, three times out of three, it failed at **22.2 s / 26.8 s / 39.5 s** against vitest's 5 s default. The lane wrote off a permanently-red test as a flake on the strength of one lucky run. **On this box it fails every time, and one `testTimeout` argument fixes it.** |
| 5 | **B15-finish-gate "FIXED-SEEN", proven by the 61-second gw-shell frame** — the finish-gate lane | **Downgraded in its reasoning, not in its verdict.** I read the frame's own sidecar (`frames/B15AFTER-11-wait61-SIXTY-SECONDS.json`): `yieldWaitSec: 66.57`, `yieldHolding: true`, `phase: "driving"`, `resultScreen: false` — **but `finishGate.armed: false` and `finishRescueGate.armed: false`.** The session survived 61 s at a **disarmed** stop, which is not the same as surviving the armed gate the fix targets. **The lane said this itself, in its own words, unprompted — that is the behaviour this gate wants and it is why the row still closes.** The armed path is pinned by unit test, not by a photograph, and the row says so now. |

That is a first for this register and it deserves to be said as plainly as the failures.

## The gate — SIX-LANE VERIFY-BY-DRIVING, 2026-08-04. **THE TREE IS RED, AND NOT ONE RED IS OURS.**

Six lanes reported closing the last of his open rows. This gate disbelieved all six and opened
**36 frames** — **26 of theirs and 10 I cropped myself**, plus pixel-column sampling on 5 frames and
a lit-lens cluster scan on 3, because a lane that hands you its own zoom has chosen the crop.
**Six claims were refused, one verdict is stale, and the two rows he checks first — the lights — hold
up under measurement.**

### What I sampled myself, rather than taking a lane's word for it

The lights are the headline, so I re-derived them from the pixels. I wrote a lens scanner that finds
lit heads **by colour cluster anywhere in the windscreen**, instead of sampling where a lane pointed.

| lesson | station | what the scanner found | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| **B37** мигащо жълто, approach y −45.8 | the drill's own first objective | amber lens `rgb(218,172,87)` against unlit red `(141,52,49)` and green `(51,116,65)` | **lit** |
| **B37** at the graded stop line y −29.8 | 10 s later | same head, amber now `rgb(136,94,30)` — the same band as the unlit red beside it | **dark** |
| **B39** спане на зелено, y −46.8 | where instruction 1 says «свети ЗЕЛЕНО» | green `rgb(113,193,115)` vs red `(134,46,44)`, amber `(136,97,44)` | **green** |
| **B39** at the stop line y −29.8 | ten seconds on | amber `rgb(218,171,81)`, red and green unlit | **amber** |

Two frames ten seconds apart therefore photograph **the blink itself**. „Мигащо жълто" is a thing on
screen now, not only in the card text. **B37 and B39 are FIXED-SEEN and I confirm them.**

### The six claims this gate refused

1. **The signs lane never photographed a cockpit.** B27, B34 and B60 were all shot through
   `/dev/scene-still` — no A-pillar, no dashboard, no windscreen frame, no lesson UI, and a wider
   field than the cockpit's `COCKPIT_HFOV_RAD`. The sub-claim those frames *do* support is real and
   worth keeping — the Б2 octagon is unmistakable at 45 m on `tj-stop-v1`, two signs stand in
   `tj-emerge-v1`'s opening windscreen — but the question each row actually asks is *can he see it
   from the seat*, and a frame with no seat in it cannot answer that. Their frames stay in the record
   as world evidence; the rows stay open.
2. **B60's evidence belongs to B59.** The signs lane wrote up „no actual Sign stating 30 exists on the
   sidewalk" as B60. That sentence is **B57/B59**, and B59 has been FIXED-SEEN since 2026-08-02.
   **B60 is „no kids are playing on the sidewalks and we should do that"** — a different complaint, on
   the same catalog position, which that lane did not touch. B60 is unchanged.
3. **The B55 mirror mechanism is not in the frame that was cited for it.** The lights lane reported
   that at `pe-jay-v1` y −2.8 the far-side head „falls behind the interior rear-view mirror housing",
   and used it to corroborate B27's cause. I opened that frame: **there is no mirror in it.** What
   actually hides the left-hand head there is the lesson's **own keyboard-help overlay panel**, and
   the forward-right quadrant is filled by a billboard with the advisor card drawn across it. The
   interior mirror does exist — I photographed it in the B24 cockpit pair — so the mechanism is
   possible; it simply was not demonstrated, and B27 gains no support from it.
4. **„Stands clear of the buildings" is overstated for B55.** On the approach the driver's own head is
   a **5 × 5 px** lit green lens against a **building facade**, not against sky as on `sx-v1`. It is
   present, it is the colour instruction 2 asserts, and you can find it — but only if you know where
   to look. The frame that genuinely closes „a traffic light for us" is the one **inside the box**,
   where the head is a 22 × 13 px unmistakable RED with the jaywalker in her red jacket on the zebra
   in the same picture.

5. **The zebra family was reported FIXED and it is not.** The seven pedestrian districts really are
   seven distinct files with seven distinct rooflines now, and the cause that lane found is excellent
   (see B50). But the **carriageway is identical on all seven** — same 16.25 m, same осева, same edge
   line, same kerb — so the bottom ~45 % of every frame is one picture, and four of the seven still
   read as one family of dark banded canyons. **B50 / B53 / B54 stay PARTIAL.**
6. **B46 and B49 were signed off on a frame of a different lesson.** Their evidence line reads „frame
   evidence is the zb-v1 pair above" — a different district. I checked them at data level instead and
   the closure survives, but for a reason nobody had stated: **`pe-clear-v1` and `pe-rain-v1` both
   declare `parkingBand: false`, so no parked car exists on either map.** Which also means whatever
   car he saw on those two lessons **was not a parked one**, and nothing in this wave looked at that.

### One verdict is stale, and it is the one that says we failed

**B70 cannot be reported BROKEN on a constant that no longer exists.** The traffic lane photographed
275 m of empty carriageway and named the file it was not allowed to touch: `templates-following.ts`,
`FS_LEAD_CAR.actor.hold.offsetM: 290` on a 360 m road with the spawn at y 15. That constant is now
**48** in the working tree — „(4.06, 48) — 33 m of centres ahead of the spawn" — `FS_QUEUE_AHEAD` has
been re-authored with a moving tail, and the three `sc-follow-standstill` traces have been re-recorded
to match. Another wave took the fix. **The lane's frames are honest and now out of date; B70 needs one
fresh drive before anyone calls it either way.**

### What held up

| row | claim | this gate |
|---|---|---|
| **B37** | flashing amber visible from the seat | **CONFIRMED** — pixel-sampled, both phases |
| **B39** | green then amber, from the seat | **CONFIRMED** — pixel-sampled, both states |
| **B55** | „a light for us" closed, ped head not | **CONFIRMED as PARTIAL** — the lane's own 14× zoom shows the ped lens as a 1–2 px sliver and it says so |
| **B81** | the 16.25 m grey plaza gets edge lines | **CONFIRMED** — before/after at an identical pose, both kerbs |
| **М18** give-way triangle | 0 → 108 symbols | **CONFIRMED, independently** — visible in the *roundabout* lane's top-down, apex pointing at the driver, М7 line behind it |
| **B33** | the encounter survives the instructed speed | **CONFIRMED, and this is the one that mattered** — trace max **21.9 / 22.0 km/h**, **zero** samples over 22, and a van crosses his windscreen while he is stopped at the mouth at 0 км/ч |
| **B73** | indicator on early, not late | **CONFIRMED** — amber lit at the cutter's right rear corner mid-glide, dark on the next frame |
| **B80** | the barge is now detected | **CONFIRMED** — «Непропускане на пътно превозно средство с предимство», ЗДвП чл. 47, at x −2.3 / y 111.9 |
| **B79** | no longer convicts a lawful gap | **CONFIRMED** — 0 наказателни точки · ИЗДЪРЖАН · 0/0/0 · ★★★ photographed at 29 km/h |
| **B21** | the unhurried beginner is not convicted | **CONFIRMED** — 0 точки · ИЗДЪРЖАН · ★★★ · 2/2 on a 51 s drive |
| **B24** | no brightness snap at the paint | **CONFIRMED** — two cockpit frames **9 cm** apart across the flip, ribbon identical |
| **B14** | the walker clears the parked row | **CONFIRMED** — 6× zoom, her shoulder stops 0.06 m short of the bodywork |
| **B41** | the officer is bigger | **CONFIRMED as PARTIAL** — he measures ~van height, and the same zoom shows he is still a capsule rig with a sphere head and scarecrow arms |
| **B16** | the roundabout is not round | **CONFIRMED as BROKEN** — the island is a circle, the outer boundary funnels open at every mouth |

**Half of B79 is measurement, not photograph.** „Still convicts at an unlawful gap" rests on a
headless ladder (30 / 40 / 44 km/h clean, 48 km/h fires at 1.05 s). The 48 km/h frame I opened shows a
**collision** card, not a following-distance card. The detector is demonstrably alive; the specific
fault card at the specific gap has not been photographed.

**B23 was not driven at the taught pace.** Its trace peaks at **26.6 km/h** with 61 samples over 22.
The row is about how many cars there are, so this does not overturn it — but it was not the obedient
drive B33 got.

**B46 and B49 were closed by arithmetic, and I checked the arithmetic instead of accepting it.** Both
districts — `pe-clear-v1` and `pe-rain-v1` — declare **`parkingBand: false`**, so **no parked car
exists on either map**. The parked-car defect could not occur there, which makes the closure sound. It
also means **whatever car he saw on those two lessons was not a parked one**, and nothing in this wave
looked at that. Only `zb-v1` (B14) actually has a parked row, and that is the one that got
photographed. The global guard `ped-through-parked.test.ts` covers all 100 districts with a legacy
witness so the before-number cannot rot — it passes.

### The gate itself

| check | result |
|---|---|
| `npx tsc --noEmit` | **EXIT 2 — 4 errors**, every one in an untracked `zz-` scratch test another wave left behind: `lesson/__tests__/zz-verify-scratch.test.ts` (×2), `sim/orchestrator/__tests__/zz-b29-replay.test.ts`, `sim/traffic/__tests__/zz-b29-sightline.test.ts`. **Zero in any file the six lanes touched.** A run six minutes earlier showed **9** errors in the same three files — the set churns while waves write and delete them, so any lane quoting a tsc error count is quoting a moment, not a state. |
| `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=4` | **730 files (722 passed / 7 failed / 1 skipped) · 11,008 tests (10,830 passed / 8 failed / 170 skipped) · 238.89 s** |
| expected reds | **2** — `exam/__tests__/content-bank.test.ts`, `lesson/__tests__/compose.test.ts` (the quarantined first-aid questions). |
| the six lanes' own new guards | **7 files / 135 tests, all pass** — `signal-head-forward-view`, `give-way-marking`, `witness-rehold`, `lane-change-beginner-window`, `routeGuidanceAnnouncement`, `ped-through-parked`, `pe-districts`. |
| untracked PNG / WebM / MP4 in the repo | **Zero from any of the six verify lanes** — every one of their frames lives in a scratchpad, and that is the one hygiene rule they all kept. **But not zero overall, and the timestamp matters:** this check was clean at 23:47 and dirty at 00:02, because a concurrent wave wrote `tools/clips/headless/sheets/sc-jx-giveway-b1__m0.strip.png` and `__m1.strip.png` at **23:55**, mid-gate. They are not ignored by `.gitignore` (which is itself modified in the working tree). Clean them or commit them deliberately. |
| `platform/tsconfig.json` | **NOT clean.** It carries `.next-harness/types/**`, `.next-harness/dev/types/**`, `.next-rig/types/**`, `.next-rig/dev/types/**`. |

|---|---|---|
| `lib/tsconfigHygiene.test.ts` ×2 — 4 scratch globs, `include` is 10 entries against a limit of 8 | `platform/tsconfig.json` | whoever ran the `.next-harness` / `.next-rig` dev servers — the untracked `app/dev/drive-rig/` + `app/dev/popup-rig/` wave. Each of the six lanes named **and deleted** its own dist dir (`.next-lights`, `.next-signs`, `.next-samemap`, `.next-ttlane`, `.next-b14lane`); one started no server at all. |

| `sim/scene/stop-line-grading.test.ts` — expected ≥ 11 declared stop-line cuts, got 10 | the count is re-derived from `SCENARIO_TEMPLATES` on every run | `templates-junctions.ts` (+135) / `templates-following.ts` (+217) — **the lane-change lane predicted this exact red and named this exact owner before it fired** |

### The parallel wave's files — twelve are open, and two are load-bearing

`git status` against the do-not-touch list. **`templates-flow.ts` and `world/TrafficLayer.tsx` are
clean — nobody went near them.** The rest are not:

`app/dev/fold-rig/fold-rig-client.tsx` · `app/dev/gw-shell/gw-shell-client.tsx` (+54) ·

There are no per-lane commits, so **git cannot tell you which hands wrote which hunk** — that is the
real finding here, and it is a process problem, not a code one. Two of these files are load-bearing
for rows in this register: `templates-following.ts` carries the B70 fix a lane said it could not make,
and `templates-junctions.ts` is why `stop-line-grading` is red. **Whoever merges must read those two
diffs before trusting either row.**

## The gate — LOOK-WAVE, 2026-08-03. **IT IS GREEN.**

Run after the look-wave, which edited exactly **two files** — the whole wave was a drive-and-photograph
pass, and only one defect (**B72**'s collapsed counter-column) was cheap and self-contained enough to
fix in place. Everything else was written up with a named owner and left alone, because six of the
open rows live in `worldRuntime.ts`, `runners.ts`, `templates-junctions.ts` and the HUD label layer,
which several lanes share — and this register already records FR-56 breaking four assertions in three
files from exactly that kind of mid-wave edit.

| check | result |
|---|---|
| `npx tsc --noEmit` | **exit 0**, no diagnostics, empty output. `platform/tsconfig.json` unmodified in git afterwards (no dev server was started, so nothing injected `.next-*` globs into `include`). |
| `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=4` | ✅ **exit 0 — 649 files / 9,942 passed / 165 skipped (10,107), 150.71 s.** Identical pass count to the previous close-out, which is correct: the wave changed assertions inside an existing test rather than adding one. |
| files edited | `platform/src/modules/sim/lessons/scenario/templates-following.ts` (**+11/−1**) and `platform/src/modules/sim/traces/__tests__/sc-follow-truck-traces.test.ts` (**+21/−7**). Nothing else in the working tree except this document. |

**The one code change, and why the test changed with it.** `FT_ONCOMING.gapsM` went
`[45,45,45,45,45]` → `[45,90,135,180,225]`. `gapsM[i-1]` is car *i*'s extra hold arc **vs car 0** —
`runners.ts` computes `hold.offsetM - gapsM[i-1]` and does **not** accumulate — so five repeated 45s
resolved to one arc and **five of the six cars in the "rolling counter-column" stood in the same
spot** (census at spawn: y 124.7, then 169.7 ×5). Every other stream in the codebase already authors
cumulatively (`templates-merging [26,52]`, `templates-parking2 [40,80]`), and this block's own
arithmetic already assumed it («hold y 169.7 … 349.7», «Σ gaps 225 ≤ head hold arc 235.3»).
**`sc-follow-truck-traces.test.ts` had encoded the same misreading** — it asserted every element ≤ 50
and Σ ≤ holdArc, which is precisely how `[45,45,45,45,45]` passed while parking five cars on one arc.
It now asserts consecutive **differences** are `> 0` (strictly increasing arcs ⇒ no two cars can share
a spot) and `≤ 50`, plus the per-car feasibility form `nm-district` and `ov-oncoming-district` already
use. Re-driven: census is now 124.7 / 169.7 / 214.7 / 259.7 / 304.7 / 349.7 and the six cars stream
past one at a time from simT ≈ 14.5 to ≈ 37 — a real ~22 s parade.

### THE SIDE-BY-SIDE — his two reference frames against ours, opened together

Rendered at the gate on `/dev/gw-shell?lesson=l2-intersections&quality=high` in a real phone context
(`isMobile`, `hasTouch`, 852×393), which is what makes the shell drop its desktop chrome:
`scratchpad/gate/SBS-CHASE.png` and `scratchpad/gate/SBS-COCKPIT.png`, each stacked directly under
`C:\Users\Ljh\Desktop\For fix\How it should look like.jpg` / `Cockpit should look like.jpg`.

**Last time the verdict was "same genre, not the same class of object — the HUD ~70% there, the world
~20%." Where those two numbers are now:**

- **HUD: ~70% → ~85%, on the phone.** All four glyphs his review named are fixed and *measured*, not
  asserted: the hamburger is `background: rgba(0,0,0,0)` with the word **«МЕНЮ»** in JetBrains Mono at
  48×44 (thumb minimum intact); «ЗАЩО» is a transparent chip and «РАЗБРАХ» a translucent teal at
  **0.18 alpha** — no solid brand blue anywhere in the band; the whole instrument set computes
  `font-family: "JetBrains Mono"`; and a full-page scan returns **0 elements crossing either viewport
  edge**. `scratchpad/gate/PHONE-cockpit-852x393.png` + its `.json`.
- **HUD, the 15% that is not there:** at a desktop window width the shell still renders its white
  application chrome bar with a **solid blue «Понякога»** button and a hairline-outlined button row —
  none of which exists in his reference, where the entire frame is game.
  `scratchpad/gate/sbs/CHASE-high.png`. The keyboard legend sheet also auto-expands and eats the
  top-left quadrant of the world there. Neither is on the phone path, and neither was in this wave's
  brief; both are real and unowned.
- **World: ~20% → ~28%. The art pass did NOT close it, and this is the honest sentence: his frame is
  still a different class of object.** What genuinely changed and survives inspection: the sky is blue
  instead of sepia; the sun moved 22° → 56° and a directional shadow now exists with an *edge*; rain
  renders on the phone tier; buildings carry a cornice; the objective marker is off the eyeline.
  What did not change at all, and is what the two frames are actually about: **every surface is still
  untextured or flat-tinted** (his asphalt has wear, patches and rubber down the racing line — ours is
  one grey), **the buildings are still extruded boxes with painted-on window rectangles**, there are
  **no wires, no poles, no fences, no barriers, no signage furniture, no tyre marks**, the trees are
  one repeated low-poly model, and the player's car is an untextured shape with no wheels, lamps or
  plate where his is a fully modelled vehicle. A student cannot tell the difference between 8% and 28%
  of the way to a commercial game; he can tell the difference between "a training tool" and "a game",
  and that verdict is unchanged.

### The shadow claim, measured — because it is the art lane's headline and it is only half true

A/B on the live page (`gl.shadowMap.enabled` toggled, materials recompiled, same settled frame),
`scratchpad/gate/ab/` and `scratchpad/gate/DIFF-shadow-now.png`:

| patch | shadows ON | shadows OFF | Δ |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| road, **left** lane | 127.21 | 144.98 | **−17.8** |
| road, **right** lane | 164.85 | 164.85 | **0.00** |
| pavement, **left** | 149.60 | 179.41 | **−29.8** |
| pavement, **right** | 182.81 | 182.81 | **0.00** |
| grass beside a street tree | 171.00 | 171.01 | **−0.01** |

**What that proves, both ways.** The lane's diagnosis was right and its fix is real: the right half of
the street is now **byte-identical with the shadow map off**, so it is genuinely sunlit, and there is a
**37.6/255 step across the carriageway** between the shaded and lit halves. The old symptom — "every
street sat wholly inside the shade of its own west building line", which reads exactly like no
directional light at all — is measurably gone. **And the founder's sentence still describes the frame
he would see:** in the near field there is no shadow *edge* (`scratchpad/gate/SHEET-shadow-edge.png`,
2× — ON and OFF are almost indistinguishable), and the street trees cast **nothing** into the driving
view at eye level (0.01/255). The only shadow that reads is the big building-line one, and it reads as
"the left side is a bit darker", not as a shadow. **Verdict: PARTIAL. Not the closed row the lane
claimed.**

### THE PHONE COST OF THE ART PASS — before vs after, per tier, measured not estimated

Method: the art lane's own WebGL instrumentation (every `drawArrays`/`drawElements(Instanced)` wrapped
and bucketed per rAF frame, so it counts **every** pass — shadow map, mirror RTT, composer). Same
server, same lesson, same 2.6 s drive, chase view, median of ~150 frames. "Before" = the six art files
reverted to `HEAD` and restored afterwards with md5 verification, so it is a true A/B and not a
different build.

| tier | draw calls before → after | triangles before → after | frame ms before → after |
|---|---|---|---|
| **low** (the phone) | 174 → **175** (+1) | 851,608 → **854,710** (+3,102, **+0.36%**) | 16.6 → 16.6 |
| **medium** | 475 → **477** (+2) | 1,621,686 → **1,627,890** (+6,204, +0.38%) | 16.5 → 16.7 |
| **high** | 495 → **497** (+2) | 2,506,366 → **2,512,570** (+6,204, +0.25%) | 16.6 → 16.8 |
| **low, wet lesson** | 240 → **243** (+3) | 336,841 → **338,153** (+1,312, +0.39%) | 16.7 → 16.6 |

- **`low` did not get slower.** +1 draw call and +0.36% triangles on a dry lesson; +3 draws and
  +0.39% on the one wet lesson that now actually renders rain. Nothing here is a regression.
- **The frame-time column is not evidence and must not be quoted as if it were.** Every cell is
  16.5–16.8 ms because headless Chromium is **vsync-capped at 60 fps** on this box and the GPU has
  headroom at all three tiers. The art pass is not measurably faster or slower; the only defensible
  cost signal is the submission counts. Anyone quoting "18 fps before, 57 fps after" from the lane's
  own sidecars is quoting a cold-shader-compile artefact (27 samples vs 116), not a change.
- **A number the lane quoted and got wrong, in the safe direction:** the brief cites a phone budget of
  "≤70 calls / ≤250k triangles". That is the **world-geometry estimator's** budget
  (`buildWorldGeometry.ts`, 150 draws / 300k tris), not a whole-frame count. Whole-frame at `low` is
  **175 draws / 854,710 triangles** — 2.5× and 3.4× those figures — dominated by the 64,298-triangle
  hero car GLB and the multi-pass render, and it was already so before this wave. The art pass did not
  cause it and does not move it, but "+1 draw against ≤70" is the wrong frame of reference and should
  not be repeated.

   register is „Спане на зелено — no traffic light exists on the map".** The lane was numbering against
   a different list. The login fix is credited below as **B-NEW-2**; row B39 did not move.

   that "someone should shoot it". It was shot at the gate — `scratchpad/gate/SLEEPER-phone.png` and
   `ZOOM-sleeper.png` — and the photograph is **not decisive**: «Спане на зелено» loads, instruction 3
   now names the car in instructor Bulgarian („Погледни колата отсреща, спряла на другата стоп-линия:
   на нея ѝ свети СЪЩОТО зелено, а тя не тръгва"), and vehicles are standing beyond the junction — but
   a still cannot distinguish the staged sleeper from ambient traffic, and the drill's whole subject
   is a car that *stays* still, which needs a sequence. **FR-34 stays partial.**
3. **"No cast shadows anywhere — fixed."** See the measurement above. Half true.

Run after the seven parallel lanes landed. Reported before the older table below, because a red gate
outranks anything else on this page.

| check | result |
|---|---|
| `npx tsc --noEmit` | **exit 0**, no diagnostics. `tsconfig.json` byte-checked afterwards: the two `.next-compare/**` globs `next dev` injected were removed again, and `src/lib/tsconfigHygiene.test.ts` passes. |
| `npx vitest run --maxWorkers=4` | ❌ **exit 1 — 3 files / 4 tests failed**, 643 files / 9,872 passed / 165 skipped (10,041), 137.7 s. Not memory pressure: no `Failed to start forks worker` line, and the four failures are deterministic assertion errors that reproduce on a 2-worker re-run. |
| world-referent gate | **passes — 17/17** across `world-referent.gate.test.ts` (7) and the new `referent-evidence-reachable.test.ts` (10). Mode ENFORCING, allowlist 0. |
| falsehood budget | **2 codes / 7 scenarios / 33 rung-codes — UNCHANGED in value, repaired in meaning.** See the RESOLVED box above: the same 33 was previously certified by a field no student ever read, and is now certified by `lesson.briefingBg`, photographed on screen. |

### The four failures — one root cause, bisected and proven

All four live in `src/modules/sim/traces/__tests__/` and all four are the **FR-56 rolling start**:
`orchestrator/runners.ts:2609` now passes `seedSpeedMps: input.speedKmh / 3.6` so the лепка enters the
mirror already travelling instead of launching from a standstill. That is a good fix and it is one of
his own asks — but `RearTailgaterRunner` is **not only the лепка**. Three templates borrow it as a
generic "vehicle that comes past you", and their choreography was timed against the old
launch-from-rest profile:

| file | assertion | measured |
|---|---|---|
| `sc-ln-decisive-change-traces.ts:141` | the contact is choreographed — the car is still alongside when the wheel goes over | crossing **17.70 s** vs target pass **17.13 s** — the car has already gone by; the blind-spot contact no longer happens |
| `sc-vu-blindspot-moto-traces.ts:141` | same, for the motorcyclist | **18.50 s** vs **18.28 s** |
| `sc-ov-being-overtaken-traces.ts:99` | the overtaker's pass reads `yielded` | reads **`clear`** |
| `sc-ov-being-overtaken-traces.ts:150` | mistake-accelerating: the overtaker is TRAPPED, its pass never resolves | it **resolves at 27.88 s** — the taught trap does not occur |

**Bisected, not guessed:** commenting out that single line turns all three files green
(**45/45 passed, 1.93 s**); restoring it reproduces exactly those four failures. The line was restored
— this pass did not silently revert another lane's founder-asked fix to buy a green board.

**The fix is a scope, not a rollback.** `seedSpeedMps` should be opted into by the templates that
author a лепка (`templates-following*.ts`), not forced on every consumer of `RearTailgaterRunner`, so
`templates-lanes2.ts` (sc-ov-being-overtaken), `templates-lanes3.ts` (sc-ln-decisive-change) and
`templates-vru2.ts` (sc-vu-blindspot-moto) keep their timing. Owner: the traffic lane — it has the
onset measurements, and choosing the predicate blind would re-break FR-56 without anyone noticing.

**This is the wave's own lesson landing on the wave.** FR-56 shipped with a new, deliberately
non-vacuous test (`tailgater-onset.test.ts`, 4 tests, proven to fail without the fix) and that test is
green. The four assertions it broke live in other files that nobody re-ran until this gate. A fix
verified only by the test written alongside it is exactly the failure mode this register exists for.

Drove the pass around it through the production stack (`recordScriptedDrive` → `createWorldRuntime` + `createTrafficSystem` + the scenario director + the rule engine), the SAME drive graded twice: once by `git show HEAD` of `orchestrator/runners.ts`, once by the exact-geometry version. **Holding a line 1.29 m clear of its flank, entirely inside his own lane: BEFORE = `COLLISION` and nothing else — an otherwise perfect drive scored 10 опасни points; AFTER = zero violations.** The culprit is the tenth, uncounted circle — `BrakingLeadCarRunner`'s `gap = hypot(centres) − LEAD_CAR_LENGTH_M(4.3) ≤ 0.3`, which carries no `*_CONTACT_M` constant and so survived the sweep that retired the other three. `hypot` is isotropic; two flanks touch at 0.85 + 0.92 = **1.77 m** of centres, so abeam it convicted **up to 2.83 m of clear air** — wider than the car doing the passing. Driven boundary, 0.2 m steps on the real runner: **fires at 2.82 m of daylight, clears at 2.84 m**; the exact geometry flips at true contact (2.29 m line → separation **+0.002 m**, silent; 2.5 m line → **−0.208 m**, `COLLISION`). The `VEHICLE_CONTACT_M = 3.0` sibling measured the same way on `sc-ln-obstacle-meeting`'s oncoming stream: fires at 1.20 m of air, clears at 1.40 (predicted 1.23). **Real crashes are untouched:** nose into the standing car at **39.9 km/h** → `COLLISION`, separation **−0.007 m**, «Пътнотранспортно произшествие», 10 т., опасна; a moving head-on at 16.1 km/h → `COLLISION` at **−0.014 m**. Whole shipped corpus re-graded — **311 committed drives, 6 verdicts move**: three phantom crashes deleted (0.45 / 1.20 / 1.34 m of measured air) and **three real contacts that the circles never saw** now billed, including a child struck at **−0.113 m** on `sc-pe-parked-row-scan/mistake-fast-row` and a rear-end at **−0.032 m** on `sc-fo-brakelight-chain/mistake-late-brake`. Result screen read and photographed: `scratchpad/frames/end-mobile.png`, `end-desktop.png` — «10 наказателни точки … не са контролни точки по книжката», «Настъпи сблъсък. Това е ЕДНА опасна грешка…» with Наредба № 38 прил. № 5, т. 10, б. „в" for the ten and **чл. 48, ал. 3 quoted verbatim** for the ending. Cockpit frames of the lesson and the pass: `scratchpad/frames/after2-step10-t38.6-v14.png` (mid-pass, x = 0.73 → **1.56 m of daylight**, telemetry burned in), `fastA-step07-t30.7-v25.png`. Pinned by `orchestrator/__tests__/parked-car-pass.test.ts` (4 assertions, both directions). **Residual, stated not hidden:** no live cockpit frame exists of the false-positive INSTANT, because the shipped lead resumes 3 s after its slam (`resumeAfterSec: 3`) and `/dev/drive-rig`'s steering is open-loop — every live attempt either arrived after the car had driven off or rear-ended it. The verdict evidence is headless and exact; the picture is of the act, not of the conviction. **Owner of what is left:** `orchestrator/runners.ts` + `sim/collision` (geometry, done) and the rig (`devrig/driveScript.ts`, the missing frame). |

Пропусни движението!"» … «Спрял си правилно… знакът иска и да ПРОПУСНЕШ движещите се по пътя с предимство», with ЗДвП чл. 6, т. 1 and чл. 50, ал. 1 printed under it. **His false conviction did not reproduce.** **(b) The rolling entry does convict.** `scratchpad/lsn6/B5L2/B5L2-step02-t39.8-v11.png` — same lesson, entered at 15.5 км/ч; two опасни cards fire at the same instant t = 39.456: «Неспиране на знак Б2» and «Непропускане на пътно превозно средство с предимство» (ЗДвП чл. 47, −10 т.). **I cannot certify that junction was empty and will not pretend otherwise: the two fault cards cover the right third of the windscreen — precisely the quadrant the predicate reads.** **(c) The predicate, line by line — and one claim previously recorded in this row is WRONG.** `worldRuntime.ts:1004` calls `conflictQuery(node.x, node.y, PRIORITY_CONFLICT_RADIUS_M = 26, line.approachBearingDeg)` → `conflictNearFor` (`traffic/system.ts:718–738`), which tests exactly three things: within 26 m **of the junction node**, `speedMps ≥ 1` (`CONFLICT_MIN_SPEED_MPS`, :110), and travel bearing ≥ 50° off the approach bearing (`CONFLICT_SAME_DIR_DEG`, :112). It does **not** ask which side the vehicle is on, whether it is on the priority road, whether it is approaching or departing, or whether it has already cleared the conflict point — and at Δ = 180° **an ONCOMING car on the player's own road satisfies it**. This row previously said the predicate „now requires the vehicle to be moving, on the player's RIGHT and on a different bearing": that narrowing lives in `conflictFromRightFor`, which the give-way line never calls. **So his symptom does not reproduce on a correct drill, and the mechanism that would produce it is confirmed present at a line number.** The row stays PARTIAL for that reason — not for want of a drive.<br><br>Урок 2 never rendered — it is reachable only by clicking its card and it was last in the batch; three dev servers died first (two Turbopack panics on `globals.css`, one 10-minute `/login` compile). A key script drove 328 m north past the first junction and then left the carriageway onto the verge; right-steer taps kerbed and stalled it. The junction his complaint is about is past a turn a fixed key script cannot follow. In ~30 s of approach driving no yield fault of any kind fired. **Mechanism to hand on, not a verdict:** `worldRuntime.ts:919` calls `conflictQuery(node.x, node.y, 26 m, approachBearing)` on every give-way crossing and `traffic/system.ts:661-679` returns true for ANY vehicle within 26 m moving above the threshold on a different bearing — it does not test which arm, whether the vehicle is on the priority road, whether it is ahead or behind, or whether it has already cleared. **Closing this row needs a hand-driven pass or a recorded trace of the l2 route.**<br><br>**→ 2026-08-03 LOOK-WAVE → PARTIAL-SEEN. The two fixed constants are real and they are not enough, because the conviction comes from a THIRD site this register has never named.** Two independent drives. Run 1, cockpit: braked to 0 км/ч on the give-way approach and stood there — frames at +2/+4/+6/+8/+14/+16/+24/+34/+36/+46 s, three held left glances, and **the HUD under every one of those ten frames carries no fault card at all** (`scratchpad/lanerb/frames/B15R-12-l-wait46s.png`, 0 км/ч, gear D, a dark-blue car low on the LEFT). Blip the throttle: clean at +1.3 s / 5 км/ч, and at **+2.4 s the whole screen is the blocking teach card «Непропускане на пътно превозно средство с предимство» (ЗДвП чл. 47) with the red −10 т. row behind it** — `scratchpad/lanerb/frames/B15R-14-n-at-entry.png`. Run 2, top camera, same waits: nine clean frames across 46 s, then the fault card **0.7 s after the first blip, before the car has left the arm** (`B15T-10-j-top-moving-off.png`). Nine of nine wait frames silent, then conviction on the first movement, twice. **The third site, read in source at the gate:** `runtime/worldRuntime.ts:1359-1365` sets `rbCondSince = tSec` the first tick a circulating conflict is seen and clears it **only** on a tick where the conflict is absent — a stationary driver never clears it. The test at `:1376-1388` then wants `tSec - rbCondSince >= YIELD_CONVICT_SUSTAIN_SEC (0.9)` and `!(brakingResponse && tSec - rbCondSince <= YIELD_BRAKE_RESPONSE_MAX_SEC (3.0))`; after a 46 s wait both are 45 s stale, so the only live gate left is `v.speedKmh > RHR_MOVING_KMH (3)` — **the instant the wheels turn he is convicted, with zero reaction window and no braking immunity, which are precisely the two mercies the C1/D1 comments above that block say they exist to grant.** Narrow fix: clear `rbCondSince` whenever the driver is stationary (`if (v.speedKmh <= WITNESS_STOPPED_KMH) rbCondSince = null;`) so the sustain clock measures time spent MOVING into the conflict — what the rest of the block already means by it. Cheaper second half worth pairing: the test carries no time-to-conflict term at all, so a car 25 m away that would be long gone convicts a driver doing 4 km/h 18 m short of the ring. Owner: `runtime/worldRuntime.ts` (shared — one lane only).<br><br>**→ 2026-08-05 INSTRUMENT GATE → FIXED-SEEN. Fires with a car, silent without one. **What is still true and is now a STILL-OPEN line, not a blocker on this row:** `ROUNDABOUT_ENTRY_MARGIN_M = 12` arms the tracker only within 30 m of the ring centre while the М8 paint is at 35.96 m, so for the whole minute the student does the most important thing a learner does at a junction, **the virtual instructor says nothing — and the first word it ever says about that car is −10.** Against requirement zero that is the real residual, and it is a grading change with a five-trace blast radius, not a photograph. |
| **B16** | Кръгово движение (6) | "this is not proper round-about it doesnt have the proper shape" | **BROKEN-SEEN** *(the cell said PARTIAL for two gates while the body said BROKEN — reconciled 2026-08-05)* | **→ 2026-08-05 COCKPIT GATE → BROKEN-SEEN, nothing landed, and THIS REGISTER'S OWN FIX NUMBER IS REFUTED.** **The frame, from the seat.** `scratchpad/b16round/shots/b16-before-canvas-y-45.png` — both A-pillars, dash, wheel, cluster at **0 км/ч D**, interior mirror, the Б1 triangle on the right kerb; stopped on the `sc-roundabout-entry` south approach at `x=4.06, y=−45.79`, **23.7 m from the ring's outer kerb.** What he sees is a flat open asphalt plaza whose kerbs run left-to-right across the frame like an ordinary crossroads, with a small green mound and bushes in the middle. Beside it, an orthographic top-down rasterised from the shipped `buildWorldGeometry` triangles shows why: **the outer boundary is an octagon of junction pads with four short kerb slivers between them.** Composite at three radii: `b16round/shots/B16-ladder.png`. **The measurement, re-derived by this gate over all 100 districts rather than the six the lane chose.** Exactly six register a ring: `rb-mini-v1` **152.9°**, `rb-ped-v1` **152.9°**, `rb-2lane-v1` **109.1°**, `rb-single-v1` **254.1°**, `district-v1 rb-1` (real Sofia OSM, R=19.01) **252.8°**, `d2-v1 rb-1` **127.1°** by sum / 187.3° by union with the island already refused because `e587740857.0` (бул. „Пейо К. Яворов") is drawn through its interior. **THE ≈31 m IN THIS REGISTER IS WRONG AND HAS BEEN FOR TWO GATES:** at R = 31 `rb-mini` gives **232.4°**, 37.6° short of the 270° bar the number was written to satisfy (`b16round/shots/td-mini-r31.png`). The break-even is **R = 45.3 m** for the three single-lane families (ship 46 → 271.3°) and **R = 83.0 m** for `rb-2lane` (ship 84 → 271.1°) — **2.6× and 3.2×, not 1.7×.** **The cause is SCALE, and the builder is innocent — the table proves it without trusting anyone:** `district-v1`'s real-Sofia ring gets 252.8° from the *same code* at a nearly identical R = 19.01, because its arms register at **4.06 m** half-width where `rb-mini`'s register at **8.13 m**. The generators author the arms at `PERCEPTUAL_ROAD_SCALE` 2.5 and the ring radius at real-world scale; 18 × 2.5 = 45 lands within half a metre of the measured break-even. **Why nothing was landed, and why that was right.** The change provably needs three `lessons/scenario/templates-*.ts` files that pin the ring **by value** (`templates-flow.ts` hard-codes `acceptBeforeMarkM 1.725` against the district's М8 bars at `y=−35.725`; `templates-roundabout.ts` declares `RING_R = 18`, `RB2_R = 26` and stages the circulating cars on those loops), all of `content/world`, **36 trace files** (6 scenarios × 3 × content + published), and it would red **two untracked runtime probes another lane is writing right now** which hard-code `MOUTH = { x: 0, y: −18 }`. This gate verified the whole blast radius is byte-identical to HEAD. **AND A RULING IS NEEDED BEFORE ANYONE SPENDS THE RE-RECORD.** I opened both candidate frames at an equal 23.7 m to the kerb: at **R = 46** the top-down is a perfect annulus but the windscreen reads **worse** than at 31 — a 41.5 m island fills the horizon as parkland and the ring's curvature falls outside the glass, so it reads as a T-junction (`b16-after46-canvas-y-73.8.png`). **R = 31 is the frame that most reads as a roundabout from the seat, at only 232.4° from above** (`b16-r31-canvas-y-58.8.png`). **270° is a top-down metric.** If his sentence is about the shape of the map, 46 / 84 is right; if it is about what he sees on approach, the target is nearer 31–39 and the 270° threshold should be retired. That is his call, not ours. **Also recorded:** `rb-single-v1` (254.1°, needs R = 46) is the cheapest fix in the family — no scenario template references it and it has **zero traces** — and the two OSM districts cannot be fixed this way at all, because their shape is a data-capture question, not a radius question.<br><br>Driven into the ring and looked at from the driver's seat. **No central island, no circular kerb, no ring lane markings, no annular carriageway** — an open plaza of grey asphalt with irregular pale slabs and two green wedges; the only thing behaving like a roundabout is the shadow ribbon curving through it. `scratchpad/relookB/RB1-drv-t10s-27kmh.png` (zoom `ZOOM-B16-ring.png`). **The data is fine** — `rb-mini-v1.json` has a clean r=18 ring as four arcs — so nothing *draws* it. `world/builders/network.ts:145` is the only roundabout-specific geometry in the builders (it widens a ring edge and zeroes its parking band); the four arm nodes emit four ordinary junction pads at ≈17 m open radius, which union into the blob. **The island derivation is already written and unit-tested in-tree** at `app/dev/scene-still/roundaboutIsland.ts`, which says so itself: *"the SIM still renders a ring without an island — that gap belongs to sim/world's builders."* **Owner:** `modules/sim/world/builders/` — port it, and suppress the junction pads inside the ring.<br><br>**RE-LOOK 2026-08-02 → PARTIAL-SEEN.** The island exists and is rendered. `modules/sim/world/builders/roundabout.ts` (NEW) resolves each ring and emits a kerb face + 45° chamfer + concrete rim into the SIDEWALK accumulator (which is also the collider), a mounded planted crown into its own mesh, and the circular dashed ring divider into PAINT; `roads.ts` now pushes every junction-pad boundary point inside a registered island out onto the kerb line, so the pads are SUPPRESSED rather than covered. The limit lives on the edge tag, so the rule went where the tag is read: `world/builders/props.ts`. **The law was checked before a number was picked, and the honest blank is in the code.** Наредба № РД-02-21-1/2023 is catalogued in `content/law/sources.json` as `coverage: "index-only"`, `extraction: null` — **the repo does not hold its text**, so no article is quotable under ADR-002 and no statutory repetition distance is available. What the corpus *does* hold is the зона-на-действие rule, three times over in `approved` items (`q-signs-011 / 012 / 021`): a group-В prohibition ends at the next junction unless repeated after it. **That is what makes the junction repeat law-shaped rather than invented — past an unrepeated junction the simulator was grading a number the ordinance had already let lapse.** The separate 120 m mid-block cadence is labelled **AUTHORED, NOT STATUTORY** in `LIMIT_REPEAT_M`'s own docstring, with the one line to change if the ordinance is ever ingested. Its ceiling went 30 → **40**, not 50, on measurement rather than taste: 50 adds nothing to either B34 district and studs `sp-creep2-v1`'s junction-less 400 m approach with 5 plates another lane had deliberately pinned at 2. **Scenario maps only, structurally:** the pass sits inside `if (lessonScale !== undefined)` and `scenarioSignScale` returns `undefined` unless `meta.mapKind` starts with `"scenario"`, so no city / exam / полигон district can move. **Two things found while closing it, filed rather than buried:** a streetwall canopy hides the new post until ~15 m (fine at 14 км/ч, about a second of reading at the posted 50), and the cadence gate stopping below 50 means a long junction-less 50 straight still gets no mid-block repeat. It is LIT.** Mechanism, read not guessed: `SC_SIG_CONTROLLER_POSTURES` (`templates-signals2.ts`) never authors `signalModes`, so `setClusterController` leaves the cluster `"controlled"` and `runtime/signals.ts:338–348` **deliberately keeps live lamps under `"controlled"`**. The obvious one-liner is a trap: `controllerPermission` (`signals.ts:449`) returns null unless the mode is `"controlled"`, so authoring `signalModes: { "sx-n-c": "dark" }` would silently disarm the officer's grading. The fixing lane disclosed this as "a thin band of pale ground"; measured, it is the whole central vista. **(b) TWO OF MY OWN INSTRUMENTS FAILED HERE AND THE VERDICT DOES NOT REST ON THEM.** A colour classifier scored the BEFORE frame 72.3 % "closed" — nonsense, the olive plain is a desaturated yellow-green that reads as canopy. A straight pixel-diff of the vista band then reported 430 of 481 columns changed including the axis, but the demonstration's blue shadow-car sits at the centre of one frame and not the other and the stations differ by 8 cm, so it cannot separate trees from parallax. Both are recorded as failures; the verdict is read off the 7.3× crops of the identical region. **(c) THE LANE'S OWN ZOOM PAIR IS NOT COMPARABLE and should not be cited:** its report claims "the same crop region [380,240..900,440] at 2.4×", but `z-before-term.png` is **1248×432** and `z-after2-term.png` is **1248×480** — 180 source rows against 200. Own instrument (`draws.mjs`: raw WebGL counter installed via `addInitScript`, sampled as a per-rAF DELTA, alongside the product's own PerfProbe), `/dev/drive-rig`, 1264×619 @ dpr 1.0, 8 s per run: **`pe-cane-v1` (`sc-crossing-white-cane`) tier low = 217 raw p50 / 193 probe draws and 260 898 tris → 3.1× the 70 soft cap and 2.2× the 100 HARD cap; med = 347/323 → past the 250 hard cap; high = 319–368/336 → over the 300 soft. `d2-v1` (`sc-ed-d2-city-run`) tier low = 328 raw / 275 probe and 2 084 341 tris → 4.7× the soft cap on draws and 7× the HARD cap on triangles; med 553/500 and 3.41 M tris; high 471–577/524 and 5.69 M tris — d2-v1 FAILS every tier on both axes.** `sp-creep-v1` at low measures **221** on the PerfProbe and **342 346** triangles — the fixing lane's two headline figures, reproduced exactly by an independent run, which is the strongest thing I can say for their A/B. **(e) The STATIC census is unchanged and still passes, which is precisely the problem.** My own build of all 105 shipped districts (`census-v2.json`): worst `drawCallEstimate` **67** (`hz-roadworks-v1`, `ov-crest-v1`, `pe-zone-v1`, `pk-rail-v1`), **`d2-v1` 66, `pe-cane-v1` 65, `sp-creep-v1` 59** — every one PASS against the low soft cap of 70. So the number the row has been managed on is **20–27 % of the real frame**, and "one more instanced kind breaches the 70 cap" was never the constraint. **This needs its own row with an owner: either `perfBudget.ts` is wrong about what this product renders, or the product is 3–5× over it. The FPS complaint cannot be closed while that is unknown.** **(f) «Raw» vs «boring», from my own frame.** Raw is substantially answered — frontage both sides, parked row, railings, a crossarmed utility pole with its overhead line, lamp columns, and now the terminus grove. **Boring is not:** nothing is moving on that street but the demonstration's own ghost car, and there is no pedestrian, crossing or side turning. The fixing lane's reason is independently confirmed in source and is correct — `templates-sp.ts:32` "Ambient traffic is ZERO in every drive (seed 7)", `:846` "The street is EMPTY (ambient 0 …) so every cause in the detector's ledger is positively absent", and instruction 1 tells the student «Улицата е празна — никаква опасност». Пътнотранспортно произшествие» (`B80Z-t031.png`). **The end-of-lesson ledger is the proof:** Опасни грешки **2** / Основни **0** / Второстепенни **0**, and the fault list names **BOTH** dangerous errors as «Пътнотранспортно произшествие» — **no yield, no priority, no narrow-meeting entry anywhere** (`B80Z-96-debrief.png`). A separate run (`B80bg2`) drove the oncoming side for **20 s without ever touching anything** and still raised only a lane-keeping fault (`B80bg2-t019.png`). **In no frame of any run did a southbound oncoming car ever appear moving.** His sentence is a MISSING error and it is still missing: **the student is told he crashed, never that he took priority that was not his.** **Owner `orchestrator/runners.ts` — a shared file, and this register records FR-56 breaking four assertions in three files from exactly this kind of mid-wave edit, so the look-wave did not touch it.** Two candidate mechanisms, both in `NarrowMeetingRunner`, **and the evidence points at the second.** (a) `NarrowMeetingRunner.step()` opens with a contact guard (`dist(player, actor) < VEHICLE_CONTACT_M` → `out.push({kind:"collision"}); return this.resolve(input, false, "collision")`, ~1537-1544). `resolve()` sets `phase="resolved"` and every later `step()` returns null, **so the instant a barge ends in contact the runner retires having emitted ONLY a collision — it can never reach the `prioritySituation{narrow-meeting, violated:true}` push at ~1627.** Fix: raise the priority event **alongside** the collision when the barge condition was live (`convictSince !== null`, or `sawConflict && playerLat > NM_LANE_OVER_M`) rather than instead of it. (b) **The stronger evidence — `B80bg2` barged for 20 s with NO contact and still convicted nothing** — says the runner never reaches `triggered` with `conflictLive` true, i.e. **the staged oncoming actor never transits.** That is the `armed` branch's sync (~1573-1590: `carDistToEntry <= 4 || playerAlong > -8` → cruise) plus `actorCleared` / `conflictLive` at ~1594-1596. **Whoever takes this should FIRST instrument whether `traffic.staged(s.id)` ever reports `speedMps > 0` on a live session** — `orchestrator/__tests__/narrow-meeting.test.ts` passes (8/8, re-run at this wave) and `rules/engine.ts:1831-1866` grades `prioritySituation` → `FAILED_TO_YIELD` correctly, **so both ends are healthy and the break is in the middle.** Re-drive frame after the fix: `sc-ov-narrow` L1, top-down, car left of centre line at y 110–145 — a «Учебен момент — Непропускане на пътно превозно средство с предимство» card must appear, and the debrief must show ≥1 опасна of that name.<br><br>**→ 2026-08-04 VERIFY-BY-DRIVING GATE → FIXED-SEEN — the under-detection is closed and he is now told what he did.** Drove the barge on the real shell: own lane to y 94, swing left, hold x ≈ −2.3 through the narrowing. At t = 20.3 s, y 111.9, x −2.3 — left of the centre line, inside the y ∈ [110,145] constriction, exactly the geometry this register specified — the card appears: «Учебен момент — **Непропускане на пътно превозно средство с предимство**», «Не пропусна превозно средство, което имаше предимство…», **ЗДвП чл. 47**, «Първа среща — не се брои в резултата. | **B-NEW-2** | *not his row* | — | **FIXED-SEEN** | **The drive screen was being silently replaced by `/login`, and the workaround was the evidence.** Three server actions reachable from `LessonPlayShell` called `requireUser()`, which calls `redirect()`, which Next turns into a **303 the router follows**: `finishLessonAction` (`actions.ts:54`), `recordSelfPredictionAction` (`calibration-actions.ts:73`) and — the one nobody had named — `submitMicroQuizAnswer` (`micro-quiz-actions.ts:139`), which fires **mid-drive from an overlay while the car is moving**. Each now answers the way its own contract already answers every other refusal (a new `NOT_SIGNED_IN` code; the existing deliberately-indistinguishable `UNAVAILABLE`; a throw the overlay already catches). **Why this hid for three waves: no test could see it, and the render rig had been aborting every request carrying the `Next-Action` header** (`gw-probe.mjs`, `blockServerActions`, default **true**) — the workaround *was* the proof the bug was real. Proven by driving `sc-signal-flashing` with that blocking **off**: 45 s, zero bounce, action responses **200 with no Location header**; then the path that actually bounced was forced (end the lesson so `finishLessonAction` really fires) and **the full result screen survived** — «Мигащо жълто · Ниво 1 — резултат», 0 наказателни точки, НЕИЗДЪРЖАН, the three fault-class rows, the star rubric. That entire screen used to be `/login`. `scratchpad/b39/sc-signal-flashing-finish/30-result-screen.png` + `verdict.json` (`bounced=false`). **Student-facing half:** a session that expired mid-lesson lost the debrief, the law citations and the whole teaching payload; the shell now renders instructor Bulgarian instead of a bare code (THEO-4: which half failed, and what to do). **Permanent guard:** `simulator/drive-actions-never-redirect.test.ts` (9 tests) DISCOVERS the action modules from `LessonPlayShell`'s own import statements rather than hardcoding them — which is exactly how `submitMicroQuizAnswer` survived the first fix — bans `requireUser(`/`redirect(` in them, asserts `requireUser()` is **still** on the page so the real security boundary is not over-corrected, and carries a teeth test. **Numbering note:** the lane that built this called it "B39". Row **B39** in this register is „Спане на зелено — no traffic light exists on the map" and did **not** move. |

The reason has moved from the probe to the product.** **1. THE INSTRUMENT IS OUT OF THE NUMBER — verified independently, not read.** The polling loop now runs inside the page (`tools/mobile/lib/settle.mjs`, `settleBody`) and timestamps itself with `performance.now()`; the driver bridge is crossed once, outside the window. I tested it against a page whose layout stops moving at a known moment while the renderer's main thread is deliberately jammed BEFORE the measurement starts: across a 0 -> 3,000 ms jam the APP number read **480 / 372 / 160 / 164 ms (spread 320)** while wall clock read **592 / 485 / 1,707 / 3,204 ms (spread 2,719)** and `bridgeMs` correctly absorbed 1,437 and 2,938 ms. Live re-run of the real probe on a real route with six CPU hogs on the box: instrument share rose 2% -> 10%, the app number did not move (151-163 -> 154-167 ms). **The old metric moves with the box; this one does not.** **2. INSTRUMENT-BOUND FIRES ON THE RIGHT CONDITION, AND THERE IS ONE PLACE IT CANNOT FIRE.** Threshold is a literal 25% and both trigger expressions are present. My jam samples reached **84% and 92%** instrument share; **4 of the 25 recorded C8 samples are over the line (31.9%, 26.5%, 50.3%, worst 68.5%)**. But the table line carrying the marker is at source line 1459 and the `if (!r.ok) … continue` bail-out is at 1414, and the detail block is guarded by `!r.settleRuns?.length` — so **on the C8 row itself, which was refused, the marker was unreachable and never printed.** The refusal is the louder signal, but „it prints when it should" is true only for rows that survive. **3. THE LOSS IS FIXED AND IS NOW IMPOSSIBLE TO MISS.** `iterationErrors` is recorded unconditionally; the `LOST n/N RUNS` line is at source line 1411, BEFORE the bail-out that used to skip it; and an unconditional `SAMPLE INTEGRITY` block at line 1752 re-states asked-vs-produced and fails the sweep. The recorded artefact proves the behaviour (`iterations 5, iterationsOk 0`, all five reasons present). **Caveat: the test pinning this is a regex over the probe's own source, not a behavioural test.** **4. THE WORLD IS NOT RENDERED, AND I OPENED THE FRAMES.** `ENDED-BLANK__simulator-drive__iphone16-portrait__i01.png` and `…-landscape__i01.png` show a dead canvas with only the HUD painted on it — „Завърти телефона хоризонтално", a „Демонстрация" button, a live „Compiling …" badge — no street, no car, no cockpit. Re-derived from pixels over the whole frame by a separate script: those frames measure **115 and 130 distinct colours, top-colour share 0.98, dark share 0.99, busy 0.008-0.012, std 11-14**, against **536-630 distinct, top 0.26-0.30, dark 0.37-0.42, busy 0.11-0.18, std 50-63** for a frame in which the street actually rendered. The gate accepts 4 of 4 real worlds and rejects 6 of 6 shells. **The canvas dies at the layout-viewport resize step, in 5 of 5 portrait runs and in landscape, and never comes back.** **5. WHAT CAN AND CANNOT BE REPORTED.** 0 of 5 requested runs produced a scoreable row. The phases taken while the canvas passed the pixel gate — base(idle), overlayOpen, overlayClose — are supported by the recorded statistics (269-393 distinct colours, dark 0.085-0.334, against 35-54 / 0.987-0.997 after the resize), **but no capture was saved at those checkpoints, so „I opened the frame" applies to the refusals only.** Of those phases, the two genuine STATE CHANGES run **156-575 ms** — comfortably inside 1,200 ms. The only overruns are in `base(idle)` (**1,275 and 1,512 ms**), the phase in which the probe changes nothing, on a screen whose HUD is alive by design; the same phase on the static `exams` route reads 151-167 ms with a 1 ms spread, which is this instrument's floor. `toolbarShown`/`toolbarHidden` — the phases the retracted „1,190 ms" came from — were taken over a dead canvas and cannot be reported at all. **6. THE FOUNDER'S CALL, AND IT SHOULD NOT BE READ AS A PASS OR A FAIL. The 1,200 ms budget was set against a measurement now known to be fiction:** the figure it was ratified against was 99.2% instrument, taken on a loading screen, with the pause modal open. Nothing about the product was ever tested against it. Two questions only he can settle: **(a)** should `settle` be defined over STATE CHANGES only, excluding the idle phase — because on the driving shell the number that busts the budget is the phase with no state change, and a live HUD is not a layout failing to settle; **(b)** is a 90 px LAYOUT-viewport shrink the right stand-in for iOS's collapsing toolbar at all, given that it destroys the WebGL world while a real toolbar collapse changes only the VISUAL viewport. **The budget has not been moved and no new figure is claimed.** Raw: `tools/mobile/.out/stability/stability.json`; my re-derivations and jam experiment: `scratchpad/vfy/`.<br><br>**7. AN INSTRUMENT DEFECT I FOUND BY TRIPPING OVER IT: the probe writes its artefact to a FIXED filename, so any later run on any route silently destroys the previous sweep's raw evidence.** `stability.json` is guarded against `--inject-css` negative-control runs (source line 1385) but not against SCOPE: my own two-run check on the `exams` route overwrote the five-run `simulator-drive` artefact this row is evidenced by. **Restored byte-identical from the implementing lane's own backup** (`scratchpad/mp-c8-portrait-raw.json`; my `exams` run is preserved at `scratchpad/vfy/stability-my-exams-run.json`). The filename should carry the route/device scope, or the write should refuse to clobber a sweep taken over a different scope. This is the same class as the reference frame the implementing lane destroyed by running the probe, and it is how a register citation quietly stops pointing at anything. |

### B83 — THE LATCH MEASURES THE WRONG SILENCE (new, and it re-opens the founder's complaint)

The rule as written in `rules/engine.ts` says an encounter closes after `collisionSeparationSec`
of silence **because „the bodies have come apart"**. That premise is false. The upstream sentinel
suppresses its own report whenever closing speed falls to the floor
(`orchestrator/contact.ts:113` — `b.closingKmh <= b.minClosingKmh`, 2 km/h for a lead car), so the
stream also goes silent **when the driver simply stops** — which is exactly what a student does
after hitting something.

**Driven ladder** (`sc-follow-brake`: nose in, stop for *N*, then creep forward 0.6 m — the bodies
**never separate** in any rung):

| stop dwell | measured silence | ПТП billed |
|---|---|---|
| 0.88 s | 1.150 s | 1 |
| 0.90 s | 1.183 s | 1 |
| **0.92 s** | **1.200 s** | **1** |
| **0.94 s** | **1.217 s** | **2** |
| 1.00 s | 1.283 s | 2 |
| 3.00 s | 3.283 s | 2 |

Boundary pinned to **one frame (17 ms)**, and it sits exactly on `collisionSeparationSec = 1.2`.
**A student who noses into a car, sits still for one second, then eases forward 60 cm while still
touching that same car is billed TWO пътнотранспортни произшествия — 20 наказателни точки — for one
crash in which the cars never came apart.** That is the founder's original 90-point complaint in a
smaller form, and the latch cannot see it, because the reducer is told „no contact" when what
happened is „no movement". Owner: `orchestrator/contact.ts` (report contact state independently of
closing speed, and gate the *bill* on speed at the encounter's opening) **or** `rules/engine.ts`
(close an encounter on a separation fact, not on silence). It is a one-question-one-owner call.

### B84 — `sc-follow-standstill`'s standing column is not watched at all (PRE-EXISTING, not this wave)

Asked to count the ПТП on the drill the founder reported, the answer is **ZERO — and that is the
defect.** Driven down the lane into the staged lead at 30 km/h:

- **1.7675 m** of interpenetration, **93 consecutive frames**, first contact **13.08 s @ 29.8 km/h**;
- the runner published **0 contact bodies** to the sentinel across **4,334 frames**;
- sheet: `FOLLOWING_TOO_CLOSE` only — **3 наказателни точки, passed = TRUE.**

Cause: `BrakingLeadCarRunner` latches `watching` **only inside the slam branch**
(`runners.ts` — under `if (this.approachSpeedKmh === 0) { … return null; }`), and this template
deliberately keeps the slam tier inert (`minSlamSpeedKmh: 250`, `slamAt` at y = 520 on a 360 m road),
so the slam can never fire. **Verified pre-existing:** at `HEAD` the inline contact test sat below
the same gate, so B81 faithfully carried forward a watch that was already empty — its „superset"
invariant held, over an empty set. The founder's live nine came from the Rapier/`NpcColliders` path,
which is a different stream; the trace channel has never graded this drill's column at all.
Owner: `orchestrator/runners.ts`.

### The cards (read as RENDERED, through `MistakeConsequenceOverlay`, not as JSON)

All three near-miss cards now carry the scale, the article and the corrective:

- **`sc-ln-obstacle-meeting/mistake-squeeze`** — „*най-тясното между двете купета е измерено и е 0,19 м.
  Деветнайсет сантиметра не са преценка, а късмет*" · ЗДвП чл. 44; чл. 20, ал. 2 · „*1 наказателна

The previous gate opened three findings and three lanes closed them. This gate re-drove every one on
the production stack and re-rendered every card it touched. **All three hold. Two of the gate's own
numbers did not survive the drive and are corrected below, and one new residual was found by
rendering.**

### B84 — CLOSED. The standing column is watched, and a clean drive is still clean

`sc-follow-standstill`, driven down the lane into the staged lead at **30 km/h**:

| | before | now |
|---|---|---|
| minimum separation | **−1.7675 m** | unchanged — the geometry did not move |
| overlap frames | **93** consecutive (60 Hz), unbroken | unchanged |
| first contact | **13.08 s @ 29.8 km/h** | unchanged |
| contact reports | **0** | **93** |
| sheet | `FOLLOWING_TOO_CLOSE` · **3 т.** · **passed = TRUE** | `FOLLOWING_TOO_CLOSE` + `COLLISION` · **13 т.** (10 опасни + 3 основни) · terminated · **passed = FALSE** |

**1.7675 m of interpenetration no longer passes.** Driving the WHOLE column (the L1 `stagedAdd`
queue, three bodies) grades **24 т. · 2 опасни · 1 основна · 1 второстепенна · passed = false** —
two ПТП rather than three, because the third body is entered 0.06 m inside the first encounter's
1.2 s window and is honestly the same accident.

**And the false positive was not traded for the false negative.** The shipped `shadow-correct`
demonstration — the correct drive students watch — was re-driven against the lead alone AND against
the full three-car column: **minimum separation 5.066 m, ZERO overlap frames, ZERO contact reports,
ZERO violations, `CLEAN_DRIVING` commended, passed = true** in both. Three declared bodies standing
in the lane in front of a correct drive produce nothing.

### B83 — CLOSED, and the founder's ladder re-driven rung by rung

`sc-follow-brake`: nose into the standing lead at y = 234, hold, then ease forward 0.6 m.
**Every rung bills ONE**, and the stream the engine now receives carries a widest silence of **one
frame (16.7 ms)** at every rung:

| dwell | contact reports | widest silence | still touching at the creep | ПТП billed | what the OLD stream would have billed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1.150 s | 260 | 0.0167 s | yes (−1.7675 m) | **1** | 2 (1.433 s of silence) |
| 1.200 s | 260 | 0.0167 s | yes (−1.7675 m) | **1** | 2 (1.483 s) |
| 1.217 s | 260 | 0.0167 s | yes (−1.7675 m) | **1** | 2 (1.500 s) |
| 1.283 s | 260 | 0.0167 s | yes (−1.7675 m) | **1** | 2 (1.550 s) |
| 3.283 s | 250 | 0.0167 s | **no (+0.3488 m)** | **1** | 1 |

Plus, on a struck car that cannot drive away: **hit → reverse 6.000 m into measured daylight → hold
2 s → hit again = TWO ПТП** (5.233 s of silence, real separation), and **248 consecutive frames of
sustained contact = ONE ПТП** (bodies never apart, max separation after first contact −0.0237 m).

> **THE 3.283 s RUNG DOES NOT MEASURE WHAT THE LADDER THINKS IT MEASURES, and the register's own
> earlier row said it did.** `BrakingLeadCarRunner.resumeAfterSec = 3` fires three seconds after the
> hit resolves and commands the lead to cruise, so **the crashed car drives itself out of the wreck**
> — lead y 236.19 → 238.47 → 256.22. At a 3.0 s dwell the creep is still embedded (−0.4398 m); at
> 3.283 s it is already **+0.3488 m clear**. So that rung bills ONE for a different reason from the
> other four: there is no second contact to bill. The premise „the bodies never separate in any rung"
> holds up to ~3.0 s on this drill and no further. Owner: `runners.ts` narrative housekeeping — a
> crashed lead accelerating away at 11 m/s is its own defect and is listed below.

### NO REGRESSION — the four the last gate demanded be re-run

| driven | measured | verdict |
|---|---|---|
