## B21-RB — the 2026-08-11 DRIVE-HIS-LESSON gate. **HIS COMPLAINT REPRODUCED IN THE COCKPIT, AND THE SAME DRIVE NOW PASSES.**

> **His words:** he drove „Кръговото движение", signalled his exit, and was told he had not.
>
> **Verdict: `FIXED-SEEN`.** Frames:
> `scratchpad/COCKPIT-1-signal-on-the-ring-d15.0m.png` (the stalk lit, on the ring),
> `scratchpad/COCKPIT-4-full-right-lock-telltale-lit-d22.3m.png` (still lit at 22.3 m — **inside** the
> boundary the objective used to start looking at).

### What was wrong, in one sentence

The objective asked "is the right stalk lit **right now**, and is the car more than `enterRadiusM`
from the island?" — and `scene/cabin.ts` extinguishes the stalk **by itself** when the exit turn
straightens the wheel (ARM 0.22 rad → RELEASE 0.05), which on every shipped geometry happens
**before** the car reaches `enterRadiusM`. The two facts never overlapped, so the student was
convicted for a signal he gave and the **car** cancelled.

### The founder's own lesson, driven tick by tick

`l3-pass` · `district-v1` rb-1, centre (−38.03, −342.96), ring lane r 19.83, **enter 26 / exit 45**,
286.2° traversal. Real Rapier `VehicleSim`, real `CabinControls`, real `stepObjective`; keyboard
input, 18 km/h, the textbook signal 90° before the exit.

| moment | t | d from island | steer | stalk |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| right stalk pressed, ON the ring | 25.48 s | **18.09 m** | 0.000 | **right** |
| exit turn, full right lock | 30.07 s | 18.76 m | **−0.597 rad** (arm is 0.22) | right |
| wheel comes back to centre — **the car cancels itself** | 31.07 s | **22.94 m** | 0.000 | **off** |
| the objective's window finally opens (d > 26) | 31.65 s | 26.07 m | 0.000 | off |

**He missed it by 0.58 s and 3.06 m.** Old evaluator: `voidedExits = 1`, progress 0.50 → 0.00,
«Излезе от кръговото без десен мигач». New evaluator: **arc banked 1° at d = 26, 4° at d = 45**
against a 120° gate → **credited, progress 1.00, voidedExits 0.**

### The family — every roundabout objective in the corpus, before vs after

32 drives each (12/15/18/22 km/h × keyboard/analog × four exit sharpnesses, peel −35°…+4°), textbook
signal 90° before the exit:

| objective | drill | map | ring | enter/exit | traversal | before | **after** |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sc-rb-ring` | sc-roundabout-entry «Кръгово движение» | rb-mini-v1 | 18.00 | 24 / 34 | 180° | 16/32 | **32/32** |
| `sc-rbx-exit` | sc-rb-exit-signal | rb-mini-v1 | 18.00 | 24 / 34 | 270° | 16/32 | **32/32** |
| `sc-rbc-exit` | sc-rb-circulate-priority | rb-mini-v1 | 18.00 | 24 / 34 | 180° | *(= sc-rb-ring)* | **32/32** |
| `sc-rbg-exit` | sc-rb-busy-gap | rb-mini-v1 | 18.00 | 24 / 34 | 180° | *(= sc-rb-ring)* | **32/32** |
| `sc-rbp-exit` | sc-rb-ped-exit | rb-ped-v1 | 18.00 | **29 / 34** | 180° | **13/32** | **32/32** |
| `sc-rb2-exit` | sc-rb-lane-choice (inner lane) | rb-2lane-v1 | 21.94 | 33 / 46 | 270° | 13/32 | **32/32** |
| `l3-pass` | l3-roundabout — **his lesson** | district-v1 | 19.83 | 26 / 45 | 286.2° | 16/32 | **32/32** |
| `ex-roundabout` | lex-exam-1 route exam | district-v1 | 19.83 | 26 / 45 | 286.2° | *(= l3-pass)* | **32/32** |
| `xA-rb` | **all 18,396 exam-bank variants** | district-v1 | 19.83 | 26 / 45 | 286.2° | *(= l3-pass)* | **32/32** |

**The blast radius is not eight objectives — it is 18,427 authored instances**, because every single
exam-bank variant carries `xA-rb` on his lesson's exact geometry. Half of every exam sitting's
roundabout was a coin toss.

**The only reliable way to pass, before:** re-flick the stalk **after** you are already out of the
ring — **32/32 on all five geometries**. The drill exists to punish exactly that.

### What still fails, and must — driven, not argued

| control | before | after | `voidedExits` raised |
|---|---|---|---|
| never signalled at all | 0/32 | **0/32** | **32/32** |
| stalk flicked on the approach *outside* `enterRadiusM`, then silent | 0/32 | **0/32** | **32/32** |
| stalk flicked at the give-way line, *inside* `enterRadiusM`, then silent | 0/32 | **0/32** | **32/32** |
| signal announced a whole traversal early (RB-06) | 0/32 | 4/32 | — |

The silent drive, pushed through the **real lesson engine** (`createLessonSession` + `applyTick`) on
both the compiled `sc-rb-exit-signal` L1 and the real `l3-pass` spec, raises the card verbatim:

> **«Излезе от кръговото без десен мигач»** — „Излизането от кръгово е маневра надясно и се
> сигнализира — мигачът казва на колите зад теб и на чакащите на изхода, че напускаш кръга. Задачата
> остава отворена: върни се в кръговото и излез с пуснат десен мигач. Ако продължиш напред, урокът
> приключва и разборът показва точно това място." · `lawRef: ЗДвП чл. 25`

### Why the fix is measured in DEGREES and not in seconds

The honest and the dishonest drive **overlap in seconds and separate by 120° in arc**:

| | seconds since the stalk last lit | ring arc since the stalk last lit |
|---|---|---|
| correct signal, all 160 drives | 0.0 – 9.3 s | **0.2 – 33.5°** |
| approach-flick cheat, all 160 drives | 9.9 – 36.6 s | **153.8 – 269.3°** |

A 0.6 s gap versus a 120° gap. **And the seconds gap closes to nothing the moment the drill asks the
student to stop:** `sc-rb-ped-exit` makes him wait for a pedestrian between the ring and the zebra —
driven at 3 / 6 / 10 / 20 s of waiting, the stalk was last lit **6.9 / 9.9 / 13.9 / 23.9 s** before
the exit (deep inside the cheat range) while sweeping **1.4° of arc**. All eight halted drives are
credited; all eight failed before. A stationary car burns seconds and sweeps no arc — that is the
whole argument, and it is now measured rather than reasoned.

### Where the student may signal — the honest window, mapped

Credited after the fix, on every geometry: **135° · 90° · 45° · 20° before his exit → 32/32** (and of
the drives that managed to signal at all in the 20° row, **100%**). Announcing at the entry mouth, a
full traversal early, is still refused (4/32) — that is RB-06, the „подвеждащ сигнал" the sibling
template exists to teach.

### Why nothing caught this for months

`content/traces/sc-rb-exit-signal/shadow-correct.trace.json` — the committed "correct drive" every
trace test replays — sets the indicator as a **manual latch with no auto-cancel**: it goes `right` at
t = 41.55 s (d = 17.98 m) and is **still lit at t = 60**, so it sails through `enterRadiusM = 24` at
t ≈ 48.75 and the old level test passes. The recorder has no `CabinControls`. Every bot and trace
test was green while the defect was live in the only place it mattered — a human's hand on a stalk.

### The cockpit, photographed

`/dev/drive-rig?scenario=sc-rb-exit-signal&level=1`, real `LessonPlayShell`, headless Chromium at
2560×1440. Two drives:

- **Gentle exit.** Stalk on at t = 44.96 s, (9.46, 12.11), d = **15.37 m** — on the ring. Objective 0
  «Подмини първите два изхода и остани в кръга» ticks. Stalk survives to d = 31.5 m.
- **Sharp exit — his case.** Stalk on at t = 44.96 s, d = 15.37 m; the car **cancels it itself at
  t = 51.61 s, d = 23.06 m — 0.94 m inside `enterRadiusM = 24`**; and the objective
  «Излез на третия изход с включен десен мигач» **completes at t = 59.07 s.** Under the shipped code
  that drive was void.
  *(The `FAILED_TO_YIELD` / `WRONG_WAY` toasts in that run are the open-loop rig steering into the
  oncoming half of the exit arm — a probe artefact, not the objective.)*

### Gate

`tsc --noEmit --incremental false` **0** · `validate:content` **OK** · `test:tools` **290/290** ·
`vitest run --maxWorkers=4` — **777 files, 11,917 tests, 3 failed.** Two are the declared quarantine
(`exam/content-bank` `ptp-i-parva-pomosht`, `lesson/compose` `l-accidents-first-aid` — the medical
ruling). The third is **not this lane's and not a regression**:
`src/modules/sim/hud/__tests__/predrive-clip-lazy.test.ts` — an **untracked** file written at 11:41
today by the pre-drive-clip lane, asserting source text (`▶ Пусни видеото · ${weightBg}`) its own
`PreDriveTutorial.tsx` does not yet contain. This lane changed **no product code at all**; its whole
output is evidence.

> **A FOURTH red appeared mid-gate and was removed: `src/lib/tsconfigHygiene.test.ts`. The cause is
> now known and it is not an agent.** `next dev` with `KNIJKA_DIST_DIR=<scratch>` **rewrites
> `platform/tsconfig.json` itself** on startup and says so —
> *"We detected TypeScript in your project and reconfigured your tsconfig.json file for you …
> include was updated to add `.next-<dir>/types/**/*.ts`"*. It did it to this lane
> (`.next-rbshot`, reverted within the minute) and it had already done it for a concurrent lane
> (`.next-photo`, reverted here). **AGENTS.md's rule "do NOT add a matching glob to tsconfig.json" is
> unactionable as written — nobody is adding it; Next is.** The durable fix is a `tsconfigPath`
> pointed at a throwaway copy for scratch-dir servers, or a hook that reverts the file on dev-server
> start. Owner: tooling.

Hygiene: `include` is back to the same **6** entries; `git status` carries no probe; the vitest probe
lived in `lessons/__probe__/` and the two Playwright probes in `tools/clips/headless/`, all created,
run and deleted in-session; `.next-rbshot` created by this gate and removed. Four scratch dist dirs
survive from other lanes (`.next-c5rsw`, `.next-photo`, `.next-player`, `.next-rig`, `.next-ttlane`)

## STILL OPEN after the 2026-08-11 DRIVE-THE-SEVEN gate — every item has a NAME, an OWNER and a REASON

**Read these first — they need your judgement, not more engineering.**

1. **You gave two instructions today that contradict each other, and the code is following the newer
   one.** The brief for this gate said: *„stop, lift, press ↓ and hold — «Вдигни крак от педала,
   натисни пак» must still appear ~1.5 s after the flip."* It does not, and it will not, because
   earlier the same day you ruled: *„before when pressing S or Arrow Down it automatically went to R
   and moved backwards and it has to stay like that because thats automatic transmition."* A lane
   built exactly that. Driven: the lever goes to R at 0.49 s and **the car is already reversing on
   the same frame**, reaching 25 km/h; nothing is said, because there is nothing left to explain.
   **What you are trading:** the warning between the R telltale lighting up and the car actually
   moving has gone from „a deliberate lift and a second press" to about **eight hundredths of a
   second**. A nervous re-dab of the brake at a stop line now reverses. That is what a real automatic
   does, and it is what you asked for — but it is the one place where a 17-year-old gets no second
   chance, so say which you want and we will stop moving it. **The message itself is safe either
   way:** on the other routes into R the car still holds at **0.000 km/h** under a held foot and
   still says the line at **+1.47 s**. This gate drove and photographed the `[`-keys route itself;
   the touch gear sheet and the cockpit lever are that lane's own measurements, not this gate's.
   *Owner: you; then `engine/reverseAssist.ts` one line either way.*

2. **298 demonstration captions are still cut short — but only when the phone is SIDEWAYS.** Held
   upright, every one of the 1 811 captions now fits (0 clamped on both portrait phones, measured in
   the real box in a real browser). Turn the phone sideways and 298 of them lose their last line —
   the worst is a roundabout explanation that drops *«Правилното място е едно: джобът между кръга и
   пътеката»*, which is the whole lesson. **This cannot be fixed by moving boxes:** sideways, the
   deck lives in a 116 px corridor and two lines is all the geometry there is. The fix is to shorten
   those 298 lines and put the rest behind „ЗАЩО" — a content job over the trace bank, roughly a day.

   parked car should be — and **the cockpit now says so unprompted** («Ръчната спирачка е вдигната —
   колата е задържана … клавиш Space»), which is exactly the work this gate was checking. But it is
   worth your ruling whether *restart* should hand the student a car he then has to release, or a car
   ready to drive. *Owner: you; then `LessonScene.tsx`'s `resetCar`.*

**Ours, named, not fixed.**

5. **The clutchless-shift refusal was not verified.** On „Напреднал", pressing `[` without the clutch

6. **„Напреднал" has no measured top speed in this gate.** The tier correctly prints no ceiling (it
   has none), which is the row that mattered — but the hand-shifts in the probe stopped taking after
   2nd gear and the pull ended at 55.0 km/h in gear 2, gear-limited. No number is claimed for that

**Added by the 2026-08-11 DRIVE-IT-AND-PHOTOGRAPH-IT gate, including two corrections to the list
above.**

7. **NEEDS YOUR JUDGEMENT — B67, and read your own sentence back before anyone codes it.** You wrote
   *„I cant go more than 100-105 … the car must go much beyond 160-180"*: you are saying the car is
   too **slow** and asking for **more**. The 100–105 reproduces exactly — Нормален on a 90 km/h road
   tops at **96.6 km/h** — so your complaint is the tier governor and nothing else. Every tier,
   measured off the shipped governor: Нормален **58.8 / 96.6 / 126.1 / 145.2** km/h on the 50 / 90 /
   120 / 140 roads; Начинаещ **39.1 / 77.1 / 105.4 / 126.1**; Напреднал uncapped, terminal **168.3**.
   To reach 160–180 on the tier a student actually gets, the governor would have to allow **25–40
   km/h over the posted motorway limit** — on a product whose exam fails him outright for 10 (Наредба
   № 38, прил. № 5, т. 10, б. „в"). **A derived alternative if you want one number: a 160 km/h
   ceiling on „Напреднал"** — 140 (ЗДвП чл. 21, ал. 1, автомагистрала) + 10 (the опасна line) = 150,
   the highest speed this product ever grades, plus one governor band of headroom; measured, a cap of
   160 achieves 154.7. It meets the floor of your band and refuses 180 on the law. *Owner: you; then
   `vehicle/difficulty.ts`.*

8. **NOT A CURRICULUM CALL — A DEFECT, AND IT IS UNDER B67.** The **10-point опасна speeding fault is
   UNREACHABLE on the default tier on every road category B ever drives.** It needs `limit + 10`;
   Нормален's cap **is** `limit + 10` (`NORMAL_CAP_MARGIN_KMH === dangerousSpeedOverKmh`) and the
   governor eases the throttle to zero a full `GOVERNOR_BAND_KMH` (6) below its own cap — so 58.8 <
   60, 96.6 < 100, 126.1 < 130, 145.2 < 150. One of only six опасни грешки in the whole practical
   exam cannot be committed. The второстепенна band survives but has **0.2 km/h** of room on the
   motorway. Measured fix: margin 10 → **16** minimum, **20** for a usable 4.7–8.5 km/h of headroom
   (`scratchpad/b67/b67-measured.txt`, re-runnable). Not landed: the file is owned and being edited by
   the silent-refusal sweep. *Owner: `vehicle/difficulty.ts`, then a guard asserting
   `NORMAL_CAP_MARGIN_KMH > dangerousSpeedOverKmh + GOVERNOR_BAND_KMH`.*

9. **CORRECTION to item 3 above: it is not only the demonstration button.** That item says „with the
   deck open there is **nothing** under 44 px". Measured on all four profiles, in **both** deck
   states, with the car in R: the three tier pills are **78 × 25**, **78 × 25** and **85 × 25 px**,
   and in landscape the overhead cue's own button is **224 × 31 px**. `TouchControls`' own cells are
   all exactly 44 × 44, so the floor is being kept where it was designed in and missed on three HUD
   affordances outside it. *Owner: `hud/` — the tier picker and `CameraAidHint.tsx`.*

10. **The overhead cue prints on top of the demonstration deck again, on portrait phones with the
    deck open — 8 294 px² on 393×852, 5 220 px² on 360×780, photographed.** This is row B13's defect
    returning in a state B13 never measured. **It is not the copy growing a line**, which is what the

11. **CORRECTION to item 6 above, and a note for whoever writes the next probe.** „Напреднал" does
    have a measured ceiling — **168.3 km/h**, solved off the shipped tractive curve, on every domain.
    Separately: `page.waitForFunction(fn, options)` passes `options` as the function's **argument**,
    so `drive-rig.mjs` and `seat.mjs` both ask for 120 s and silently get Playwright's 30 s default.
    On a loaded box that is three failed runs before anyone looks at the right line. *Owner: whoever
    owns `tools/clips/headless/`.*

12. **`platform/next-env.d.ts` is the hole the tsconfig guard does not cover, and it is live.** The
    tracked `platform/tsconfig.json` survived this whole session byte-identical to HEAD (blob
    `264e681e`, 6 include entries) with **six** lanes' dev servers running under `KNIJKA_DIST_DIR`
    — `.next-caption`, `.next-harness`, `.next-parity`, `.next-rig`, `.next-touchaudit`, `.next-w87`.
    The guard works. But `next-env.d.ts` is gitignored, is inside `include`, and Next rewrites its
    `import "./.next-<lane>/dev/types/routes.d.ts"` on every boot: it read `.next-touchaudit` at the
    start of this gate and `.next-parity` at the end. `npm run typecheck` normalises it;
    `npx tsc --noEmit` — the command this register's gates actually run — does not. Both were green
    today by luck of the other lane's file being well-formed. *Owner: whoever owns the gate script.*

7. **A collision raises a blocking „Учебен момент" that PAUSES the simulation, and nothing in the
   telemetry says so.** Three separate measurements in this gate were taken behind a frozen clock
   before the freeze was noticed — the sim clock pinned at one value while speed and gear kept
   reporting their last frame, which reads exactly like a clean, stable measurement. Any future probe
   must assert that `tSec` is advancing before it believes anything. Recorded here so the next lane
   does not lose the same hour. *Owner: the rig; a `paused` flag on `DriveRigSample` would close it.*

8. **The mouse pedals stay hidden for 15.7 s, not 12, on a loaded machine.** `KEYBOARD_HIDE_MS` is
   12 000 ms and a `setTimeout` ran ~3.7 s long with four other browsers on the box. It no longer
   traps anyone — the way back is a quarter-second of mouse movement and the card names it — so this
   is recorded, not fixed. *Owner: nobody, unless the founder wants a shorter hide.*

## STILL OPEN after the 2026-08-10 COUNT-THE-FRAME gate — every item has a NAME, an OWNER and a REASON

**Blocked on the founder — the numbers he needs are in each line, in plain words.**

1. **The phone draw-call budget is unachievable as written, and it is a decision, not a bug.**
   The cockpit and hero car are **56 draw calls in every district** — the tier-low budget for the
   *whole frame* is 70 (hard cap 100). The cockpit alone is 80 % of it before one metre of road
   exists, and this product's whole premise is that you sit in that cockpit. The 70 was never
   derived: doc 82 §2.1's arithmetic supports the resolution and fill lines only, and the allocation
   table it cites gives the hero car **30–50 draws inside a 150-draw laptop frame**. The choice:
   **(a)** re-derive the phone line (a realistic figure looks like 130–160 given a 56-draw cockpit),
   **(b)** rebuild the cockpit GLB so its static shell merges by material — the single biggest
   remaining win, maybe −30 draws, but it needs the animated parts (wheels, needles, steering wheel)
   separated first and it carries real visual risk, or **(c)** accept that every district ships at
   1.2×–2.3× the hard cap and stop scoring against it. Doing nothing keeps a gate red that nothing
   in the renderer can turn green. *Owner: founder + an ADR; `PERF_BUDGETS` deliberately untouched.*
2. **A visual-fidelity-versus-frame-rate trade you should rule on: the prop chunk grid is set to
   600 m, and 400 m is one constant away.** Measured on the heaviest map (d2-v1, tier low): at
   **600 m** the frame is 232.9 draws / 1,226,977 triangles; at **400 m** it is 245.7 draws /
   1,046,833 triangles. So 400 m buys **180,000 fewer triangles for 13 more draw calls**. The lane
   chose 600 because at rung 3 the 400 m grid measured **+7.8 % draws against the pre-fix code**,
   and because doc 82 §2.1 says the phone's scarce resource is draw-call submission, not shading.
   **If you would rather have the triangles than the draw calls, say so** — `PROP_CHUNK_M` is one
   number and the sweep table is in its docstring. Nothing about either setting changes what is on
   screen: the culling A/B moves fewer pixels than half a second of the same frame does.
3. **d2-v1 is 4.1× the tier-low triangle cap and no renderer change will fix it.** 1,228,234
   triangles/frame after a 30 % cut, of which the trees are the majority: the district holds
   **4,276 trees** against a 105-district median of **70**, and there is no low-poly tree — the only
   tree LOD in the product is `treeFraction`, which throws half of them away and leaves the survivors
   at 280–544 triangles each. Either d2 gets a billboard/low-poly tree at tier low (art work), or its
   tree count comes down (content work), or the exam city ships over budget. *Owner: founder to pick
   which; then `tools/maps` or the art pipeline.*

**Ours, named, not fixed.**

4. **The demonstration deck cannot be closed on a 619 px-tall window while the briefing card is up** —
   and that is the state the lesson ships in. 8 of the open deck's controls answer the notification
   column on `elementFromPoint`, **including the deck's own close toggle**, which also carries
   `tabIndex={-1}` so there is no keyboard route. Three forced clicks left it
   `data-deck-open="true"`. The escape hatch is an 18 × 18 px «Скрий инструкциите» button the
   student has to find first; dismiss the briefing and the same profile measures 0 overlaps and 0
   dead controls, so the deck's own geometry is right and the collision is with the column. The reserve
   rule meant to prevent this is a measured no-op (the shell writes the same `max-height` inline, and
   inline outranks any selector) and **must not simply be given `!important`** — that leaves the
   column 102.5 px and clips a five-step authored briefing, which is a THEO-4 violation traded for a
   layout fix. It needs the treatment landscape got: the open deck changes corridor. Clear at
   1440 × 900 and on all four phones, so it is a short-window defect. *Owner: `PlayAreaStyles.tsx`
   + `LessonScene.tsx`'s `DemoDeck`.*
5. **`frameCost.ts`'s ceiling and ratchet bind 6 districts out of 105.** A new district, or an
   existing one made heavier, passes everything until somebody re-measures it. The corpus run is
   ~90 s per district on the existing rig; extending `MEASURED_FRAMES` to the four family
   representatives per class would make the ratchet mean something. Also: `measuredFrame()` stops an
   accident, not a lie — a forged provenance string scores `pass`.
6. **NPC and parked traffic still have 15 `frustumCulled = false` sites** (`traffic/vehicleFleet.ts`
   ×8, `traffic/TrafficLayer.tsx` ×7+) and cost 40–41 draws / 207,209 triangles per frame in d2-v1.
   The same `enableInstancedCulling` + `chunkTransforms` treatment applies directly. Untouched
   because `TrafficLayer.tsx` is in another lane's live edit set.
7. **The rear mirror still re-renders the world at full prop detail** — 83 draws and 492,716
   triangles per entry into a **24,576-pixel** target on d2 (down from 156 / 1,286,132, so the fix
   halved it). A layer-based diet — drop bollards, benches, bins and signs from the mirror pass,
   where they are sub-pixel — is worth roughly another 6 draws/frame at tier low and double at med.
   It is a visual trade-off on the founder's own safety-critical mirror (doc 62 #44), so it is his
   call, not a lane's.
8. **The collapsed deck pill is 134.2 × 26.5 px on every phone.** It is the one control that opens
   the demonstration, and row C6 closed on "0 controls under 44 px on any dashboard route".
9. **Still no frame time, anywhere, for any of this.** Every number in the COUNT-THE-FRAME gate is
   headless chromium on ANGLE/D3D11 with SwiftShader available. Draw and triangle counts are
   scene-graph properties and transfer between machines; milliseconds do not. Doc 82 §2.4's Galaxy
   A16 gate is still open, and **the causal link from these counts to the founder's actual FPS
   complaint is still unproven.** €125 and four hours over `chrome://inspect` closes it permanently.
10. **The fifth vitest failure is handed up, not closed over.** `mistakeBadge.test.tsx` is red on the
    shared tree; everything checkable says it belongs to the collision-billing lane mid-edit (the
    test file is untracked, its whole import graph is in that lane's modified set, and that lane's
    own +1,590-line diff argues for the ordering the test does not expect). It needs their pin
    updated, not a change here.

Mechanism: the template never authors `signalModes`, so `setClusterController` leaves the cluster `"controlled"` and `signals.ts:338–348` keeps live lamps there on purpose. The one-line fix is a trap — `controllerPermission` (`signals.ts:449`) returns null off `"controlled"`, so pinning the mode dark would disarm the officer's grading. **A rig note that cost this lane an hour, so nobody pays it twice.** The play area is NOT a fixed
16:9 — it is sized to its container, so it came out **1422×799 (1.780)** on one lesson and
**1425×843 (1.691)** on another *in the same 1440×900 window*, and it goes to 1.691 whenever the
shell has auto-**fullscreened**. Any frame offered as evidence must print its measured aspect;
`scratchpad/RR-cap.mjs` leaves fullscreen, then trims the window until the play area itself is 16:9,
and logs the result. Two further traps, both hit here: a stop-and-dwell drive **wakes staged actors
early** (it photographed an empty junction on B40 and cost a whole frame set — drive to the pose in
ONE continuous step), and `page.screenshot`'s 30 s default times out on this box under load.

## STILL OPEN after the 2026-08-09 SCALES gate — every item has a NAME, an OWNER and a REASON

**No percentage closes this section and this report does not end in one.** This gate did not build;

report above it. **It supersedes the two lists below it only where it names a row; everything else in
them is still open.** Three of the lanes' own disclaimers turn out to be wrong, and they are named
below rather than repeated.

Nine surfaces photographed at desktop **and** phone through the real components on the real app CSS
(`/dev/popup-rig?state=scales|column|end`, `/dev/micro-quiz`), plus **three live cockpit drives** on
`/dev/drive-rig` at a measured 16:9 (`sc-speed-dangerous` L1 · 1281×720 aspect 1.779; `lex-exam-1` ·
1280×719). Frames and text dumps:
`scratchpad/V-frames/` (`01-scales-desktop.png` … `12-calibration-before.png`, `b58/`, `exam/`).
Every figure below was re-derived by running the shipped function, not by reading it.

| # | what is wrong | owner (the actual file) | why it is not fixed |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`verifyCitations` proves a citation is INTERNALLY consistent, not that it is about the row it prices — and the exact defect the wave says it closed still ships** | `lib/content/law/corpus.ts` — `verifyCitations` check (3) | Reproduced: take `pen-speeding-urban-21-30` („Превишена скорост в населено място с 21 – 30 km/h"), set `contextQuoteBg` to the **traffic-light** case and move `offencePhraseBg` to match. **`verifyCitations` returns `[]`.** The check compares the phrase to the *citation's own quotes*; nothing compares it to the row's `id`, its `titleBg`, or the violation code it prices. The wave's summary says the check answers „is this sentence ABOUT THIS OFFENCE?" — it answers „is this sentence about the offence this citation claims", which is a different question. **The fix is cheap and mechanical:** require each row to declare the conduct once and assert the phrase against *that*, or key `offencePhraseBg` off a per-row enum so two rows about different conduct cannot carry the same phrase. |
| 2 | **Every „0 контролни точки" verdict on the fault card is proven against a copy of the exhaustive list that is missing an item and carries a vendor watermark inside the statute text** | `modules/sim/rules/consequences.ts` — `CP_LIST_HEADER` (line 339), plus `CONTROL_POINTS_BUDGET.max` / `.newDriver` (323, 330) and the т. 20 / т. 21 quotes (1034, 1057) | `penalties.json` is clean — **0 snapshot citations** — but `consequences.ts` still carries **five** bare „Наредба № Iз-2539", which `actIdForActName` resolves to the **2025 SARS snapshot**. Two measured facts about that file: (a) its чл. 6 has **21 enumerated items, not 22 — т. 3 is truncated mid-sentence** („…откаже да му") by a „Източник: Правно-информационни системи „Сиела“ / 24/01/2025 г." pagination watermark, which appears **inside 16 units** of the act; (b) its чл. 2, ал. 6 is the **repealed** restoration rule. The 0-point *claim* is still true under the consolidation (new т. 12 reaches only чл. 182, ал. 1, т. 5 и 6, ал. 2, т. 6, ал. 3, т. 6) — **but the evidence link a student taps opens a superseded, physically incomplete list.** |
| 3 | **The version guard is sound for positive claims and structurally blind to negative ones** | `modules/sim/rules/__tests__/citation-version.test.ts` — „a quote cut from the 2025 snapshot is wording the current text still carries" | It requires only that the **quoted words** survive into the consolidation. `CP_LIST_HEADER` quotes the list's *header line*, which is identical in both editions — so the guard passes while the **list underneath it** was amended. A claim of the form „X is not in this exhaustive list" cannot be secured by checking that the list's title is unchanged. **One clause closes it:** when a quote is a list header and the figure is `not-listed`, require the whole cited unit to be byte-identical across editions, or forbid `not-listed` from citing a superseded act at all. |
| 4 | **Three lanes disclaimed the third red as „another lane's untracked file". It is this wave's own new file.** | the B35 / four-rows lane | `src/modules/sim/__tests__/law-citations.test.ts` fails on `modules/sim/world/components/__tests__/world-label.test.ts:286`, whose canvas-overflow fixture passes `lawRef: "к"`. `git ls-files --error-unmatch` fails on it — and it is `??` **alongside `worldLabel.ts` and `signalHeadLabels.ts`, the two source files that lane created**. It is not another lane's in-flight file; it is the B35 build's own test, and it is a **one-token fix in their own fixture** (use a real citation, or rename the key so the scanner does not read it as one). |
| 5 | **The live exam protocol bar and the compact end-of-drive chip are still unphotographed — and the reason is structural, not a dead dev server** | `components/sim/lesson-ui/LessonPlayShell.tsx:2687` and `:1812` | The previous lane blamed a Turbopack compile. It is not that. The bar renders only when `examMode && snap.phase === "driving"`, and the only exam lesson (`lex-exam-1`) opens on a **13-step `preDriveMode: "assess"`** that must be performed on real cockpit **mouse** controls. Driven for 111 s with a keyboard rig: `phase=preDrive obj=0/13`, `v=0.0` at every frame (`V-frames/exam/Vexam-t48.0-v0-y-88.1.png` — the frame is real and shows exam chrome, just not the bar). **Any future lane will hit the same wall.** Both are one-line `pointsBg("exam", …)` calls covered by the scanner; closing them needs either a rig that clicks the pre-drive, or a `?state=` on the existing popup-rig that mounts the shell in `examMode` + `driving`. This is the same wall as row 5 of the „open the app yourself" list above (Урок 1, 1 of 13, mouse-only). |
| 6 | **The theory exam — the other half of the product — still prints a bare „т.", and the disclosure of that gap is two files short** | `app/(dashboard)/exams/page.tsx` (3), `exams/[attemptId]/page.tsx` (5), **`components/exam/ExamResultView.tsx:134`**, **`components/exam/ExamRunner.tsx:671`** | Ten bare sites on the 97-point scale. The wave declared this deliberately out of scope and it was **right not to relabel it „изпитни т."** — in this product that phrase now means the practical sheet. But its `left` list named only `app/(dashboard)/exams/*` and `admin/page.tsx`; **the two `components/exam` files are the ones the student actually sits in front of during the mock exam** and they were not named. The consequence on screen: a student who has just learned „изпитни т. = the driving mark" opens an *изпит* that says only „т.". Needs a fifth scale (`theoryExam`) in `scales.ts` or its equivalent in `modules/exam`. |
| 7 | **`MistakeConsequenceOverlay` has still never been photographed — but it is no longer unread** | `components/sim/lesson-ui/MistakeConsequenceOverlay.tsx` | `/dev/popup-rig?state=overlay` renders the scene but not the overlay (text dump: 55 characters, „ЗАДАЧА 2/3 · +1 · Спри плътно до бордюра"). It needs a recorded mistake trace to mount, and **no frame exists, so by this file's own rule the row stays open.** **What changed 2026-08-10:** „verified by source and by the scanner only" is no longer true. `lesson-ui/mistakeBadge.test.tsx` mounts the real component through `renderToStaticMarkup` and asserts the visible Bulgarian, and the CLOSE-THE-SEAM gate re-rendered **all 336 demo cards on both the demonstration and the live path** — the badge defect was invisible in the JSON and visible in that markup. Markup is not pixels: what is still unseen is the LAYOUT (does the −10 chip collide with the title on a phone, does the чл. 48 rider fit), not the words. |

### Not a defect — recorded so it is not re-filed

| what | why |
|---|---|
| **`app/(dashboard)/review/ReviewClient.tsx:673` prints `{q.points} т.`** | It is the **dev-only** founder review tool: `page.tsx` calls `notFound()` when `NODE_ENV === "production"` and it is never linked from navigation. Not a student surface. |
| **„×1.5 / ×2.0" on the teach card** | Pre-existing — the wave only re-indented the line (`git diff` shows the string unchanged). Not a figure this wave introduced. |
| **The mirror assembly's top 22 canvas rows sit above the frame at 16:9** | Measured by the B58 lane and confirmed here. A real interior mirror sits at the top of the driver's view; the **glass** is fully in frame and unclipped. |

closed, found, or is handing over. **Two rows are waiting on the founder and are named as his, not
counted against us.**

### Closed by this gate

| what | evidence |
|---|---|
| **`THE-TEN-POINTS` — a student's exam ended for driving around a car door on an empty street** | **THE COMPLAINT IS FOUND AND THE CAUSE IS NAMED.** Наредба № 38 приложение № 5 т. 10 б. „в" is a closed list of six cases, read out of the ingested act by this gate. `CROSSED_SOLID_LINE` matched none of them and is now **основна (3)**. The decider was the catalogue contradicting itself: знак **В24** requires a measured overtake against a real lead car and bills **3**; the paint required only position and billed **10**. All 53 codes grounded clause-by-clause in `rules/n38.ts`; a 22-test pin re-derives the act's numbers at run time and **forbids any ten-point charge that rests on geometry alone**. *(The verdict label stays open — see „Still open", row 1.)* |
| **`HIS-ACTUAL-PHONE` — both runners scored 0 of 20 on the founder's iPhone, in both orientations** | **CLOSED, with the insets proven applied rather than assumed.** One token: `app/layout` ships `viewport-fit=cover`, `globals.css` pays the inset back on `<body>`, and `DashboardShell` then asked for a full `min-h-dvh` *inside* that padded box — so every page was permanently 34 px (portrait) / 21 px (landscape) taller than the screen, and `useQuestionBudget` cut the stem against a floor that could never be reached. Re-derived here: practice **0/20 → 20/20** portrait, exam **0/20 → 20/20** both orientations; `saRewrites > 0` on 160/160 and `<body>` padding reading back the asked inset on every row. The two zero-inset Android columns did not move — the control. |
| **`B65` — no lamps, wires, poles, fences, tyre marks; one repeated tree; a street ending in a field** | **CLOSED from the seat**, `b65furn/B65-before-after.png`. One predicate: `buildProps` gated all street furniture on `ARTERIAL_CLASSES`, and every authored teaching street is `residential` — so `sp-creep-v1` had **0** lamp columns (not the 1 the register printed), and because every bench, bin, planter and bollard derives from the lamp run, the product had no street furniture on any teaching street at all. |
| **Door 6 — post-exam review re-read the question text** | **CLOSED, and without paying either price §10.3 assumed.** A 16-hex fingerprint of exactly the bytes the grader taught from — 720 B per 45-question paper, 1.8% of what audit M-1 removed. Re-run here over all 1 089 rows in all three pin states: **0 rows speak a forbidden string**, and the probe was made to prove its own reach first. |
| **Four trace-determinism failures that parked the previous attempt at this wave** | **GONE.** Four traces re-recorded; spot-checked `sc-rb-busy-gap/mistake-barge-lead` by parsing both revisions — `meta` identical, `samples` identical (361 → 361), only the annotation string moved. The seven PE districts gained islands/tables/skews and needed **no** re-recording: the crossing point does not move and no edge changes width, so the `x=4.06` rail every PE trace drives is untouched. All 22 replay green. |
| **The tree was red in six places across the reporting lanes** | **GREEN.** `11 039 passed / 2 failed / 170 skipped` across 737 files, and the 2 are exactly the protected reds. The `pe-districts` lamp-column collision, the trace determinism four and the `clearanceProvenance` pair all resolved as the lanes converged. |

### Still open — with a name, an owner and a reason

| # | name | owner | reason it is still open |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **`THE-TEN-POINTS` has never been read off a result screen** | whoever next drives `sc-ov-solid-return` L3 | The row is about a number he **saw**. The act is right, the code is right, 813 tests are green — and no one has photographed a result screen reading „3 наказателни точки". One drive closes it. This gate refused the `FIXED-SEEN` label for exactly this reason. |
| 2 | **`worldRuntime.ts:1797` — nothing measures oncoming traffic on a solid centre line** | `runtime/worldRuntime.ts` | `tick.solidCenterLine !== true` disables the corridor tracker precisely where sight distance is worst. Before the ruling a geometric 10 masked it; now it is a visible hole — **a genuinely dangerous overtake across a solid line bills 3 unless it ends in a collision.** Deleting the clause changes `ocArmed` for every М1 scenario and requires the affected traces re-recorded, so it was not smuggled in behind a severity edit. |
| 3 | **`EMERGENCY_LANE_DRIVING` is the weakest remaining 10, and its detector is the same shape as the one just demoted** | lead ruling | Read here: position in an authored emergency lane + `laneCount > 1` + moving + forward gear + sustained, **with no test for another road user** — structurally identical to what `CROSSED_SOLID_LINE` was. It survives on a legal distinction (the lane's *purpose* is to hold stopped vehicles; the opposing half of a two-way street is empty most of the time). The lane declared this itself in `contestedBg` rather than hiding it. If the lead rejects the distinction, the code falls to основна with it. |
| 4 | **`sc-vu-door-zone`'s mistake demo now PASSES the drill** | `templates-vru.ts` | Uncovered by the fix, not caused by it: the „late dodge" counter-proof used to fail on the unlawful 10 points alone. Strip the charge and the swerve ticks every objective the template authored — the drill never encoded „не пресичай осевата" as a gate; the severity was doing the objectives' job. Pinned deliberately in `s10-vru-pack-bot-completion.test.ts` so closing the hole turns those assertions red on purpose. |
| 5 | **`q-vehicle-058` in practice at 852×393 needs 11 px of scroll** | founder's call | Nothing is hidden — every word and all five options are visible. The lever is measured: practice draws 14 px answer text at **22.75 px** line-height where the exam draws it at **20.3 px**, so three wrapped rows cost practice ~21 px more. Not pulled, because tightening answer leading across the whole practice runner in the tightest orientation is a readability decision. Two exam cases at the same size (`q-krastovishta-029`, `q-vehicle-063` with the under-5-minutes banner) still clip one stem line; same lever. |
| 6 | **C6 — the exam bar's „Следващ →" is 38 px tall** | lead ruling | **PRE-EXISTING and provably so:** the className is byte-identical at HEAD, carries no `min-h-11`, and the bar is `flex items-center` so no parent padding can set it. Also „→ Следващ въпрос" 40×38 on small-portrait and „Отбележи за преглед" 43×44. Growing them to 44 px takes 6 px off the answers on the surface under a 40-minute clock — a trade, not a commit. |
| 7 | **The in-scene rear-view quad swallows three separate rows** | `scene/CameraRig` | **The single most useful finding of the cockpit lane.** Not a DOM element — it is CameraRig's `rv` quad parked in front of the camera, at roughly x 784–945, y 222–305 on a 1264×620 canvas, i.e. ~224 px right of centre and ~80 px above the horizon: exactly where a 3 m sign on the right verge at 20–60 m projects. Measured landing inside it: B58's В26 «50» at (874, 291), B27's Б2 at (876, 245), B55's right-hand pedestrian head at (852, 287). Its rect is identical across four districts and eight poses, so it is camera-parked, not world geometry. **One camera constant sits between the product and three rows.** |
| 8 | **`pe-bus`'s refuge island exists in the JSON and cannot be seen from the seat** | PE crossings lane | Found by this gate. `island: {widthM: 2, approachM: 9, departM: 9}` is the most compact in the family and at 11 m the zebra still reads as unbroken bars. A schema field is not a thing he can see. |
| 9 | **At the spawn, `pe-bus` / `pe-child` / `pe-cane` still read as one street** | PE crossings lane | Three of seven, down from five, but not zero. The measured lever is counter-intuitive and worth acting on: the driver sits at `x=+4.06` and the left bay centre is `x=−10.13`, so a left-kerb car 6–19 m ahead is 37–67° off-axis, behind the A-pillar. **What pays is which kerb is EMPTY, not which is full.** |
| 10 | **The look: detail collapses past ~15 m** | surfaces/materials lane | The road is *not* untextured — PBR colour+normal+roughness, detile, UDN detail normal, macro noise, wear decals. Against validated controls (flat 0.16 / detailed 5.02) it measures 1.34–1.60 at 8 m and **0.72–0.93 at 20 m**: the exact failure `roadSurface.ts`'s own header claims to have fixed. **Attack MIP/anisotropy/detail-tile falloff and missing macro features — not new base maps, which cost bytes and change nothing at 20 m.** |
| 11 | **Draw-call headroom at `low` is nearly gone** | next world lane | Measured in this gate: `pe-cane` **65** and `d2-v1` **66** against a **70** soft cap. Triangles are fine (33% of budget). **One more instanced kind on the PE family breaches the soft cap.** Also: at `low` the tier is `colorOnly`, so the three normal maps that are 75% of the texture weight are never fetched — **any fix expressed as a normal-map improvement is invisible on the tier his FPS complaint is about.** |
| 12 | **`B58` (b) — `ov-keepright-v1` still ends into a field with a flow vehicle on open grass** | TERMINUS pass over `gen_ov_keepright.mjs` | In the windscreen for the last two seconds of the graded drive (ends `y=321.0` of 360 m). Same family as B65's terminus. Should run in the **same sweep** as the PE terminus work, not as a second convention. |
| 13 | **`B64` — the bus stop is still three grey volumes** | B65 furniture pass | Needs a placeable street-furniture kind: `world/types.ts` + builder + renderer + district authoring. One entry in that pass, not a lane of its own. |
| 14 | **`B35` — a dead signal head still reads as three coloured discs** | world/HUD label layer | The pixels are right (every lens at the unlit tint, junction unoccluded), but a student cannot tell a dead head from a working one at 14×. The prescribed affordance (the B42 bubble anchored to the head) is unbuilt. |
| 15 | **The controller bubble's painted `lawRef` grew 32 → 43 chars and was never rendered** | `traffic/TrafficLayer.tsx` | `drawControllerBubble` paints it with a bare `fillText`, centred, **no wrap and no `measureText` clamp**, on a 1024 px canvas. Guarded with a 48-char budget derived from a line that *has* been looked at — but that is arithmetic, not a frame, and it is a what-can-he-see question. |
| 16 | **74 bank citations hide an annex number behind „прил."** | `content/questions` owner | `normaliseUnitRef` knows only the full word „приложение", so „прил. № 2, знак Б2" reads as numberless to every check in the repo — including the citation freeze that reports „0 with a number on an act we cannot open". 40 distinct, **100% on Наредба № РД-02-21-1**, which the repo does not hold. The sim scanner now refuses the shape; 74 bank rows need re-pinning. |
| 17 | **No 2026-consolidated Наредба № Iз-2539 exists to ingest** | law corpus | Proven, not assumed: a HEAD on the pinned SARS URL returns the same **226 435 bytes, last-modified 2025-01-28** — re-fetching cannot produce the 2026 text. Both amending acts are held verbatim instead, but the consolidated act is not, so `pen-alcohol-05-08` **withholds** its control-points figure rather than serving the superseded 8. Erring honest, by design. |
| 18 | **`gen_streetwall.mjs` is not idempotent at HEAD** | maps tooling | A landmine for everyone: it is a mandatory post-pass to `gen_pe_crossings.mjs`, but running it also silently rewrites `d2-v1.json` and `tj-emerge-v1.json`. **Those are exactly the two files and the two diff shapes that reddened the previous attempt at this wave.** Reverted here; any agent who runs any map generator will re-trip it. |
| 19 | **`LessonPlayShell`'s fullscreen request is non-deterministic headless** | `tools/` rigs | Requested from a mount effect relying on the start click's transient activation; headless there is no gesture, so it is granted or denied **between identical runs**, and the two outcomes give different canvas geometry (1264×620 vs 1028×577). Every projected pixel and crop rectangle moves with it — it nearly cost the B55 verdict. The cockpit rig now forces and verifies the state; `drive-rig.mjs` does not. |
| 20 | **`tools/mobile/lib/probe.mjs` measures the fold against the viewport edge** | mobile harness | It now runs with real insets so its safe-area column is honest for the first time, but any fold number out of the shared budget sweep is still optimistic by the bar's height. **`fold-sweep.mjs` is the instrument to trust for fold questions.** |
| 21 | **A first-encounter основна is taught, not scored** | lead ruling | Shipped A12 behaviour for the whole class, not something this wave invented — but `CROSSED_SOLID_LINE` has now joined it, so the sim→theory recommender will **miss a student's first solid-line crossing entirely**. Worth a ruling: is teach-first right for a marking offence taught from lesson one? |

That IS the fault the drill teaches and the new detector warns 2 s out, so it is arguably the lesson working. **But the guidance marker is the one thing on screen telling him where to go, and it points past the hazard.** Worth a ruling: either the marker follows the tail, or the copy says „stop where the queue is, not where the circle is". |

### Opened by this gate — named, owned, and none of them a percentage

| # | what is wrong | owner (the actual file) | why it is not fixed |
|---:|---|---|---|
| 1 | **A ruling is owed before anyone spends B16's re-record, and it is his, not ours** | the founder | `270°` of kerb is satisfied only from **above**. At R = 46 the top-down is a perfect annulus and the **cockpit frame reads worse than at R = 31** — a 41.5 m island fills the horizon as parkland and the ring's curvature leaves the windscreen, so it reads as a T-junction. Both frames are on the B16 row at an equal 23.7 m to the kerb. **If his sentence is about the shape of the map, 46 / 84 is right; if it is about what he sees on approach, the target is nearer 31–39 and the 270° threshold should be retired.** The spend behind the wrong answer is 36 trace files, four generators, a `gen_streetwall.mjs` re-run and three `templates-*.ts` files. |
| 2 | **The parked row is right-of-travel only — every bay is drawn on both sides and filled on one** | `modules/sim/traffic/TrafficLayer.tsx` — `computeParkedCars` | `const nx = dy; const ny = -dx` is the right-hand normal with **no side alternation**, so all 16 bodies across the six bayed PE districts stand at `x = +10.13` and the left bay is always empty tarmac. **This is the single biggest reason five of the seven pedestrian streets still read as one composition.** The file was on the do-not-touch list and another wave has it open. |
| 3 | **No median, refuge island, raised table, staggered or angled crossing is expressible at all** | `world/types.ts` + `runtime/district.ts` (mirrored) + `builders/markings.ts`, `decals.ts` | `DistrictCrossing` is `{id, x, y, kind, signalized, edgeId}` and `CrossingKind` is `signals\|marked\|unmarked\|unknown` — there is no field for any of it, so that half of the PE brief was not deliverable from the generator. **A central reservation cannot be faked with a building either:** `cityBuildings.DATA_HEIGHT_MIN_M` clamps every authored volume up to 3 m, so a 0.3 m island renders as a 3 m wall across the road. A schema change, and `runtime/**` is reserved. |
| 4 | **Наредба № РД-02-21-1/2023 is cited constantly by the content bank and its text is not in the repo** | `content/law/sources.json` + `content/law/README.md` (the re-fetch recipe is already written) | The row is catalogued `coverage: "index-only"`, `extraction: null`, **no sha256**, while five `content/audits/*.audit.json` files carry the rule line *"every sourceLine is quoted verbatim from … the locally extracted ППЗДвП and Наредба № РД-02-21-1 text (sha256-pinned in `content/law/sources.json`)"*. `sources.json` holds three full-text acts and this is not one of them, **so every `lawRef` citing that ordinance is currently unverifiable against the corpus — and the signs bank cites it constantly.** Ingesting it would also settle whether a statutory В26 repetition distance exists and replace the authored 120 m with a cited number. *(Flagged only: `content/**` is reserved.)* |
| 5 | **The В26 repeat is a junction restatement, not a mid-block cadence — and the deep block is still silent** | `world/builders/props.ts` — `LIMIT_REPEAT_MAX_KMH` | The new plate stands 35 m past the junction; the cadence that would fill the rest stops **below 50 km/h on purpose** (50 is ЗДвП чл. 21's inferable built-up default; 30 and 40 are reductions a driver cannot infer). So on `tj-emerge-v1`'s 160 m westbound arm he sees one plate at 35 m and then nothing for ~125 m. **Deliberate and measured** — widening to 50 buys these two districts nothing and over-signs `sp-creep2-v1` by 5 plates — but it is a judgement call worth revisiting if he drives a long junction-less 50 stretch and still feels blind. |
| 6 | **A streetwall canopy hides the new В26 until ~15 m** | `tools/maps/gen_streetwall.mjs` (the occluder), not the sign pass | `b34lane-xm16` shows the pole but not the disc; it clears at about `x = −20`. Fine at 14 км/ч; roughly one second of reading time at the posted 50. The post's station is correct by construction — the occluder is another lane's building. |

### FR-21 — the pavement parking, measured rather than estimated

**68 of 100 committed districts still place at least one procedurally parked body off every
carriageway.** 58 of them need **only a `parkingBand` tag** — the frontage headroom is already there,
from +0.03 m to +101 m. **Ten are genuinely blocked, and they are four different problems:**

- **Six generators share one building constant and one number** — a b-approach/corner block at
  14.12 m against a 15.975 m need, deficit ~1.85 m: `mv-uturn-v1`, `pk-ban2-v1`, `pk-banx-v1`,
  `pk-busstop-v1`, `pk-double-v1`, `pk-rail-v1`. **152 bodies come off the pavement for a 2 m nudge
  of one constant in six `tools/maps/gen_*.mjs` files** — the same nudge 57 other generators already
  have. *This is the cheapest 150-body win left in the product.*
- **`pk-drive-v1`** (8 bodies): the blocker is `pkd-b-garage` at 12.000 m and **it cannot move — the
  drill is a driveway meeting the street.** Needs a parking band with a gap at the driveway.
- **`rx-tram-stop-v1` and `rx-tram-island-v1`** (26 bodies): the blockers are a tram shelter and a
  tram island at 0.400 m. **A tram island is 0.4 m off the centreline because that is what a tram
  island is.** The row must be suppressed on the island side.
- **`district-v1`** (80 bodies) — the OSM-derived Sofia map, the only non-micro one. 13 of its 18
  offending edges are blocked, worst frontage **3.139 m**. **Nothing can move: those buildings are
  surveyed from the real city.** That map needs the row placed from each edge's *actual*
  kerb-to-frontage distance instead of a fixed cross-section.

### Housekeeping this gate is handing over rather than fixing under other lanes' running servers

- **`platform/tsconfig.json` carries six scratch globs across three families** — `.next-harness/**`,
  `.next-rig/**` and now `.next-exhdr/**`. The last register said "four"; it is six, and a new family
  has appeared since. `src/lib/tsconfigHygiene.test.ts` fires on it. **Reverting is futile while those
  dev servers run — they re-inject on the next compile — so it needs the servers stopped and the file
  stripped in the same minute.**
- **`tools/clips/headless/sheets/` is not gitignored** and holds **50 untracked PNGs**, two of them
  written by this wave. One line in `.gitignore` closes it; the lane claim "zero PNGs in the repo" was
  false, and the directory has been like this since 21 July.
- **`drive-rig.mjs:145` swallows its own timeout** — `page.waitForFunction(fn, { timeout })` passes the
  options object as Playwright's `arg`, so the **30 s default** applies. Needs
  `waitForFunction(fn, null, { timeout })`.
- **`dev-surfaces-gated.test.ts` is permanently red on this hardware, and was written off as a flake.**
  `KNIJKA_DIST_DIR never reaches application code` file-walks the whole app source tree against
  vitest's **5 s default** `testTimeout`; the walk costs **22–40 s** on E:. It failed three times out
  of three in isolation. **The safety property it guards is fine — the test is not.** One
  `testTimeout` argument closes it, and until then every gate on this box reports a red it will be
  tempted to explain away.
- **`world/__tests__/zzz-zebra-scratch.test.ts` has 0 tests** and vitest counts that as a failed file.
  Somebody's leftover; delete it.
- **A long drive on the shared `:3200` dev server can be silently spliced by a Fast Refresh.** This
  gate's first 70-second wait was destroyed twice that way and only the rig's own clock-reset guard
  caught it. **Two waves of B15 evidence were produced on that server.** The guard is the best thing
  either instrument lane built; nobody should run a long drive without reading its warning line.

| **FR-50's second half** | `mw-v1` is now **2600 m** per carriageway (was 1006) and `gen_motorway.mjs` refuses a ≥130 segment shorter than 2400 m. **Nobody has driven it to see 160.** | one drive |
| **Tyre marks** | Named and refused by the art lane rather than faked: a real skid needs a new `DecalKind`, a manifest cell and an along-ribbon placement rule in `world/builders/decals.ts` + `constants.ts`. | world/builders |

## WHAT IS STILL BROKEN AND WHY — including what is not ours to fix

*Rewritten 2026-08-02. Everything the previous version listed under "no excuse" is closed; this is the
new list, and it is longer because we finally looked.*

**Closed since the last version of this section:** B7 / B20 (the pre-drive is mouse-only, 13/13, with a
keydown tripwire) · B16 (the roundabout has an island — downgraded to PARTIAL, not closed, because the
outer boundary is still not circular on tight rings) · B-NEW-1 (the frame-zero placeholder pose armed
the finish gate; 75 s parked in the real app, still driving) · the headlight half of the falsehood
budget — **in the gate's arithmetic only, see below.**

### Broken, ours, not fixed, no excuse

- **B64 — `instructionsBg` is never rendered to a student, and it is the biggest thing on this page.**
  All 150+ templates author numbered instructor steps. They are validated for contiguity and read by
  the world-referent gate. `scenario/compile.ts` never maps them into the compiled lesson and no
  component reads them. The "virtual driving instructor that explains every decision" — doc 64
  THEO-4, founder-ratified — is authored and invisible. **This also means three of this wave's copy
  fixes were delivered into a dead field:** the headlight lines on 16 templates (FR-40), the
  plural-pedestrian rewrites (FR-42), and the reason-to-brake line (FR-49). Owner: a lane that takes
  `instructionsBg` → compiled lesson → a real surface, and re-tests FR-40/42/49 against a frame.
- **B15 — the roundabout convicts you for a car that never comes.** Waited 46 s on the give-way paint
  with held glances, no circulating car in any frame, then took −10 t. the instant I moved.
  `RB_LOCK_PLAYER_ENTRY_M = 14 m` but the М8 bars are 18.2 m out, so a lawful stop stays in the SYNC
  branch where the ETA floors at 7.28 s and re-times the car forever. The sibling
  `PriorityFromRightRunner` got a stopped-witness release this wave; the roundabout runner did not.
  Owner: `orchestrator/runners.ts` — port the release, or raise the lock above 19 m.
- **B29 / B30 — you are told to give way from where you cannot see.** Measured: the Б1 line is at
  y 122.275, the credited zone is 104…122, and the priority carriageway's near kerb is at 145.94 —
  **23.7 m beyond the line.** Plus a nose-to-tail parked row in the sight corridor. Both of this
  scenario's mistake clips also fail the rig's R1 gate, so the drill cannot even be taught by clip.
  Owner: `templates-junctions.ts` (lineDistM / the yield anchor) + the junction's parked-body clear zone.
- **B38 / B27's second half / B30 — scenario lessons default to ZERO ambient traffic.**
  `compile.ts SCENARIO_DEFAULT_TRAFFIC.vehicleCount = 0`, and only **11 `traffic:` declarations exist
  across ~197 template entries**. One scripted car crosses and the boulevard is dead. This is also
  FR-13 and FR-27. Owner: templates data.
- **B40 / B50 / B53 / B63 — the same street, over and over.** Five signal lessons (catalog 10, 17, 18,
  19, 21) are sx-v1 from one spawn; seven pedestrian lessons (24–30) are seven copies of one 150 m
  ribbon — all **1 edge / 2 nodes / 1 crossing / 0 signs / 0 signals**; 33 and 36 open on the same
  seat view. Each lesson passes every gate it has. **The SEQUENCE is the defect**, which is exactly
  the failure mode this register was created to catch. Owner: `tools/maps` — road variety, not facades.
- **B80 — barging into oncoming traffic at the narrowing raises no fault at all.** Two runs, 44 s and
  52 s; the only fault either timeline records is lane-keeping. Owner: the give-way rule for a
  narrowing.
- **B81 / FR-32 — markings and signs are class-dependent.** On the trunk road: crisp edge lines,
  centre dashes, lane dividers, a В24 on its post. On the residential street he actually drives: one
  sparse dashed centre line and bare asphalt. One district carries two sign faces in total.
- **The parked-car-on-the-footway arithmetic (FR-21).** `edgeParkingWidthM = 0` for `residential`, so
  `TrafficLayer.tsx:544` puts every procedurally parked body at 10.125 m against a kerb at 8.125 m.
  **75 of 100 committed districts have no parking-band class**, so on 75 districts every parked car
  stands on the pavement. The walker no longer walks through them; the cars are still up there.
- **The marker pillar blinds the driver at the stop (B24 / B27 / B28).** An 11 m × r 1.0 m open
  cylinder, DoubleSide, AdditiveBlending — stand where it tells you to stand and your eye is inside
  it, so the whole windscreen is a green wash and the Б2, the priority road and the parked row all
  vanish. Two drills grade a left-right-left scan the marker makes impossible. Owner: `RouteGuidance.tsx`.
- **The falsehood budget still convicts on 2 codes / 7 scenarios / 33 rung-codes.** `WRONG_WAY` 6/29
  (down from 19/88 — real geometry) and `SPEED_TOO_FAST_FOR_CURVE` 1/4.
- **New debt this wave took on:** three census classes rose by exactly the ten new parking drills
  (S3 13→23, B3 8→18, L12 56→65). No existing lesson regressed, but the ten new drills owe a
  terminability anchor.

### Blocked on money, not on engineering

- **FR-19 / D3 — real video tutorials.** Atlas **402**, FAL **−0.21 and locked**, Poyo no entitlement.
  The seam ships stills that swap for clips with no rework. Nothing is waiting on us.
- **P0–P7 criterion 7 — the AI assistant** returns HTTP 402 *insufficient balance*. Code and diagnosis
  complete.

### Blocked on your machine

- **B52 / FR-43 (children) and B41 / FR-35 (the officer).** Blender work on your box. The child is now
  *photographed* as the capsule rig it is, rather than recorded as "never reached". The officer is

### Blocked on a decision only you can make

- **FR-13 — what L4 and L5 should ADD.** The ladder removes aids and multiplies a zero baseline.
  Giving the upper rungs authored complications is a curriculum decision. This is the single highest-
  leverage item on the list: it is 4 of the 5 rungs of all 150 lessons.
- **FR-08 / A5 — how fast Нормален should be.** 30 is gone, 49 is photographed. Your number.
- **FR-15 residual — parking on a slope.** There is no elevation anywhere in the engine. Building it is
  a real project; a flat map titled «наклон» is the lie this register exists to stop.
- **B45 / FR-39 — "1-2 more pedestrians."** Two now cross. If you meant three, say so.
- **B56 — speed grace on speed-adaptation drills.** The L1 ladder widens every cap by +5, which on the
  rain drill pushes the on-screen gate above the envelope the lesson exists to teach.

### The box — no longer the biggest risk, and that is the real change

Last time this section said *56 of 107 rows never rendered, and not one failed for a product reason* —
a 16 GB box, Turbopack PostCSS panics latching `/simulator` at HTTP 500, a 6.8-minute `/login` compile,
PGlite dropping connections, Chromium OOM-killed. **That number is now 10, and 4 of those 10 carry no
complaint at all.** What changed was tooling, not hardware: a login helper that no longer inherits a
30 s default timeout, a pg client with a connect deadline, a login-free cockpit route
(`/dev/ghost-demo?scenario=`) that reaches all 150 scenario templates, a clip rig on its own port, and
probes that measure the LANDING state instead of dismissing it first.

**The one blocker still standing is B5**, and it is honest: Урок 2 runs on the full district-v1 Sofia
map and its complaint sits past a turn a fixed key script cannot follow. It needs a hand-driven pass or
a recorded trace, not a better script.
